# Step 04.7 — Login Screen

Sekarang bagian yang lebih penting.

`lib/features/authentication/presentation/login_screen.dart`

```dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';

import '../../../core/auth/auth_controller.dart';
import '../../../design_system/colors/void_colors.dart';
import '../../../design_system/typography/void_typography.dart';
import '../../../design_system/widgets/branding/void_logo.dart';
import '../../../design_system/widgets/void_button.dart';

class LoginScreen extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const LoginScreen({
    super.key,
    required this.onRegister,
    required this.onForgotPassword,
  });

  final VoidCallback onRegister;
  final VoidCallback onForgotPassword;

  @override
  ConsumerState<LoginScreen> createState() =>
      _LoginScreenState();
}

class _LoginScreenState
    extends ConsumerState<LoginScreen> {
  final _emailController = TextEditingController();
  final _passwordController = TextEditingController();

  bool _rememberMe = true;
  bool _obscurePassword = true;

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _emailController.dispose();
    _passwordController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _login() async {
    await ref
        .read(authControllerProvider.notifier)
        .login(
          email: _emailController.text.trim(),
          password: _passwordController.text,
        );
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final authState = ref.watch(
      authControllerProvider,
    );

    final loading = authState.isLoading;

    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: VoidColors.background,
      body: Center(
        child: SingleChildScrollView(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
          child: ConstrainedBox(
            constraints: const BoxConstraints(
              maxWidth: 420,
            ),
            child: Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(30),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: VoidColors.surface,
                borderRadius:
                    BorderRadius.circular(16),
                border: Border.all(
                  color: VoidColors.borderSoft,
                ),
              ),
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment:
                    CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                children: [
                  const Center(
                    child: VoidLogo(),
                  ),

                  const SizedBox(height: 34),

                  const Text(
                    'Welcome back',
                    textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                    style:
                        VoidTypography.headingLarge,
                  ),

                  const SizedBox(height: 8),

                  const Text(
                    'Sign in to continue to Void Scriptor.',
                    textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                    style: VoidTypography.body,
                  ),

                  const SizedBox(height: 30),

                  const Text(
                    'Email',
                    style: VoidTypography.label,
                  ),

                  const SizedBox(height: 8),

                  TextField(
                    controller: _emailController,
                    keyboardType:
                        TextInputType.emailAddress,
                    autofillHints: const [
                      AutofillHints.email,
                    ],
                    decoration:
                        const InputDecoration(
                      hintText:
                          'creator@example.com',
                    ),
                  ),

                  const SizedBox(height: 18),

                  const Text(
                    'Password',
                    style: VoidTypography.label,
                  ),

                  const SizedBox(height: 8),

                  TextField(
                    controller:
                        _passwordController,
                    obscureText:
                        _obscurePassword,
                    autofillHints: const [
                      AutofillHints.password,
                    ],
                    decoration: InputDecoration(
                      hintText: 'Password',
                      suffixIcon: IconButton(
                        onPressed: () {
                          setState(() {
                            _obscurePassword =
                                !_obscurePassword;
                          });
                        },
                        icon: Icon(
                          _obscurePassword
                              ? Icons
                                  .visibility_outlined
                              : Icons
                                  .visibility_off_outlined,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),

                  const SizedBox(height: 10),

                  Row(
                    children: [
                      Checkbox(
                        value: _rememberMe,
                        onChanged: (value) {
                          setState(() {
                            _rememberMe =
                                value ?? false;
                          });
                        },
                      ),
                      const Text(
                        'Remember me',
                        style:
                            VoidTypography.caption,
                      ),
                      const Spacer(),
                      TextButton(
                        onPressed:
                            widget.onForgotPassword,
                        child: const Text(
                          'Forgot password?',
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),

                  const SizedBox(height: 14),

                  SizedBox(
                    width: double.infinity,
                    child: VoidButton(
                      label: loading
                          ? 'Signing In...'
                          : 'Sign In',
                      icon:
                          Icons.login_rounded,
                      onPressed:
                          loading ? null : _login,
                    ),
                  ),

                  const SizedBox(height: 20),

                  const Row(
                    children: [
                      Expanded(
                        child: Divider(),
                      ),
                      Padding(
                        padding:
                            EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                          horizontal: 12,
                        ),
                        child: Text(
                          'OR',
                          style:
                              VoidTypography.caption,
                        ),
                      ),
                      Expanded(
                        child: Divider(),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),

                  const SizedBox(height: 18),

                  OutlinedButton.icon(
                    onPressed: loading
                        ? null
                        : () {
                            ref
                                .read(
                                  authControllerProvider
                                      .notifier,
                                )
                                .continueAsGuest();
                          },
                    icon: const Icon(
                      Icons.person_outline,
                    ),
                    label: const Text(
                      'Continue as Guest',
                    ),
                  ),

                  const SizedBox(height: 10),

                  OutlinedButton.icon(
                    onPressed: loading
                        ? null
                        : () {
                            ref
                                .read(
                                  authControllerProvider
                                      .notifier,
                                )
                                .continueOffline();
                          },
                    icon: const Icon(
                      Icons.wifi_off_outlined,
                    ),
                    label: const Text(
                      'Work Offline',
                    ),
                  ),

                  const SizedBox(height: 24),

                  Row(
                    mainAxisAlignment:
                        MainAxisAlignment.center,
                    children: [
                      const Text(
                        'No account?',
                        style:
                            VoidTypography.caption,
                      ),
                      TextButton(
                        onPressed:
                            widget.onRegister,
                        child: const Text(
                          'Create account',
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

```dart
class DashboardScreen extends ConsumerWidget {
```

# Step 04.11 — Router

Sekarang kita hubungkan seluruh flow menggunakan `go_router`.

`lib/app/routing/app_router.dart`

```dart
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';

import '../../core/auth/auth_controller.dart';
import '../../core/auth/auth_status.dart';
import '../../features/authentication/presentation/forgot_password_screen.dart';
import '../../features/authentication/presentation/login_screen.dart';
import '../../features/authentication/presentation/register_screen.dart';
import '../../features/dashboard/presentation/dashboard_screen.dart';
import '../../features/splash/presentation/splash_screen.dart';
import '../shell/void_app_shell.dart';
import 'app_routes.dart';

final appRouterProvider = Provider<GoRouter>(
  (ref) {
    final auth = ref.watch(
      authControllerProvider,
    );

    return GoRouter(
      initialLocation: AppRoutes.splash,

      redirect: (context, state) {
        final path = state.uri.path;

        if (auth.isLoading) {
          return path == AppRoutes.splash
              ? null
              : AppRoutes.splash;
        }

        final session = auth.valueOrNull;

        if (session == null) {
          return AppRoutes.login;
        }

        final loggedOut =
            session.status ==
                AuthStatus.unauthenticated;

        final authenticated =
            session.status ==
                    AuthStatus.authenticated ||
                session.status ==
                    AuthStatus.guest ||
                session.status ==
                    AuthStatus.offline;

        final authPage =
            path == AppRoutes.login ||
                path == AppRoutes.register ||
                path ==
                    AppRoutes.forgotPassword;

        if (loggedOut) {
          if (authPage) return null;
          return AppRoutes.login;
        }

        if (authenticated) {
          if (authPage ||
              path == AppRoutes.splash) {
            return AppRoutes.dashboard;
          }
        }

        return null;
      },

      routes: [
        GoRoute(
          path: AppRoutes.splash,
          builder: (_, __) =>
              const SplashScreen(),
        ),

        GoRoute(
          path: AppRoutes.login,
          builder: (context, state) {
            return LoginScreen(
              onRegister: () {
                context.go(
                  AppRoutes.register,
                );
              },
              onForgotPassword: () {
                context.go(
                  AppRoutes.forgotPassword,
                );
              },
            );
          },
        ),

        GoRoute(
          path: AppRoutes.register,
          builder: (context, state) {
            return RegisterScreen(
              onBackToLogin: () {
                context.go(
                  AppRoutes.login,
                );
              },
            );
          },
        ),

        GoRoute(
          path:
              AppRoutes.forgotPassword,
          builder: (context, state) {
            return ForgotPasswordScreen(
              onBack: () {
                context.go(
                  AppRoutes.login,
                );
              },
            );
          },
        ),

        GoRoute(
          path: AppRoutes.dashboard,
          builder: (_, __) =>
              const DashboardScreen(),
        ),

        GoRoute(
          path: AppRoutes.studio,
          builder: (_, __) =>
              const VoidAppShell(),
        ),
      ],
    );
  },
);
```

Flutter menyediakan `Router` untuk navigasi yang lebih kompleks, dan dokumentasinya juga menunjukkan `go_router` sebagai solusi untuk routing/deep links. citeturn960847search1turn960847search4

---

# Step 04.12 — Update Root App

`lib/app/void_app.dart`

```dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';

import '../design_system/theme/void_theme.dart';
import 'routing/app_router.dart';

class VoidScriptorApp
    extends ConsumerWidget {
  const VoidScriptorApp({
    super.key,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(
    BuildContext context,
    WidgetRef ref,
  ) {
    final router = ref.watch(
      appRouterProvider,
    );

    return MaterialApp.router(
      title: 'Void Scriptor',
      debugShowCheckedModeBanner: false,
      themeMode: ThemeMode.dark,
      darkTheme: VoidTheme.dark,
      routerConfig: router,
    );
  }
}
```

---

# Step 04.13 — Satu perbaikan penting

Karena `GoRouter` di atas dibuat ulang saat state authentication berubah, nanti setelah struktur stabil kita sebaiknya memisahkan router lifecycle dari auth refresh supaya navigation stack tidak dibuat ulang tanpa perlu. Tetapi untuk **prototype Sprint 04**, struktur ini sudah cukup untuk memvalidasi flow:

```text
Launch
↓
Splash
↓
Login
↓
Guest / Offline / Login
↓
Dashboard
↓
Studio
```

Setelah flow bekerja, baru kita rapikan router dengan notifier/listenable khusus.

---

# Step 04.14 — Test flow

Jalankan:

```bash
dart format lib
flutter analyze
flutter run -d windows
```

Test:

```text
1. App terbuka
2. Splash muncul
3. Login muncul
4. Tekan Guest
5. Masuk Dashboard
6. Open Studio
7. Explorer + Workspace + Inspector muncul
```

Kemudian:

```text
Logout
→ kembali Login
```

Untuk Android:

```bash
flutter run -d <android-device>
```

Pastikan layar authentication tidak overflow pada portrait kecil. Flutter menyarankan memecah UI menjadi komponen-komponen lebih kecil dan memilih layout/navigation yang berbeda berdasarkan ruang yang tersedia; prinsip itu akan terus kita pakai untuk Dashboard dan Studio mobile. citeturn960847search2turn960847search8turn960847search16

## Status proyek sekarang

Kita sudah punya:

**Sprint 01:** Multiplatform Foundation  
**Sprint 02:** Design System  
**Sprint 03:** Desktop + Mobile Studio Shell  
**Sprint 04:** Splash + Authentication + Session + Routing

Berikutnya adalah **Sprint 05 — Dashboard**. Di sana kita akan membuat panel awal yang benar-benar seperti software profesional: Welcome, Continue Working, Recent Projects, Quick Create, Project Statistics, Recent Assets, AI Activity, Storage, Notifications, Marketplace, dan **Support / Donation (QRIS, DANA, ShopeePay, Bank)**.

lanjutkan

Lanjut ke **Sprint 05 — Dashboard**. Ini akan menjadi panel awal setelah login, sebelum user membuka project atau masuk ke Studio Shell. Fokus sprint ini adalah membuat dashboard yang benar-benar berguna, adaptif, dan siap dipakai di desktop maupun Android.

Kita akan membuat modul Dashboard dengan struktur:

```text
features/
└── dashboard/
    ├── data/
    ├── domain/
    └── presentation/
        ├── dashboard_screen.dart
        └── widgets/
            ├── welcome_card.dart
            ├── quick_actions.dart
            ├── recent_projects.dart
            ├── recent_assets.dart
            ├── statistics_panel.dart
            ├── ai_activity_card.dart
            ├── storage_card.dart
            ├── notifications_card.dart
            ├── marketplace_card.dart
            └── donation_card.dart
```

Untuk Sprint 05, data masih dummy. Database nyata kita sambungkan nanti.

## Step 05.1 — Dashboard Screen

Ganti `dashboard_screen.dart` menjadi:

```dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';

import '../../../app/routing/app_routes.dart';
import '../../../core/auth/auth_controller.dart';
import '../../../design_system/colors/void_colors.dart';
import '../../../design_system/spacing/void_spacing.dart';
import '../../../design_system/typography/void_typography.dart';
import 'widgets/ai_activity_card.dart';
import 'widgets/donation_card.dart';
import 'widgets/marketplace_card.dart';
import 'widgets/notifications_card.dart';
import 'widgets/quick_actions.dart';
import 'widgets/recent_assets.dart';
import 'widgets/recent_projects.dart';
import 'widgets/statistics_panel.dart';
import 'widgets/storage_card.dart';
import 'widgets/welcome_card.dart';

class DashboardScreen extends ConsumerWidget {
  const DashboardScreen({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
    final session = ref.watch(authControllerProvider);

    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: VoidColors.background,
      body: SafeArea(
        child: session.when(
          loading: () => const Center(
            child: CircularProgressIndicator(),
          ),
          error: (error, stack) => Center(
            child: Text('$error'),
          ),
          data: (session) {
            return LayoutBuilder(
              builder: (context, constraints) {
                final desktop = constraints.maxWidth >= 1200;

                return SingleChildScrollView(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(
                    VoidSpacing.xxl,
                  ),
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      const Text(
                        'Dashboard',
                        style: VoidTypography.displayMedium,
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 6),
                      const Text(
                        'Your creative universe at a glance.',
                        style: VoidTypography.body,
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 28),

                      WelcomeCard(
                        displayName:
                            session.displayName ?? 'Creator',
                      ),

                      const SizedBox(height: 20),

                      QuickActions(
                        onOpenStudio: () {
                          context.go(AppRoutes.studio);
                        },
                      ),

                      const SizedBox(height: 20),

                      if (desktop)
                        const Row(
                          crossAxisAlignment:
                              CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                          children: [
                            Expanded(
                              flex: 2,
                              child: RecentProjects(),
                            ),
                            SizedBox(width: 16),
                            Expanded(
                              child: StatisticsPanel(),
                            ),
                          ],
                        )
                      else ...[
                        const RecentProjects(),
                        const SizedBox(height: 16),
                        const StatisticsPanel(),
                      ],

                      const SizedBox(height: 20),

                      if (desktop)
                        const Row(
                          crossAxisAlignment:
                              CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                          children: [
                            Expanded(
                              flex: 2,
                              child: RecentAssets(),
                            ),
                            SizedBox(width: 16),
                            Expanded(
                              child: AiActivityCard(),
                            ),
                          ],
                        )
                      else ...[
                        const RecentAssets(),
                        const SizedBox(height: 16),
                        const AiActivityCard(),
                      ],

                      const SizedBox(height: 20),

                      if (desktop)
                        const Row(
                          crossAxisAlignment:
                              CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                          children: [
                            Expanded(
                              child: StorageCard(),
                            ),
                            SizedBox(width: 16),
                            Expanded(
                              child: NotificationsCard(),
                            ),
                            SizedBox(width: 16),
                            Expanded(
                              child: MarketplaceCard(),
                            ),
                          ],
                        )
                      else ...[
                        const StorageCard(),
                        const SizedBox(height: 16),
                        const NotificationsCard(),
                        const SizedBox(height: 16),
                        const MarketplaceCard(),
                      ],

                      const SizedBox(height: 20),

                      const DonationCard(),
                    ],
                  ),
                );
              },
            );
          },
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

## Step 05.2 — Welcome Card

`welcome_card.dart`

```dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../../../design_system/colors/void_colors.dart';
```

lanjutkan

Lanjut ke **Sprint 06 — Project System + Project Creation Flow**.

Mulai sprint ini, Void Scriptor tidak lagi sekadar shell dan dashboard. Kita mulai membuat **project nyata** yang bisa dibuat, dibuka, ditampilkan di Recent Projects, dan nantinya menjadi wadah untuk Character, Wardrobe, Weapon, Story, dan AI assets.

# Step 06.2 — Project Model

Buat:

`lib/features/projects/domain/project_model.dart`

```dart
class ProjectModel {
  const ProjectModel({
    required this.id,
    required this.name,
    required this.description,
    required this.createdAt,
    required this.updatedAt,
    required this.lastOpenedAt,
    required this.localPath,
    required this.version,
    this.thumbnailPath,
    this.tags = const [],
  });

  final String id;
  final String name;
  final String description;

  final DateTime createdAt;
  final DateTime updatedAt;
  final DateTime lastOpenedAt;

  final String localPath;
  final String version;

  final String? thumbnailPath;
  final List<String> tags;

  ProjectModel copyWith({
    String? id,
    String? name,
    String? description,
    DateTime? createdAt,
    DateTime? updatedAt,
    DateTime? lastOpenedAt,
    String? localPath,
    String? version,
    String? thumbnailPath,
    List<String>? tags,
  }) {
    return ProjectModel(
      id: id ?? this.id,
      name: name ?? this.name,
      description: description ?? this.description,
      createdAt: createdAt ?? this.createdAt,
      updatedAt: updatedAt ?? this.updatedAt,
      lastOpenedAt: lastOpenedAt ?? this.lastOpenedAt,
      localPath: localPath ?? this.localPath,
      version: version ?? this.version,
      thumbnailPath:
          thumbnailPath ?? this.thumbnailPath,
      tags: tags ?? this.tags,
    );
  }
}
```

Untuk saat ini kita sengaja belum memakai `freezed`. Nanti ketika model sudah stabil, baru kita generate immutable model otomatis.

# Step 06.3 — Repository Contract

Buat:

`project_repository.dart`

```dart
import 'project_model.dart';

abstract interface class ProjectRepository {
  Future<List<ProjectModel>> getProjects();

  Future<ProjectModel?> getProject(
    String projectId,
  );

  Future<void> createProject(
    ProjectModel project,
  );

  Future<void> updateProject(
    ProjectModel project,
  );

  Future<void> deleteProject(
    String projectId,
  );
}
```

Dengan pola ini, UI tidak tahu apakah data berasal dari JSON, SQLite, Isar, Supabase, atau cloud.

---

# Step 06.4 — Local Data Source sementara

Untuk Sprint 06 kita gunakan memory storage dulu supaya flow bisa diuji.

`project_local_data_source.dart`

```dart
import '../domain/project_model.dart';

class ProjectLocalDataSource {
  final List<ProjectModel> _projects = [];

  Future<List<ProjectModel>> getProjects() async {
    return List.unmodifiable(_projects);
  }

  Future<ProjectModel?> getProject(
    String projectId,
  ) async {
    for (final project in _projects) {
      if (project.id == projectId) {
        return project;
      }
    }

    return null;
  }

  Future<void> createProject(
    ProjectModel project,
  ) async {
    _projects.add(project);
  }

  Future<void> updateProject(
    ProjectModel project,
  ) async {
    final index = _projects.indexWhere(
      (item) => item.id == project.id,
    );

    if (index == -1) return;

    _projects[index] = project;
  }

  Future<void> deleteProject(
    String projectId,
  ) async {
    _projects.removeWhere(
      (project) => project.id == projectId,
    );
  }
}
```

Database permanen kita sambungkan nanti. Ini lebih aman daripada menambah database kompleks sebelum project flow bekerja.

---

# Step 06.5 — Repository Implementation

`project_repository_impl.dart`

```dart
import '../domain/project_model.dart';
import '../domain/project_repository.dart';
import 'project_local_data_source.dart';

class ProjectRepositoryImpl
    implements ProjectRepository {
  ProjectRepositoryImpl(
    this.dataSource,
  );

  final ProjectLocalDataSource dataSource;

  @override
  Future<List<ProjectModel>> getProjects() {
    return dataSource.getProjects();
  }

  @override
  Future<ProjectModel?> getProject(
    String projectId,
  ) {
    return dataSource.getProject(projectId);
  }

  @override
  Future<void> createProject(
    ProjectModel project,
  ) {
    return dataSource.createProject(project);
  }

  @override
  Future<void> updateProject(
    ProjectModel project,
  ) {
    return dataSource.updateProject(project);
  }

  @override
  Future<void> deleteProject(
    String projectId,
  ) {
    return dataSource.deleteProject(projectId);
  }
}
```

---

# Step 06.6 — Riverpod Providers

Buat:

`project_controller.dart`

```dart
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:uuid/uuid.dart';

import '../data/project_local_data_source.dart';
import '../data/project_repository_impl.dart';
import '../domain/project_model.dart';
import '../domain/project_repository.dart';

final projectLocalDataSourceProvider =
    Provider<ProjectLocalDataSource>(
  (ref) {
    return ProjectLocalDataSource();
  },
);

final projectRepositoryProvider =
    Provider<ProjectRepository>(
  (ref) {
    return ProjectRepositoryImpl(
      ref.watch(
        projectLocalDataSourceProvider,
      ),
    );
  },
);

class ProjectController
    extends AsyncNotifier<List<ProjectModel>> {
  @override
  Future<List<ProjectModel>> build() async {
    return ref
        .watch(projectRepositoryProvider)
        .getProjects();
  }

  Future<ProjectModel> createProject({
    required String name,
    required String description,
    required String localPath,
  }) async {
    final repository = ref.read(
      projectRepositoryProvider,
    );

    final now = DateTime.now();

    final project = ProjectModel(
      id: const Uuid().v4(),
      name: name,
      description: description,
      createdAt: now,
      updatedAt: now,
      lastOpenedAt: now,
      localPath: localPath,
      version: '0.1.0',
    );

    await repository.createProject(project);

    state = AsyncData(
      await repository.getProjects(),
    );

    return project;
  }

  Future<void> deleteProject(
    String projectId,
  ) async {
    final repository = ref.read(
      projectRepositoryProvider,
    );

    await repository.deleteProject(
      projectId,
    );

    state = AsyncData(
      await repository.getProjects(),
    );
  }
}

final projectControllerProvider =
    AsyncNotifierProvider<
        ProjectController,
        List<ProjectModel>>(
  ProjectController.new,
);
```

Kita sudah memasang `uuid` pada Sprint 01, jadi project sekarang mendapatkan ID yang tidak bergantung pada nama.

---

# Step 06.7 — Current Project State

Kita perlu tahu project mana yang sedang dibuka.

Tambahkan:

```dart
final currentProjectProvider =
    StateProvider<ProjectModel?>(
  (ref) => null,
);
```

Nanti hampir semua fitur Studio akan membaca provider ini.

Contoh:

```text
Current Project
    │
    ├── Character Database
    ├── Wardrobe Database
    ├── Weapon Database
    ├── Story
    ├── Timeline
    └── AI Context
```

Ini salah satu bagian terpenting dari arsitektur kita.

# Step 06.8 — Route Project

Tambahkan pada `app_routes.dart`:

```dart
static const projects = '/projects';
static const createProject = '/projects/create';
static const studioProject = '/studio/:projectId';
```

Studio sekarang nantinya tidak cukup hanya:

```text
/studio
```

tetapi menjadi:

```text
/studio/UUID
```

Contoh:

```text
/studio/75be9b78-...
```

Ini memungkinkan deep-link dan multiple project support di kemudian hari.

```dart
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';

import '../../../app/routing/app_routes.dart';
import '../../../design_system/colors/void_colors.dart';
import '../../../design_system/spacing/void_spacing.dart';
import '../../../design_system/typography/void_typography.dart';
import 'project_controller.dart';
import 'widgets/project_card.dart';
import 'widgets/project_empty_state.dart';

class ProjectListScreen
    extends ConsumerWidget {
  const ProjectListScreen({
    super.key,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(
    BuildContext context,
    WidgetRef ref,
  ) {
    final projects = ref.watch(
      projectControllerProvider,
    );

    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: VoidColors.background,
      body: SafeArea(
        child: Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(
            VoidSpacing.xxl,
          ),
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment:
                CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              Row(
                children: [
                  const Expanded(
                    child: Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment:
                          CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        Text(
                          'Projects',
                          style:
                              VoidTypography.displayMedium,
                        ),
                        SizedBox(height: 6),
                        Text(
                          'Create and manage your universes.',
                          style:
                              VoidTypography.body,
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                  FilledButton.icon(
                    onPressed: () {
                      context.go(
                        AppRoutes.createProject,
                      );
                    },
                    icon: const Icon(
                      Icons.add,
                    ),
                    label:
                        const Text('New Project'),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 28),
              Expanded(
                child: projects.when(
                  loading: () =>
                      const Center(
                    child:
                        CircularProgressIndicator(),
                  ),
                  error: (error, stack) =>
                      Center(
                    child: Text('$error'),
                  ),
                  data: (items) {
                    if (items.isEmpty) {
                      return const ProjectEmptyState();
                    }

                    return LayoutBuilder(
                      builder: (
                        context,
                        constraints,
                      ) {
                        final width =
                            constraints.maxWidth;

                        var columns = 1;

                        if (width >= 1600) {
                          columns = 4;
                        } else if (width >= 1100) {
                          columns = 3;
                        } else if (width >= 700) {
                          columns = 2;
                        }

                        return GridView.builder(
                          gridDelegate:
                              SliverGridDelegateWithFixedCrossAxisCount(
                            crossAxisCount:
                                columns,
                            crossAxisSpacing: 16,
                            mainAxisSpacing: 16,
                            childAspectRatio:
                                1.55,
                          ),
                          itemCount:
                              items.length,
                          itemBuilder: (
                            context,
                            index,
                          ) {
                            return ProjectCard(
                              project:
                                  items[index],
                            );
                          },
                        );
                      },
                    );
                  },
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

# Step 06.11 — Project Card

`project_card.dart`

```dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';

import '../../../../design_system/colors/void_colors.dart';
import '../../../../design_system/typography/void_typography.dart';
import '../../../../design_system/widgets/void_card.dart';
import '../../domain/project_model.dart';
import '../project_controller.dart';

class ProjectCard
    extends ConsumerWidget {
  const ProjectCard({
    super.key,
    required this.project,
  });

  final ProjectModel project;

  @override
  Widget build(
    BuildContext context,
    WidgetRef ref,
  ) {
    return VoidCard(
      onTap: () {
        ref
            .read(
              currentProjectProvider
                  .notifier,
            )
            .state = project;

        context.go(
          '/studio/${project.id}',
        );
      },
      padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment:
            CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Expanded(
            child: Container(
              decoration:
                  const BoxDecoration(
                color: VoidColors
                    .surfaceElevated,
                borderRadius:
                    BorderRadius.vertical(
                  top: Radius.circular(12),
                ),
              ),
              child: const Center(
                child: Icon(
                  Icons.auto_awesome_mosaic_outlined,
                  size: 38,
                  color:
                      VoidColors.textMuted,
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
          Padding(
            padding:
                const EdgeInsets.all(14),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment:
                  CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text(
                  project.name,
                  maxLines: 1,
                  overflow:
                      TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                  style:
                      VoidTypography.title,
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 4),
                Text(
                  project.description
                          .isEmpty
                      ? 'No description'
                      : project.description,
                  maxLines: 1,
                  overflow:
                      TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                  style:
                      VoidTypography.caption,
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 10),
                Text(
                  'Version ${project.version}',
                  style:
                      VoidTypography.caption,
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

# Step 06.12 — Create Project Screen

Sekarang kita buat Project Wizard pertama.

`create_project_screen.dart`

```dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';
import 'package:path_provider/path_provider.dart';

import '../../../design_system/colors/void_colors.dart';
import '../../../design_system/typography/void_typography.dart';
import '../../../design_system/widgets/void_button.dart';
import 'project_controller.dart';

class CreateProjectScreen
    extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const CreateProjectScreen({
    super.key,
  });

  @override
  ConsumerState<CreateProjectScreen>
      createState() =>
          _CreateProjectScreenState();
}

class _CreateProjectScreenState
    extends ConsumerState<
        CreateProjectScreen> {
  final _nameController =
      TextEditingController();

  final _descriptionController =
      TextEditingController();

  bool _creating = false;

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _nameController.dispose();
    _descriptionController.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  Future<void> _create() async {
    final name =
        _nameController.text.trim();

    if (name.isEmpty) {
      ScaffoldMessenger.of(context)
          .showSnackBar(
        const SnackBar(
          content:
              Text('Project name is required.'),
        ),
      );
      return;
    }

    setState(() {
      _creating = true;
    });

    try {
      final directory =
          await getApplicationDocumentsDirectory();

      final project =
          await ref
              .read(
                projectControllerProvider
                    .notifier,
              )
              .createProject(
                name: name,
                description:
                    _descriptionController
                        .text
                        .trim(),
                localPath:
                    '${directory.path}/VoidScriptor/$name',
              );

      ref
          .read(
            currentProjectProvider
                .notifier,
          )
          .state = project;

      if (mounted) {
        context.go(
          '/studio/${project.id}',
        );
      }
    } finally {
      if (mounted) {
        setState(() {
          _creating = false;
        });
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor:
          VoidColors.background,
      body: Center(
        child: SingleChildScrollView(
          padding:
              const EdgeInsets.all(24),
          child: ConstrainedBox(
            constraints:
                const BoxConstraints(
              maxWidth: 560,
            ),
            child: Container(
              padding:
                  const EdgeInsets.all(30),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color:
                    VoidColors.surface,
                borderRadius:
                    BorderRadius.circular(
                  16,
                ),
                border: Border.all(
                  color:
                      VoidColors.borderSoft,
                ),
              ),
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment:
                    CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                children: [
                  const Text(
                    'Create Project',
                    style:
                        VoidTypography.headingLarge,
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 8),
                  const Text(
                    'Create a new universe workspace.',
                    style:
                        VoidTypography.body,
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 26),
                  TextField(
                    controller:
                        _nameController,
                    autofocus: true,
                    decoration:
                        const InputDecoration(
                      labelText:
                          'Project name',
                      hintText:
                          'Void Universe',
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),
                  TextField(
                    controller:
                        _descriptionController,
                    maxLines: 4,
                    decoration:
                        const InputDecoration(
                      labelText:
                          'Description',
                      hintText:
                          'Describe this universe...',
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 22),
                  Row(
                    children: [
                      TextButton(
                        onPressed:
                            _creating
                                ? null
                                : () {
                                    context
                                        .pop();
                                  },
                        child:
                            const Text(
                          'Cancel',
                        ),
                      ),
                      const Spacer(),
                      VoidButton(
                        label: _creating
                            ? 'Creating...'
                            : 'Create Project',
                        icon:
                            Icons.add,
                        onPressed:
                            _creating
                                ? null
                                : _create,
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

Ini menggunakan `path_provider` yang sudah kita tambahkan pada fondasi.

# Step 06.13 — Router

Tambahkan import Project screens ke `app_router.dart`, lalu route:

```dart
GoRoute(
  path: AppRoutes.projects,
  builder: (_, __) =>
      const ProjectListScreen(),
),

GoRoute(
  path: AppRoutes.createProject,
  builder: (_, __) =>
      const CreateProjectScreen(),
),

GoRoute(
  path: '/studio/:projectId',
  builder: (context, state) {
    final projectId =
        state.pathParameters[
            'projectId'
        ]!;

    return VoidAppShell(
      projectId: projectId,
    );
  },
),
```

Sekarang ubah `VoidAppShell` agar menerima:

```dart
final String projectId;
```

Constructor:

```dart
const VoidAppShell({
  super.key,
  required this.projectId,
});
```

Desktop dan mobile shell nantinya juga bisa menerima project ID tersebut.

# Step 06.16 — Project Folder yang sebenarnya

Kita belum membuat directory fisiknya.

Nanti saat project dibuat, tambahkan:

```dart
final projectDirectory =
    Directory(
  '${directory.path}/VoidScriptor/${project.id}',
);

await projectDirectory.create(
  recursive: true,
);
```

Saya lebih menyarankan folder berdasarkan UUID daripada nama:

```text
Documents/
└── VoidScriptor/
    └── Projects/
        └── 5bcd6312-.../
            ├── project.json
            ├── assets/
            ├── thumbnails/
            ├── characters/
            ├── wardrobe/
            ├── weapons/
            ├── story/
            ├── ai/
            ├── cache/
            └── backup/
```

**Jangan menggunakan nama project sebagai folder utama.**

Karena:

```text
Void Universe
```

bisa diubah menjadi:

```text
Void Universe Remastered
```

tetapi UUID tetap sama.

Ini sama dengan filosofi Asset Engine yang sudah kita tetapkan sebelumnya.

# Step 06.17 — Project Manifest

Nanti setiap project memiliki:

```text
project.json
```

Format awal:

```json
{
  "schemaVersion": 1,
  "id": "UUID",
  "name": "Void Universe",
  "description": "",
  "version": "0.1.0",
  "createdAt": "",
  "updatedAt": "",
  "application": {
    "name": "Void Scriptor",
    "version": "0.1.0"
  }
}
```

`schemaVersion` sangat penting karena nanti kita bisa melakukan migration ketika struktur project berubah.

# Setelah Sprint 06

Sekarang proyek kita sudah mempunyai struktur yang jauh lebih serius:

```text
VOID SCRIPTOR

Authentication
      ↓
Dashboard
      ↓
Project Manager
      ↓
Current Project
      ↓
Studio Shell
      ↓
Creative Modules
```

Status:

**Sprint 01** — Multiplatform Foundation ✅  
**Sprint 02** — Design System ✅  
**Sprint 03** — Adaptive Studio Shell ✅  
**Sprint 04** — Authentication ✅  
**Sprint 05** — Dashboard ✅  
**Sprint 06** — Project System ✅

Sprint berikutnya adalah **Sprint 07 — Local Database + Project Persistence**.

Di sana kita mengganti memory storage tadi dengan penyimpanan permanen sehingga setelah aplikasi ditutup dan komputer/Android direstart, `Void Universe` tetap ada. Kita juga mulai membuat struktur **Project Manifest, migrations, autosave, backup, dan recovery**, sebelum masuk ke Character Database.

lanjutkan









Kita lanjut ke **Sprint 07 — Local Database + Project Persistence**.

Ini sprint penting karena kita akan mengubah project dari data sementara di RAM menjadi data yang **tetap ada setelah aplikasi ditutup/restart**.

Satu koreksi arsitektur dari rancangan sebelumnya: untuk tahap ini saya menyarankan kita **tidak menjadikan database global sebagai sumber utama isi project**. Setiap project Void Scriptor akan menjadi paket mandiri dengan `project.json` + database project sendiri. Ini jauh lebih baik untuk backup, pindah komputer, ekspor, dan recovery.

## Step 07.1 — Tambahkan SQLite

Untuk project database kita gunakan SQLite.

Jalankan:

```bash
flutter pub add drift
flutter pub add sqlite3_flutter_libs
flutter pub add path
flutter pub add path_provider

flutter pub add --dev drift_dev
flutter pub add --dev build_runner
```

Kita memakai Drift sebagai layer Dart di atas SQLite.

Alasannya bukan sekadar penyimpanan project. Nanti kita membutuhkan query seperti:

```text
Character
    ↓
Wardrobe
    ↓
Weapon
    ↓
Environment
    ↓
Story Event
    ↓
Timeline
```

Relasi seperti ini lebih cocok menggunakan database terstruktur.

# Step 07.3 — Project Directory

Buat:

`core/persistence/project_paths.dart`

```dart
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:path/path.dart' as p;
import 'package:path_provider/path_provider.dart';

abstract final class ProjectPaths {
  static Future<Directory> root() async {
    final documents =
        await getApplicationDocumentsDirectory();

    final directory = Directory(
      p.join(
        documents.path,
        'VoidScriptor',
        'Projects',
      ),
    );

    if (!await directory.exists()) {
      await directory.create(
        recursive: true,
      );
    }

    return directory;
  }

  static Future<Directory> project(
    String projectId,
  ) async {
    final rootDirectory = await root();

    return Directory(
      p.join(
        rootDirectory.path,
        projectId,
      ),
    );
  }

  static Future<File> manifest(
    String projectId,
  ) async {
    final directory =
        await project(projectId);

    return File(
      p.join(
        directory.path,
        'project.json',
      ),
    );
  }

  static Future<File> database(
    String projectId,
  ) async {
    final directory =
        await project(projectId);

    return File(
      p.join(
        directory.path,
        'project.db',
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

Sekarang lokasi file tidak lagi dibuat sembarangan di berbagai fitur.

Semua melalui `ProjectPaths`.

---

# Step 07.4 — Struktur Project

Ketika project dibuat, hasil fisiknya harus:

```text
Projects/
└── e4b9.../
    ├── project.json
    ├── project.db
    │
    ├── assets/
    │   ├── characters/
    │   ├── wardrobe/
    │   ├── weapons/
    │   ├── creatures/
    │   ├── vehicles/
    │   ├── environments/
    │   ├── audio/
    │   └── references/
    │
    ├── thumbnails/
    ├── exports/
    ├── cache/
    ├── backups/
    └── recovery/
```

Perhatikan bahwa kita memisahkan:

**database metadata**

dengan:

**binary assets**

Jangan masukkan gambar besar, video, PSD, audio, dan sejenisnya langsung ke database.

Database menyimpan referensi/path.

# Step 07.5 — Project Manifest

Buat:

`project_manifest.dart`

```dart
class ProjectManifest {
  const ProjectManifest({
    required this.schemaVersion,
    required this.id,
    required this.name,
    required this.description,
    required this.projectVersion,
    required this.createdAt,
    required this.updatedAt,
    required this.appVersion,
  });

  final int schemaVersion;

  final String id;
  final String name;
  final String description;

  final String projectVersion;

  final DateTime createdAt;
  final DateTime updatedAt;

  final String appVersion;

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() {
    return {
      'schemaVersion': schemaVersion,
      'id': id,
      'name': name,
      'description': description,
      'projectVersion': projectVersion,
      'createdAt':
          createdAt.toIso8601String(),
      'updatedAt':
          updatedAt.toIso8601String(),
      'application': {
        'name': 'Void Scriptor',
        'version': appVersion,
      },
    };
  }

  factory ProjectManifest.fromJson(
    Map<String, dynamic> json,
  ) {
    final application =
        json['application']
            as Map<String, dynamic>;

    return ProjectManifest(
      schemaVersion:
          json['schemaVersion'] as int,
      id: json['id'] as String,
      name: json['name'] as String,
      description:
          json['description'] as String? ?? '',
      projectVersion:
          json['projectVersion']
                  as String? ??
              '0.1.0',
      createdAt: DateTime.parse(
        json['createdAt'] as String,
      ),
      updatedAt: DateTime.parse(
        json['updatedAt'] as String,
      ),
      appVersion:
          application['version']
                  as String? ??
              'unknown',
    );
  }
}
```

`schemaVersion` jangan dihapus.

Ini akan menjadi dasar migration.

---

# Step 07.6 — Manifest Service

`project_manifest_service.dart`

```dart
import 'dart:convert';

import 'project_manifest.dart';
import 'project_paths.dart';

class ProjectManifestService {
  Future<void> write(
    ProjectManifest manifest,
  ) async {
    final file =
        await ProjectPaths.manifest(
      manifest.id,
    );

    await file.parent.create(
      recursive: true,
    );

    const encoder =
        JsonEncoder.withIndent('  ');

    await file.writeAsString(
      encoder.convert(
        manifest.toJson(),
      ),
      flush: true,
    );
  }

  Future<ProjectManifest> read(
    String projectId,
  ) async {
    final file =
        await ProjectPaths.manifest(
      projectId,
    );

    final json = jsonDecode(
      await file.readAsString(),
    ) as Map<String, dynamic>;

    return ProjectManifest.fromJson(
      json,
    );
  }
}
```

Sekarang project mempunyai metadata independen dari database.

---

# Step 07.7 — Project File Service

Buat:

`project_file_service.dart`

```dart
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:path/path.dart' as p;

import 'project_paths.dart';

class ProjectFileService {
  Future<Directory> createStructure(
    String projectId,
  ) async {
    final projectDirectory =
        await ProjectPaths.project(
      projectId,
    );

    final folders = [
      'assets',
      'assets/characters',
      'assets/wardrobe',
      'assets/weapons',
      'assets/creatures',
      'assets/vehicles',
      'assets/environments',
      'assets/audio',
      'assets/references',
      'thumbnails',
      'exports',
      'cache',
      'backups',
      'recovery',
    ];

    await projectDirectory.create(
      recursive: true,
    );

    for (final folder in folders) {
      await Directory(
        p.join(
          projectDirectory.path,
          folder,
        ),
      ).create(
        recursive: true,
      );
    }

    return projectDirectory;
  }
}
```

```text
      ↓

      ↓

      ↓

      ↓
```

# Step 07.10 — Project Creation Service

Kita buat service khusus.

```text
features/projects/
└── application/
    └── create_project_service.dart
```

Isi dasar:

```dart
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:uuid/uuid.dart';

import '../../../core/persistence/project_file_service.dart';
import '../../../core/persistence/project_manifest.dart';
import '../../../core/persistence/project_manifest_service.dart';
import '../../../core/persistence/project_paths.dart';
import '../domain/project_model.dart';

class CreateProjectService {
  CreateProjectService({
    required this.fileService,
    required this.manifestService,
  });

  final ProjectFileService fileService;
  final ProjectManifestService
      manifestService;

  Future<ProjectModel> create({
    required String name,
    required String description,
  }) async {
    final id = const Uuid().v4();

    final now = DateTime.now();

    try {
      final directory =
          await fileService.createStructure(
        id,
      );

      final manifest =
          ProjectManifest(
        schemaVersion: 1,
        id: id,
        name: name,
        description: description,
        projectVersion: '0.1.0',
        createdAt: now,
        updatedAt: now,
        appVersion: '0.1.0',
      );

      await manifestService.write(
        manifest,
      );

      return ProjectModel(
        id: id,
        name: name,
        description: description,
        createdAt: now,
        updatedAt: now,
        lastOpenedAt: now,
        localPath: directory.path,
        version: '0.1.0',
      );
    } catch (_) {
      final directory =
          await ProjectPaths.project(id);

      if (await directory.exists()) {
        await directory.delete(
          recursive: true,
        );
      }

      rethrow;
    }
  }
}
```

Ini jauh lebih aman daripada menaruh seluruh proses di UI.

# Step 07.11 — Project Registry

Sekarang muncul masalah penting.

Bagaimana Dashboard tahu project apa saja yang pernah dibuat?

Kita membutuhkan **Project Registry**.

Contohnya:

```text
VoidScriptor/
├── app/
│   └── projects.json
│
└── Projects/
```

`projects.json` hanya menyimpan registry:

```json
{
  "schemaVersion": 1,
  "projects": [
    {
      "id": "UUID",
      "path": "...",
      "lastOpenedAt": "..."
    }
  ]
}
```

Bukan seluruh isi project.

Dengan begitu Dashboard bisa menemukan project tanpa membuka semua database.

---

# Step 07.12 — Recovery tambahan

Kita jangan menunggu file corrupt baru memikirkan recovery.

Arsitektur save:

```text
CURRENT DATA
     │
     ▼
temporary write
     │
     ▼
validation
     │
     ▼
atomic replace
```

Untuk manifest, jangan langsung menimpa `project.json`.

Idealnya:

```text
project.json
project.json.tmp
project.json.bak
```

Flow:

```text
write tmp
↓
validate tmp
↓
old → bak
↓
tmp → project.json
```

Nanti kita implementasikan penuh di Persistence Hardening.

---

# Step 07.13 — Autosave

Kita siapkan tiga jenis save.

```text
MANUAL SAVE
Ctrl + S

AUTOSAVE
after meaningful change

RECOVERY SNAPSHOT
periodic
```

Tetapi jangan autosave setiap karakter keyboard langsung ke disk.

Gunakan debounce:

```text
User editing
   ↓
change
   ↓
change
   ↓
change
   ↓
idle ~1–2 sec
   ↓
AUTOSAVE
```

Ini penting ketika Story Editor nanti menangani teks panjang.

---

# Step 07.14 — Dirty State

Tambahkan state:

```dart
final projectDirtyProvider =
    StateProvider<bool>(
  (ref) => false,
);
```

Ketika ada perubahan:

```text
Void Universe
```

menjadi:

```text
Void Universe •
```

Tanda `•` berarti belum tersimpan.

Setelah save:

```text
Void Universe
```

Ini pola UX kecil tetapi penting untuk aplikasi desktop.

# Step 07.18 — Backup Structure

Setiap project:

```text
backups/
├── 2026-08-12_130000/
│   ├── project.json
│   └── project.db
│
├── 2026-08-12_140000/
│   ├── project.json
│   └── project.db
│
└── ...
```

Asset besar tidak perlu disalin setiap autosave.

Nanti backup engine dapat menggunakan:

```text
metadata snapshot
database snapshot
changed assets only
```

supaya tidak menghabiskan storage.

# Step 07.19 — Project Health Check

Ketika project dibuka:

```text
OPEN PROJECT
     ↓
manifest exists?
     ↓
schema supported?
     ↓
database exists?
     ↓
database readable?
     ↓
assets directory valid?
     ↓
```

Jika gagal:


# Step 07.20 — Migration Engine

Kita sudah punya:

```json
"schemaVersion": 1
```

Misalnya di masa depan:

```text
Void Scriptor 0.1

schemaVersion 1
```

kemudian:

```text
Void Scriptor 2.0

schemaVersion 7
```

Saat project lama dibuka:

```text
1
↓
2
↓
3
↓
4
↓
5
↓
6
↓
7
```

Bukan:

```text
1 → langsung menebak struktur 7
```

Setiap migration harus eksplisit.

---

# Step 07.21 — Project Lock

Kita juga perlu menyiapkan sistem:

```text
.project.lock
```

Ketika project sedang dibuka:

```text
Void Scriptor
    ↓
creates lock
```

Jika instance lain mencoba membuka project sama:

```text
This project is already open.

[Open Read Only]
[Cancel]
```

Ini mencegah dua instance menulis database yang sama secara bersamaan.

Implementasinya kita lakukan ketika multi-window dimulai, tetapi struktur ini perlu dipikirkan sekarang.

# Step 07.22 — Android

Android menggunakan engine project yang sama:

```text
project.json
project.db
assets/
backups/
```

Perbedaannya hanya lokasi storage.

Kita tidak boleh hard-code:

```text
C:\Users\...
```

atau:

```text
/home/user/...
```

Karena `path_provider` yang menentukan storage root berdasarkan platform.

Dengan begitu:

```text
Windows ─┐
macOS ───┤
Linux ───┼── ProjectPaths
Android ─┤
iOS ─────┘
```

Core persistence tetap sama.

lanjutkan





Lanjut ke **Sprint 08 — Asset Core + Character Database**.

Mulai sprint ini, Void Scriptor berubah dari project manager menjadi **creative universe database**. Kita akan membangun fondasi yang nanti dipakai bersama oleh Character, Wardrobe, Weapon, Environment, Story, AI Prompt, dan Relation Graph.

# Step 08.1 — Tambahkan Asset Type

Buat:

`lib/features/assets/domain/asset_type.dart`

```dart
enum AssetType {
  character,
  wardrobe,
  weapon,
  creature,
  vehicle,
  environment,
  story,
  timeline,
  prompt,
  reference,
}
```

# Step 08.2 — Base Asset Model

Buat:

`lib/features/assets/domain/asset_model.dart`

```dart
import 'asset_type.dart';

class AssetModel {
  const AssetModel({
    required this.id,
    required this.projectId,
    required this.name,
    required this.type,
    required this.version,
    required this.createdAt,
    required this.updatedAt,
    this.description = '',
    this.thumbnailPath,
    this.tags = const [],
  });

  final String id;
  final String projectId;

  final String name;
  final String description;

  final AssetType type;

  final String version;

  final String? thumbnailPath;

  final List<String> tags;

  final DateTime createdAt;
  final DateTime updatedAt;

  AssetModel copyWith({
    String? name,
    String? description,
    String? version,
    String? thumbnailPath,
    List<String>? tags,
    DateTime? updatedAt,
  }) {
    return AssetModel(
      id: id,
      projectId: projectId,
      name: name ?? this.name,
      description:
          description ?? this.description,
      type: type,
      version: version ?? this.version,
      thumbnailPath:
          thumbnailPath ?? this.thumbnailPath,
      tags: tags ?? this.tags,
      createdAt: createdAt,
      updatedAt:
          updatedAt ?? this.updatedAt,
    );
  }
}
```

# Step 08.3 — Database Asset Table

Di Drift project database, buat:

`tables/assets.dart`

```dart
import 'package:drift/drift.dart';

class Assets extends Table {
  TextColumn get id => text()();

  TextColumn get projectId => text()();

  TextColumn get name => text()();

  TextColumn get description =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant(''),
      )();

  TextColumn get type => text()();

  TextColumn get version =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant('1.0.0'),
      )();

  TextColumn get thumbnailPath =>
      text().nullable()();

  DateTimeColumn get createdAt =>
      dateTime()();

  DateTimeColumn get updatedAt =>
      dateTime()();

  @override
  Set<Column<Object>> get primaryKey => {
        id,
      };
}
```

# Step 08.4 — Tags

Jangan menyimpan tag sebagai satu string seperti:

```text
male,purple,hair,long
```

Kita buat tabel relasional.

`asset_tags.dart`

```dart
import 'package:drift/drift.dart';

class AssetTags extends Table {
  IntColumn get id =>
      integer().autoIncrement()();

  TextColumn get assetId => text()();

  TextColumn get tag => text()();
}
```

Ini memungkinkan nanti query:

```text
type:character
tag:void
tag:white-hair
```

# Step 08.5 — Character Model

Sekarang buat domain Character.

```dart
class CharacterModel {
  const CharacterModel({
    required this.assetId,
    required this.projectId,
    required this.displayName,
    this.alias = '',
    this.biography = '',
    this.personality = '',
    this.gender = '',
    this.age,
    this.heightCm,
    this.hair = '',
    this.eyes = '',
    this.skin = '',
  });

  final String assetId;
  final String projectId;

  final String displayName;
  final String alias;

  final String biography;
  final String personality;

  final String gender;

  final int? age;
  final double? heightCm;

  final String hair;
  final String eyes;
  final String skin;
}
```

Jangan masukkan semua field karakter ke `Assets`. `Assets` hanya menyimpan data universal, sementara Character menyimpan data spesifik.

# Step 08.6 — Character Table

Buat:

```dart
import 'package:drift/drift.dart';

class Characters extends Table {
  TextColumn get assetId => text()();

  TextColumn get projectId => text()();

  TextColumn get displayName => text()();

  TextColumn get alias =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant(''),
      )();

  TextColumn get biography =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant(''),
      )();

  TextColumn get personality =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant(''),
      )();

  TextColumn get gender =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant(''),
      )();

  IntColumn get age => integer().nullable()();

  RealColumn get heightCm =>
      real().nullable()();

  TextColumn get hair =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant(''),
      )();

  TextColumn get eyes =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant(''),
      )();

  TextColumn get skin =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant(''),
      )();

  @override
  Set<Column<Object>> get primaryKey => {
        assetId,
      };
}
```

`assetId` juga merupakan UUID dari Asset utama.

Jadi:

```text
ASSETS
id = CHAR-UUID

        │
        ▼

CHARACTERS
assetId = CHAR-UUID
```

# Step 08.7 — Character Identity Preservation

```dart
class CharacterIdentityProfile {
  const CharacterIdentityProfile({
    required this.characterId,

    this.hairLock = true,
    this.bodyLock = true,
    this.colorLock = true,
    this.outfitLock = false,
  });

  final bool faceLock;
  final bool hairLock;
  final bool bodyLock;
  final bool colorLock;
  final bool outfitLock;
```

```dart
class CharacterReferences
```

# Step 08.11 — Asset Repository

Karena Character adalah Asset, create flow tidak boleh:

```text
Create Character Row
```

saja.

Harus:

```text
CREATE CHARACTER
      │
      ├── create Asset
      │
      ├── create Character
      │
      ├── create Identity Profile
      │
      └── commit transaction
```

Kalau satu gagal:

```text
ROLLBACK
```

Ini sangat penting agar database tidak memiliki Asset tanpa Character atau sebaliknya.

```dart
class CreateCharacterService {

        const Uuid().v4();
```

Implementasi query Drift-nya kita sambungkan setelah DAO dibuat.

# Step 08.13 — DAO

Kita jangan menyebarkan query SQL/Drift ke UI.

Struktur:

```text
database/project/
├── daos/
│   ├── asset_dao.dart
│   └── character_dao.dart
└── tables/
```

Contoh:

```dart
@DriftAccessor(
  tables: [
    Assets,
    AssetTags,
  ],
)
class AssetDao
    extends DatabaseAccessor<ProjectDatabase>
    with _$AssetDaoMixin {
  AssetDao(super.db);

  Future<List<Asset>> getAllAssets() {
    return select(assets).get();
  }
}
```

Character DAO mengikuti pola yang sama.

# Step 08.14 — Database Update

`ProjectDatabase` sekarang memiliki:

```dart
@DriftDatabase(
  tables: [
    ProjectMetadata,
    Assets,
    AssetTags,
    Characters,
    CharacterIdentityProfiles,
    CharacterReferences,
  ],
  daos: [
    AssetDao,
    CharacterDao,
  ],
)
class ProjectDatabase
    extends _$ProjectDatabase {
  ...
}
```

Schema:

```dart
@override
int get schemaVersion => 2;
```

Karena Sprint 07 menggunakan schema 1.

# Step 08.15 — Migration

Jangan reset database saat schema berubah.

Gunakan migration:

```dart
@override
MigrationStrategy get migration {
  return MigrationStrategy(
    onCreate: (m) async {
      await m.createAll();
    },
    onUpgrade: (
      m,
      from,
      to,
    ) async {
      if (from < 2) {
        await m.createTable(assets);
        await m.createTable(assetTags);
        await m.createTable(characters);
        await m.createTable(
          characterIdentityProfiles,
        );
        await m.createTable(
          characterReferences,
        );
      }
    },
  );
}
```

Sekarang filosofi migration yang kita buat di Sprint 07 benar-benar mulai dipakai.

```dart
final characterControllerProvider =
```

# Step 08.20 — Relation Core

Walaupun Relation Graph penuh baru nanti, tabel dasarnya sebaiknya dibuat sekarang.

```dart
class AssetRelations extends Table {
  TextColumn get id => text()();

  TextColumn get sourceAssetId => text()();

  TextColumn get targetAssetId => text()();

  TextColumn get relationType => text()();

  @override
  Set<Column<Object>> get primaryKey => {
        id,
      };
}
```

Contoh data:

```text
Qowner
    uses
Dual Dagger
```

Database:

```text
sourceAssetId = Qowner UUID
targetAssetId = Dual Dagger UUID
relationType = "uses"
```

Atau:

```text
Qowner
    wears
Void Coat
```

# Step 08.21 — Relation Types

Jangan menggunakan string acak di seluruh aplikasi.

Buat enum/domain:

```dart
enum AssetRelationType {
  uses,
  wears,
  owns,
  appearsIn,
  locatedIn,
  createdBy,
  memberOf,
  enemyOf,
  allyOf,
  relatedTo,
}
```

Nanti bisa diperluas.

# Step 08.22 — Asset Version

Asset sudah punya:

```text
version
```

Tetapi nantinya versi tidak hanya string.

Kita akan membuat:

```text
AssetVersionHistory
```

Contohnya:

```text
Qowner
├── v1 Original
├── v2 Hair Update
├── v3 Outfit Update
└── v4 Current
```

Belum kita implementasikan di Sprint 08, tetapi UUID asset sudah memastikan version history bisa ditambahkan tanpa mengubah identitas utama.

# Step 08.23 — File Layout Character

Folder project sekarang:

```text
assets/
└── characters/
    └── <character-uuid>/
        ├── references/
        │   ├── face/
        │   ├── body/
        │   ├── hair/
        │   ├── poses/
        │   └── expressions/
        │
        ├── generated/
        ├── thumbnails/
        └── exports/
```

Jangan:

```text
characters/Qowner/
```

Tetap gunakan UUID.

Nama Character bisa berubah tanpa memindahkan directory.

lanjutkan





Lanjut ke **Sprint 09 — Character Workspace + Identity Preservation UI**.

# Step 09.4 — Character Header

`character_header.dart`

```dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../../../design_system/colors/void_colors.dart';
import '../../../../design_system/typography/void_typography.dart';

class CharacterHeader extends StatelessWidget {
  const CharacterHeader({
    super.key,
    required this.name,
    required this.alias,
  });

  final String name;
  final String alias;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      height: 58,
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
        horizontal: 18,
      ),
      decoration: const BoxDecoration(
        color: VoidColors.panelSecondary,
        border: Border(
          bottom: BorderSide(
            color: VoidColors.borderSoft,
          ),
        ),
      ),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          const Icon(
            Icons.person_outline,
            size: 18,
            color: VoidColors.primaryBright,
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 10),
          Column(
            mainAxisAlignment:
                MainAxisAlignment.center,
            crossAxisAlignment:
                CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              Text(
                name,
                style: VoidTypography.title,
              ),
              if (alias.isNotEmpty)
                Text(
                  alias,
                  style: VoidTypography.caption,
                ),
            ],
          ),
          const Spacer(),
          IconButton(
            tooltip: 'Favorite',
            onPressed: () {},
            icon: const Icon(
              Icons.star_border,
            ),
          ),
          IconButton(
            tooltip: 'History',
            onPressed: () {},
            icon: const Icon(
              Icons.history,
            ),
          ),
          IconButton(
            tooltip: 'More',
            onPressed: () {},
            icon: const Icon(
              Icons.more_horiz,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

# Step 09.5 — Portrait Card

`character_portrait_card.dart`

```dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../../../design_system/colors/void_colors.dart';
import '../../../../design_system/typography/void_typography.dart';
import '../../../../design_system/widgets/void_card.dart';

class CharacterPortraitCard
    extends StatelessWidget {
  const CharacterPortraitCard({
    super.key,
    this.imagePath,
  });

  final String? imagePath;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return VoidCard(
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment:
            CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          const Text(
            'Portrait',
            style:
                VoidTypography.headingMedium,
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 14),
          AspectRatio(
            aspectRatio: 3 / 4,
            child: Container(
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color:
                    VoidColors.surfaceElevated,
                borderRadius:
                    BorderRadius.circular(10),
                border: Border.all(
                  color:
                      VoidColors.borderSoft,
                ),
              ),
              child: imagePath == null
                  ? const Center(
                      child: Column(
                        mainAxisSize:
                            MainAxisSize.min,
                        children: [
                          Icon(
                            Icons.person_outline,
                            size: 42,
                            color:
                                VoidColors.textMuted,
                          ),
                          SizedBox(height: 10),
                          Text(
                            'No portrait',
                            style:
                                VoidTypography.caption,
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    )
                  : ClipRRect(
                      borderRadius:
                          BorderRadius.circular(10),
                      child: Image.asset(
                        imagePath!,
                        fit: BoxFit.cover,
                      ),
                    ),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          SizedBox(
            width: double.infinity,
            child: OutlinedButton.icon(
              onPressed: () {},
              icon: const Icon(
                Icons.add_photo_alternate_outlined,
              ),
              label: const Text(
                'Add Portrait',
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

Nanti asset lokal jangan selalu memakai `Image.asset`; file project akan memakai `Image.file`. Tetapi untuk UI foundation cukup.

# Step 09.6 — Identity Card

`character_identity_card.dart`

```dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../../../design_system/typography/void_typography.dart';
import '../../../../design_system/widgets/void_card.dart';

class CharacterIdentityCard
    extends StatelessWidget {
  const CharacterIdentityCard({
    super.key,
    required this.name,
    required this.alias,
    required this.height,
    required this.hair,
    required this.eyes,
    required this.age,
  });

  final String name;
  final String alias;
  final String height;
  final String hair;
  final String eyes;
  final String age;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return VoidCard(
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment:
            CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          const Text(
            'Character Identity',
            style:
                VoidTypography.headingMedium,
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 18),

          _IdentityRow(
            label: 'Name',
            value: name,
          ),
          _IdentityRow(
            label: 'Alias',
            value:
                alias.isEmpty ? '—' : alias,
          ),
          _IdentityRow(
            label: 'Age',
            value: age,
          ),
          _IdentityRow(
            label: 'Height',
            value: height,
          ),
          _IdentityRow(
            label: 'Hair',
            value: hair,
          ),
          _IdentityRow(
            label: 'Eyes',
            value: eyes,
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _IdentityRow extends StatelessWidget {
  const _IdentityRow({
    required this.label,
    required this.value,
  });

  final String label;
  final String value;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Padding(
      padding:
          const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
        vertical: 7,
      ),
      child: Row(
        crossAxisAlignment:
            CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          SizedBox(
            width: 90,
            child: Text(
              label,
              style:
                  VoidTypography.caption,
            ),
          ),
          Expanded(
            child: Text(
              value.isEmpty ? '—' : value,
              style:
                  VoidTypography.body,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}
```
