# Step 09.7 — Turnaround Card

```dart
class TurnaroundCard extends StatelessWidget {
  const TurnaroundCard({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return VoidCard(
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment:
            CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          const Text(
            'Turnaround',
            style:
                VoidTypography.headingMedium,
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 14),
          GridView.count(
            shrinkWrap: true,
            physics:
                const NeverScrollableScrollPhysics(),
            crossAxisCount: 4,
            crossAxisSpacing: 10,
            mainAxisSpacing: 10,
            childAspectRatio: .72,
            children: const [
              _TurnaroundSlot(
                label: 'Front',
              ),
              _TurnaroundSlot(
                label: 'Side',
              ),
              _TurnaroundSlot(
                label: 'Back',
              ),
              _TurnaroundSlot(
                label: '3/4',
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _TurnaroundSlot
    extends StatelessWidget {
  const _TurnaroundSlot({
    required this.label,
  });

  final String label;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color:
            VoidColors.surfaceElevated,
        borderRadius:
            BorderRadius.circular(8),
        border: Border.all(
          color:
              VoidColors.borderSoft,
        ),
      ),
      child: Column(
        mainAxisAlignment:
            MainAxisAlignment.center,
        children: [
          const Icon(
            Icons.accessibility_new,
            color:
                VoidColors.textMuted,
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          Text(
            label,
            style:
                VoidTypography.caption,
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

Tambahkan imports dari `VoidColors`, `VoidTypography`, dan `VoidCard`.

# Step 09.8 — Color Palette

Kita mulai siapkan sistem warna karakter:

```dart
class ColorPaletteCard
    extends StatelessWidget {
  const ColorPaletteCard({
    super.key,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return VoidCard(
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment:
            CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: const [
          Text(
            'Color Palette',
            style:
                VoidTypography.headingMedium,
          ),
          SizedBox(height: 16),

          Wrap(
            spacing: 10,
            runSpacing: 10,
            children: [
              _ColorChip(
                label: 'Void Black',
                color:
                    Color(0xFF050507),
              ),
              _ColorChip(
                label: 'Void Violet',
                color:
                    Color(0xFF5A2DFF),
              ),
              _ColorChip(
                label:
                    'Radiant Purple',
                color:
                    Color(0xFFA347FF),
              ),
              _ColorChip(
                label:
                    'Electric Blue',
                color:
                    Color(0xFF1E8DFF),
              ),
              _ColorChip(
                label:
                    'Stellar White',
                color:
                    Color(0xFFFFF8FF),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _ColorChip
    extends StatelessWidget {
  const _ColorChip({
    required this.label,
    required this.color,
  });

  final String label;
  final Color color;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Tooltip(
      message: label,
      child: Container(
        width: 46,
        height: 46,
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: color,
          borderRadius:
              BorderRadius.circular(8),
          border: Border.all(
            color:
                VoidColors.border,
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

Nanti warna tidak hard-code di widget. Kita akan menyimpan palette ke database. Untuk saat ini ini hanya visual prototype.

# Step 09.10 — Accessories Card

Untuk sekarang:

```text
Accessories

Necklace
Bracelet
Watch
Other
```

UI:

```dart
class AccessoriesCard
    extends StatelessWidget {
  const AccessoriesCard({
    super.key,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return VoidCard(
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment:
            CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: const [
          Text(
            'Accessories',
            style:
                VoidTypography.headingMedium,
          ),
          SizedBox(height: 14),

          _AccessoryItem(
            icon:
                Icons.diamond_outlined,
            label: 'Necklace',
          ),

          _AccessoryItem(
            icon:
                Icons.circle_outlined,
            label: 'Bracelet',
          ),

          _AccessoryItem(
            icon:
                Icons.watch_outlined,
            label: 'Watch',
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _AccessoryItem
    extends StatelessWidget {
  const _AccessoryItem({
    required this.icon,
    required this.label,
  });

  final IconData icon;
  final String label;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Padding(
      padding:
          const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
        vertical: 7,
      ),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          Icon(
            icon,
            size: 17,
            color:
                VoidColors.textMuted,
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 9),
          Text(
            label,
            style:
                VoidTypography.body,
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

# Step 09.11 — Identity Preservation Card

Sekarang salah satu bagian terpenting.

```text
IDENTITY PRESERVATION

Face Lock              ON
Hair Geometry Lock     ON

Color Lock             ON
Outfit Geometry Lock   OFF
Material Lock          OFF
Accessory Lock         OFF
Weapon Lock            OFF
```

Buat:

```dart
class IdentityPreservationCard
    extends StatelessWidget {
  const IdentityPreservationCard({
    super.key,
    required this.faceLock,
    required this.hairLock,
    required this.bodyLock,
    required this.colorLock,
    required this.outfitLock,
    required this.onFaceChanged,
    required this.onHairChanged,
    required this.onBodyChanged,
    required this.onColorChanged,
    required this.onOutfitChanged,
  });

  final bool faceLock;
  final bool hairLock;
  final bool bodyLock;
  final bool colorLock;
  final bool outfitLock;

  final ValueChanged<bool>
      onFaceChanged;
  final ValueChanged<bool>
      onHairChanged;
  final ValueChanged<bool>
      onBodyChanged;
  final ValueChanged<bool>
      onColorChanged;
  final ValueChanged<bool>
      onOutfitChanged;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return VoidCard(
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment:
            CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          const Text(
            'Identity Preservation',
            style:
                VoidTypography.headingMedium,
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),
          const Text(
            'Control which character traits must remain consistent.',
            style: VoidTypography.caption,
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 14),

          _LockRow(
            label: 'Face Lock',
            value: faceLock,
            onChanged:
                onFaceChanged,
          ),

          _LockRow(
            label:
                'Hair Geometry Lock',
            value: hairLock,
            onChanged:
                onHairChanged,
          ),

          _LockRow(
            label:
                'Body Topology Lock',
            value: bodyLock,
            onChanged:
                onBodyChanged,
          ),

          _LockRow(
            label: 'Color Lock',
            value: colorLock,
            onChanged:
                onColorChanged,
          ),

          _LockRow(
            label:
                'Outfit Geometry Lock',
            value: outfitLock,
            onChanged:
                onOutfitChanged,
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _LockRow extends StatelessWidget {
  const _LockRow({
    required this.label,
    required this.value,
    required this.onChanged,
  });

  final String label;
  final bool value;
  final ValueChanged<bool> onChanged;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Row(
      children: [
        Expanded(
          child: Text(
            label,
            style:
                VoidTypography.body,
          ),
        ),
        Switch(
          value: value,
          onChanged: onChanged,
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}
```

Nanti perubahan `Switch` langsung mengupdate `CharacterIdentityProfiles` dari Sprint 08.

# Step 09.13 — Workspace Layout

`character_workspace_screen.dart`

```dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

import '../../../design_system/colors/void_colors.dart';
import 'widgets/accessories_card.dart';
import 'widgets/character_header.dart';
import 'widgets/character_identity_card.dart';
import 'widgets/character_portrait_card.dart';
import 'widgets/color_palette_card.dart';
import 'widgets/identity_preservation_card.dart';
import 'widgets/references_card.dart';
import 'widgets/silhouette_card.dart';
import 'widgets/turnaround_card.dart';

class CharacterWorkspaceScreen
    extends StatefulWidget {
  const CharacterWorkspaceScreen({
    super.key,
  });

  @override
  State<CharacterWorkspaceScreen>
      createState() =>
          _CharacterWorkspaceScreenState();
}

class _CharacterWorkspaceScreenState
    extends State<
        CharacterWorkspaceScreen> {
  bool faceLock = true;
  bool hairLock = true;
  bool bodyLock = true;
  bool colorLock = true;
  bool outfitLock = false;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      color:
          VoidColors.backgroundSecondary,
      child: Column(
        children: [
          const CharacterHeader(
            name: 'Qowner',
            alias: 'Void Scriptor',
          ),

          Expanded(
            child: LayoutBuilder(
              builder: (
                context,
                constraints,
              ) {
                final desktop =
                    constraints.maxWidth >=
                        1100;

                return SingleChildScrollView(
                  padding:
                      const EdgeInsets.all(18),
                  child: desktop
                      ? _buildDesktop()
                      : _buildCompact(),
                );
              },
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildDesktop() {
    return Column(
      children: [
        Row(
          crossAxisAlignment:
              CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            const SizedBox(
              width: 280,
              child:
                  CharacterPortraitCard(),
            ),
            const SizedBox(width: 16),
            const Expanded(
              child:
                  CharacterIdentityCard(
                name: 'Qowner',
                alias: 'Void Scriptor',
                age: '24',
                height: '185 cm',
                hair:
                    'White with violet highlights',
                eyes: 'Violet',
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        const TurnaroundCard(),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        const Row(
          crossAxisAlignment:
              CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Expanded(
              child: SilhouetteCard(),
            ),
            SizedBox(width: 16),
            Expanded(
              child: ColorPaletteCard(),
            ),
            SizedBox(width: 16),
            Expanded(
              child: AccessoriesCard(),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        IdentityPreservationCard(
          faceLock: faceLock,
          hairLock: hairLock,
          bodyLock: bodyLock,
          colorLock: colorLock,
          outfitLock: outfitLock,
          onFaceChanged: (value) {
            setState(() {
              faceLock = value;
            });
          },
          onHairChanged: (value) {
            setState(() {
              hairLock = value;
            });
          },
          onBodyChanged: (value) {
            setState(() {
              bodyLock = value;
            });
          },
          onColorChanged: (value) {
            setState(() {
              colorLock = value;
            });
          },
          onOutfitChanged: (value) {
            setState(() {
              outfitLock = value;
            });
          },
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        const ReferencesCard(),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildCompact() {
    return Column(
      children: [
        const CharacterPortraitCard(),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        const CharacterIdentityCard(
          name: 'Qowner',
          alias: 'Void Scriptor',
          age: '24',
          height: '185 cm',
          hair:
              'White with violet highlights',
          eyes: 'Violet',
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        const TurnaroundCard(),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        const SilhouetteCard(),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        const ColorPaletteCard(),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        const AccessoriesCard(),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        IdentityPreservationCard(
          faceLock: faceLock,
          hairLock: hairLock,
          bodyLock: bodyLock,
          colorLock: colorLock,
          outfitLock: outfitLock,
          onFaceChanged: (value) {
            setState(() {
              faceLock = value;
            });
          },
          onHairChanged: (value) {
            setState(() {
              hairLock = value;
            });
          },
          onBodyChanged: (value) {
            setState(() {
              bodyLock = value;
            });
          },
          onColorChanged: (value) {
            setState(() {
              colorLock = value;
            });
          },
          onOutfitChanged: (value) {
            setState(() {
              outfitLock = value;
            });
          },
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        const ReferencesCard(),
      ],
    );
  }
}
```

Berikutnya paling tepat adalah **Sprint 10 — Selection Engine + Dynamic Inspector + Asset Relation Core**.

Di sprint itu, saat user mengklik Qowner, Wardrobe, atau Weapon, Inspector kanan akan otomatis berubah sesuai objek yang dipilih. Kita juga akan mulai membuat hubungan nyata seperti `Qowner → wears → Void Coat` dan `Qowner → uses → Dual Dagger`, sehingga sistem asset Void Scriptor mulai bekerja seperti database kreatif yang benar-benar saling terhubung. memcite

lanjutkan

Lanjut ke **Sprint 10 — Selection Engine + Dynamic Inspector + Asset Relation Core**.

Ini sprint penting karena setelah ini UI tidak lagi terasa statis. Klik Character, Wardrobe, Weapon, atau asset lain akan mengubah isi Inspector secara otomatis. Relasi seperti `Qowner → wears → Void Coat` dan `Qowner → uses → Dual Dagger` juga mulai menjadi data nyata.

# Step 10.2 — Selection Provider

`selection_controller.dart`

```dart
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';

import 'selection_model.dart';

class SelectionController
    extends Notifier<SelectionModel?> {
  @override
  SelectionModel? build() {
    return null;
  }

  void select(
    SelectionModel selection,
  ) {
    state = selection;
  }

  void clear() {
    state = null;
  }
}

final selectionProvider =
    NotifierProvider<
        SelectionController,
        SelectionModel?>(
  SelectionController.new,
);
```

Mulai sekarang jangan buat provider terpisah seperti:

```text
selectedCharacterId
selectedWeaponId
selectedWardrobeId
```

sebagai sumber utama.

Gunakan satu selection engine.

# Step 10.3 — Explorer Selection

Saat user klik Character:

```dart
ref
    .read(selectionProvider.notifier)
    .select(
      SelectionModel(
        assetId: character.assetId,
        assetType: AssetType.character,
        projectId: character.projectId,
      ),
    );
```

Weapon:

```dart
ref
    .read(selectionProvider.notifier)
    .select(
      SelectionModel(
        assetId: weapon.assetId,
        assetType: AssetType.weapon,
        projectId: weapon.projectId,
      ),
    );
```

Dengan begitu seluruh aplikasi tahu asset aktif.

# Step 10.4 — Inspector Resolver

Buat:

`features/inspector/presentation/inspector_resolver.dart`

```dart
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';

import '../../../core/selection/selection_controller.dart';
import '../../../features/assets/domain/asset_type.dart';
import 'inspectors/character_inspector.dart';
import 'inspectors/empty_inspector.dart';
import 'inspectors/weapon_inspector.dart';
import 'inspectors/wardrobe_inspector.dart';

class InspectorResolver
    extends ConsumerWidget {
  const InspectorResolver({
    super.key,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(
    BuildContext context,
    WidgetRef ref,
  ) {
    final selection =
        ref.watch(selectionProvider);

    if (selection == null) {
      return const EmptyInspector();
    }

    switch (selection.assetType) {
      case AssetType.character:
        return CharacterInspector(
          characterId:
              selection.assetId,
        );

      case AssetType.wardrobe:
        return WardrobeInspector(
          wardrobeId:
              selection.assetId,
        );

      case AssetType.weapon:
        return WeaponInspector(
          weaponId:
              selection.assetId,
        );

      default:
        return const EmptyInspector();
    }
  }
}
```

---

# Step 10.10 — Relation Types

`asset_relation_type.dart`

```dart
enum AssetRelationType {
  uses,
  wears,
  owns,
  appearsIn,
  locatedIn,
  createdBy,
  memberOf,
  enemyOf,
  allyOf,
  relatedTo,
}
```

Tambahkan helper untuk database:

```dart
extension AssetRelationTypeValue
    on AssetRelationType {
  String get value {
    return switch (this) {
      AssetRelationType.uses =>
        'uses',
      AssetRelationType.wears =>
        'wears',
      AssetRelationType.owns =>
        'owns',
      AssetRelationType.appearsIn =>
        'appears_in',
      AssetRelationType.locatedIn =>
        'located_in',
      AssetRelationType.createdBy =>
        'created_by',
      AssetRelationType.memberOf =>
        'member_of',
      AssetRelationType.enemyOf =>
        'enemy_of',
      AssetRelationType.allyOf =>
        'ally_of',
      AssetRelationType.relatedTo =>
        'related_to',
    };
  }
}
```

# Step 10.11 — Asset Relations Table

Yang kita siapkan sebelumnya sekarang dibuat lebih lengkap:

```dart
class AssetRelations extends Table {
  TextColumn get id => text()();

  TextColumn get projectId =>
      text()();

  TextColumn get sourceAssetId =>
      text()();

  TextColumn get targetAssetId =>
      text()();

  TextColumn get relationType =>
      text()();

  DateTimeColumn get createdAt =>
      dateTime()();

  @override
  Set<Column<Object>> get primaryKey => {
        id,
      };
}
```

Database schema naik:

```dart
@override
int get schemaVersion => 3;
```

Migration:

```dart
if (from < 3) {
  await m.createTable(
    assetRelations,
  );
}
```

---

# Step 10.12 — Relation DAO

```dart
@DriftAccessor(
  tables: [
    AssetRelations,
    Assets,
  ],
)
class RelationDao
    extends DatabaseAccessor<ProjectDatabase>
    with _$RelationDaoMixin {
  RelationDao(super.db);

  Future<List<AssetRelation>>
      getOutgoingRelations(
    String assetId,
  ) {
    return (
      select(assetRelations)
        ..where(
          (row) =>
              row.sourceAssetId
                  .equals(assetId),
        )
    ).get();
  }

  Future<List<AssetRelation>>
      getIncomingRelations(
    String assetId,
  ) {
    return (
      select(assetRelations)
        ..where(
          (row) =>
              row.targetAssetId
                  .equals(assetId),
        )
    ).get();
  }
}
```

Nama generated class mungkin perlu dibedakan dari domain `AssetRelation`, misalnya `AssetRelationRow`.

---

# Step 10.13 — Relation Repository

```dart
abstract interface class
    RelationRepository {
  Future<List<AssetRelation>>
      outgoing(
    String assetId,
  );

  Future<List<AssetRelation>>
      incoming(
    String assetId,
  );

  Future<void> create(
    AssetRelation relation,
  );

  Future<void> delete(
    String relationId,
  );
}
```

---

# Step 10.14 — Create Relation Service

Flow:

```text
Qowner
↓
Add Relation

Type:
wears

Target:
Void Coat

↓
CREATE

Qowner ──wears──► Void Coat
```

Service:

```dart
class CreateRelationService {
  CreateRelationService(
    this.repository,
  );

  final RelationRepository repository;

  Future<void> call({
    required String projectId,
    required String sourceId,
    required String targetId,
    required AssetRelationType type,
  }) async {
    if (sourceId == targetId) {
      throw ArgumentError(
        'An asset cannot relate to itself.',
      );
    }

    final relation =
        AssetRelation(
      id: const Uuid().v4(),
      projectId: projectId,
      sourceAssetId: sourceId,
      targetAssetId: targetId,
      type: type,
      createdAt: DateTime.now(),
    );

    await repository.create(
      relation,
    );
  }
}
```

```text
uses
```

Widget:

```dart
class RelationRow
    extends StatelessWidget {
  const RelationRow({
    super.key,
    required this.type,
    required this.targetName,
    required this.onTap,
  });

  final String type;
  final String targetName;
  final VoidCallback onTap;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return InkWell(
      onTap: onTap,
      child: Padding(
        padding:
            const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
          vertical: 7,
        ),
        child: Row(
          children: [
            SizedBox(
              width: 72,
              child: Text(
                type,
                style:
                    VoidTypography.caption,
              ),
            ),
            Expanded(
              child: Text(
                targetName,
                style:
                    VoidTypography.body,
              ),
            ),
            const Icon(
              Icons.chevron_right,
              size: 16,
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

Ketika relation diklik:

```text
Void Coat
```

menjadi selected asset.

Inspector otomatis berubah dari Character ke Wardrobe.

Itulah manfaat Selection Engine.

# Step 10.17 — Workspace Resolver

Inspector bukan satu-satunya yang perlu resolver.

Sekarang kita buat:

```text
WorkspaceResolver
```

Flow:

```text
selection == null
↓
Project Overview

Character
↓
Character Workspace

Wardrobe
↓
Wardrobe Workspace

Weapon
↓
Weapon Workspace
```

Contoh:

```dart
class WorkspaceResolver
    extends ConsumerWidget {
  const WorkspaceResolver({
    super.key,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(
    BuildContext context,
    WidgetRef ref,
  ) {
    final selection =
        ref.watch(selectionProvider);

    if (selection == null) {
      return const ProjectOverview();
    }

    return switch (
      selection.assetType
    ) {
      AssetType.character =>
        CharacterWorkspaceScreen(
          characterId:
              selection.assetId,
        ),

      AssetType.wardrobe =>
        WardrobeWorkspaceScreen(
          wardrobeId:
              selection.assetId,
        ),

      AssetType.weapon =>
        WeaponWorkspaceScreen(
          weaponId:
              selection.assetId,
        ),

      _ => const ProjectOverview(),
    };
  }
}
```

Sekarang:

```text
Explorer
    │
    ▼
Selection Engine
   ┌┴─────────────┐
   ▼              ▼
Workspace      Inspector
Resolver       Resolver
```

Ini salah satu perubahan arsitektur paling penting sejauh ini.

# Step 10.23 — Delete Safety

Kalau asset memiliki relation:

```text
Delete Qowner?
```

jangan langsung hapus.

Tampilkan:

```text
This character has:

3 wardrobe relations
4 weapon relations
7 story references

Deleting it will remove these relations.

[Cancel]
[Delete]
```

Default database rule sebaiknya:

```text
delete asset
↓
delete relations referencing asset
```

tetapi tidak menghapus target asset lain.

Menghapus Qowner tidak berarti Void Coat ikut hilang.

Berikutnya adalah **Sprint 11 — Wardrobe System + Wardrobe Detail Workspace**. Kita akan membuat Wardrobe sebagai asset penuh, menghubungkannya dengan Character lewat `wears`, membuat material/fabric/color metadata, variant outfit, reference images, dan **Wardrobe Detail Sheet workspace** yang tetap mengikuti standar standalone garment tanpa mannequin/character. memcite

lanjutkan

Kita lanjut ke **Sprint 11 — Wardrobe System + Wardrobe Detail Workspace**.

Sprint ini membuat Wardrobe menjadi asset penuh yang bisa berdiri sendiri, punya metadata material, warna, komponen, variant, reference, dan relation ke Character. Kita juga mulai menyiapkan workspace khusus Wardrobe Detail Sheet.

### Step 11.1 — Wardrobe model

`features/wardrobe/domain/wardrobe_model.dart`

```dart
class WardrobeModel {
  const WardrobeModel({
    required this.assetId,
    required this.projectId,
    required this.name,
    this.category = '',
    this.description = '',
    this.primaryMaterial = '',
    this.fabric = '',
    this.fit = '',
    this.style = '',
    this.notes = '',
  });

  final String assetId;
  final String projectId;

  final String name;
  final String category;
  final String description;

  final String primaryMaterial;

  final String fit;
  final String style;

  final String notes;
```

```dart
    required String projectId,
    required String name,

    final id = const Uuid().v4();

    // Assets
    // Wardrobes
    // WardrobeVariants
```

### Step 11.11 — Wardrobe header

```dart
class WardrobeHeader extends StatelessWidget {
  const WardrobeHeader({
    super.key,
    required this.name,
    required this.category,
  });

  final String name;
  final String category;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      height: 58,
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
        horizontal: 18,
      ),
      decoration: const BoxDecoration(
        color: VoidColors.panelSecondary,
        border: Border(
          bottom: BorderSide(
            color: VoidColors.borderSoft,
          ),
        ),
      ),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          const Icon(
            Icons.checkroom_outlined,
            color: VoidColors.primaryBright,
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 10),
          Column(
            mainAxisAlignment:
                MainAxisAlignment.center,
            crossAxisAlignment:
                CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              Text(
                name,
                style: VoidTypography.title,
              ),
              Text(
                category,
                style: VoidTypography.caption,
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const Spacer(),
          IconButton(
            onPressed: () {},
            icon: const Icon(
              Icons.history,
            ),
          ),
          IconButton(
            onPressed: () {},
            icon: const Icon(
              Icons.more_horiz,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

### Step 11.16 — Wardrobe Workspace

Buat `wardrobe_workspace_screen.dart`.

Struktur sederhananya:

```dart
class WardrobeWorkspaceScreen
    extends StatelessWidget {
  const WardrobeWorkspaceScreen({
    super.key,
    required this.wardrobeId,
  });

  final String wardrobeId;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      color: VoidColors.backgroundSecondary,
      child: Column(
        children: [
          const WardrobeHeader(
            name: 'Void Coat',
            category: 'Combat Outfit',
          ),
          Expanded(
            child: LayoutBuilder(
              builder: (
                context,
                constraints,
              ) {
                final wide =
                    constraints.maxWidth >=
                        1000;

                return SingleChildScrollView(
                  padding:
                      const EdgeInsets.all(18),
                  child: wide
                      ? const _DesktopWardrobe()
                      : const _CompactWardrobe(),
                );
              },
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

Desktop:

```dart
class _DesktopWardrobe
    extends StatelessWidget {
  const _DesktopWardrobe();

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Column(
      children: [
        const Row(
          crossAxisAlignment:
              CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            SizedBox(
              width: 300,
              child:
                  WardrobeProductCard(),
            ),
            SizedBox(width: 16),
            Expanded(
              child:
                  WardrobeSpecificationCard(
                category: 'Outerwear',
                material:
                    'Technical Synthetic',
                fabric:
                    'Waterproof Fabric',
                fit: 'Oversized',
                style:
                    'Futuristic Tactical',
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        const WardrobeComponentsCard(),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        const WardrobeColorPaletteCard(),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        const WardrobeMaterialCard(),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        const WardrobeVariantsCard(),
        const SizedBox(height: 16),
        const WardrobeReferencesCard(),
      ],
    );
  }
}
```

### Step 11.17 — Workspace Resolver

Sekarang branch Wardrobe dari Sprint 10 menjadi benar-benar aktif:

```dart
AssetType.wardrobe =>
    WardrobeWorkspaceScreen(
      wardrobeId:
          selection.assetId,
    ),
```

### Step 11.18 — Wardrobe Inspector

Target:

```text
INSPECTOR

Void Coat
Wardrobe

Properties
---------
Category
Combat Outfit

Material
Technical Synthetic

Fabric
Waterproof

Fit
Oversized

Variants
--------
Normal
Combat
Damaged

Relations
---------
worn by → Qowner

Tags
----
void
combat
outerwear
```

Relation inverse sangat berguna.

Jika database berisi:

```text
Qowner ─wears→ Void Coat
```

Saat membuka Void Coat kita tampilkan:

```text
worn by ← Qowner
```

Jadi Relation Repository harus mendukung incoming + outgoing relation.

### Step 11.19 — Character → Wardrobe relation

Create relation:

```text
SOURCE
Qowner

TYPE
wears

TARGET
Void Coat
```

Database:

```text
source = Qowner UUID
target = Void Coat UUID
type   = wears
```

Character Workspace kemudian punya section:

```text
WARDROBE

Void Coat
Combat Outfit

[Open]
```

Klik Open mengubah selection menjadi Wardrobe.

### Step 11.20 — Wardrobe primary/default

Satu Character bisa punya banyak outfit:

```text
Qowner
├── Daily Outfit
├── Combat Outfit
├── Desert Outfit
└── Formal Outfit
```

Kita nanti membutuhkan relation metadata seperti:

```text
isDefault
context
priority
```

Untuk sekarang belum perlu mengubah schema relation. Tetapi kita sudah tahu Relation Core akan berkembang menjadi relation dengan metadata.

### Step 11.21 — Wardrobe Detail Sheet mode

Kita juga mulai menyiapkan mode:

```text
VIEW MODE

Workspace
Detail Sheet
Full Screen Preview
```

`Detail Sheet` akan menampilkan:

```text
WARDROBE DETAIL SHEET

Front Product View
Back Product View
Side Product View

Exploded Components

Fabric Samples

Color Palette

Construction Notes

Material Specification

Measurements
```

Dan mode ini sengaja **tidak membutuhkan karakter atau mannequin**, sehingga hasil sheet murni fokus ke garment.

### Step 11.22 — Export

Nanti Wardrobe Detail Sheet bisa diekspor sebagai:

```text
PNG
JPG
PDF
```

Belum kita implementasikan sekarang, tetapi workspace harus dirancang supaya card bisa di-render menjadi export canvas kemudian.

### Step 11.23 — Android

Pada Android layout menjadi:

```text
Void Coat

[Product View]

Specification

Components

Color Palette

Material

Variants

References

Relations
```

Tidak ada tiga kolom.

Inspector tetap lewat bottom sheet.

### Step 11.24 — Migration

Setelah table baru:

```bash
dart run build_runner build --delete-conflicting-outputs
dart format lib
flutter analyze
flutter test
```

Test schema dari project lama juga penting:

```text
Open project schema 3
↓
Migration
↓
schema 4
↓
Wardrobe tables tersedia
```

Jangan hanya test project baru.

### Step 11.25 — Test scenario

Buat:

```text
Wardrobe
Void Coat
```

Kemudian:

```text
Qowner
↓
Add Relation
↓
wears
↓
Void Coat
```

Expected:

```text
Qowner Inspector
Relations
wears → Void Coat
```

Klik Void Coat.

Expected:

```text
Workspace:
Wardrobe Detail

Inspector:
Void Coat
Wardrobe

Relations:
worn by ← Qowner
```

Restart aplikasi.

Wardrobe dan relation harus tetap ada.

Progress kita sekarang:

```text
01  Multiplatform Foundation       ✅
02  Design System                  ✅
03  Adaptive Shell                 ✅

06  Project System                 ✅

11  Wardrobe System                ◀ CURRENT
```

Berikutnya paling tepat adalah **Sprint 12 — Weapon System + Weapon Detail Workspace**. Struktur dasarnya akan mirip Wardrobe tetapi memiliki weapon type, dimensions, materials, energy/core, variants, combat properties, technical views, relation `Character → uses → Weapon`, dan Weapon Detail Sheet. Setelah itu kita bisa mulai menggabungkan Character + Wardrobe + Weapon menjadi satu **Character Loadout System**. memcite

lanjutkan

Lanjut ke **Sprint 12 — Weapon System + Weapon Detail Workspace**.

Sprint ini membuat Weapon menjadi asset penuh yang punya struktur teknis sendiri, bisa punya variant, material, dimension, energy/core, reference image, serta relation ke Character.

Target utamanya:

```text
Qowner
  └── uses ──► Void Dual Dagger
```

dan ketika Weapon dipilih:

```text
Workspace → Weapon Detail
Inspector → Weapon Inspector
```

## Struktur Weapon

```text
WEAPON ASSET
│
├── Identity
├── Weapon Type
├── Dimensions
├── Materials
├── Energy / Core
├── Combat Properties
├── Components
├── Variants
├── References
├── Technical Views
└── Relations
```

### Step 12.1 — Weapon model

Buat:

`features/weapon/domain/weapon_model.dart`

```dart
class WeaponModel {
  const WeaponModel({
    required this.assetId,
    required this.projectId,
    required this.name,
    this.weaponType = '',
    this.description = '',
    this.primaryMaterial = '',
    this.secondaryMaterial = '',
    this.energyType = '',
    this.coreType = '',
    this.lengthMm,
    this.weightKg,
    this.notes = '',
  });

  final String assetId;
  final String projectId;

  final String name;
  final String weaponType;
  final String description;

  final String primaryMaterial;
  final String secondaryMaterial;

  final String energyType;
  final String coreType;

  final double? lengthMm;
  final double? weightKg;

  final String notes;
}
```

---

### Step 12.2 — Weapon table

Tambahkan ke Drift:

```dart
class Weapons extends Table {
  TextColumn get assetId => text()();

  TextColumn get projectId => text()();

  TextColumn get name => text()();

  TextColumn get weaponType =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant(''),
      )();

  TextColumn get description =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant(''),
      )();

  TextColumn get primaryMaterial =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant(''),
      )();

  TextColumn get secondaryMaterial =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant(''),
      )();

  TextColumn get energyType =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant(''),
      )();

  TextColumn get coreType =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant(''),
      )();

  RealColumn get lengthMm =>
      real().nullable()();

  RealColumn get weightKg =>
      real().nullable()();

  TextColumn get notes =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant(''),
      )();

  @override
  Set<Column<Object>> get primaryKey => {
        assetId,
      };
}
```

Naikkan schema:

```dart
@override
int get schemaVersion => 5;
```

Migration:

```dart
if (from < 5) {
  await m.createTable(weapons);
}
```

### Step 12.3 — Weapon Components

Satu Weapon bisa terdiri dari beberapa bagian.

Contoh:

```text
Void Dagger
├── Blade
├── Edge
├── Guard
├── Handle
├── Core
└── Energy Channel
```

Buat:

```dart
class WeaponComponents extends Table {
  TextColumn get id => text()();

  TextColumn get weaponId => text()();

  TextColumn get name => text()();

  TextColumn get componentType => text()();

  TextColumn get material =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant(''),
      )();

  TextColumn get description =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant(''),
      )();

  IntColumn get sortOrder =>
      integer().withDefault(
        const Constant(0),
      )();

  @override
  Set<Column<Object>> get primaryKey => {
        id,
      };
}
```

---

### Step 12.4 — Weapon Dimensions

Untuk detail sheet, dimensi penting.

Contoh:

```text
Overall Length    420 mm
Blade Length      260 mm
Handle Length     145 mm
Blade Width        38 mm
Weight            0.82 kg
```

Untuk sekarang bisa ditaruh pada `WeaponComponents`, tetapi kita sebaiknya siapkan table khusus:

```dart
class WeaponMeasurements extends Table {
  TextColumn get id => text()();

  TextColumn get weaponId => text()();

  TextColumn get label => text()();

  RealColumn get value => real()();

  TextColumn get unit => text()();

  IntColumn get sortOrder =>
      integer().withDefault(
        const Constant(0),
      )();

  @override
  Set<Column<Object>> get primaryKey => {
        id,
      };
}
```

Dengan ini Weapon tidak terkunci hanya pada unit `mm`.

### Step 12.6 — Energy/Core

Karena Weapon Void Scriptor bisa bersifat teknologi/fantasi, kita buat struktur yang tidak terlalu sempit.

```dart
class WeaponEnergyProfiles extends Table {
  TextColumn get weaponId => text()();

  TextColumn get energyType =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant(''),
      )();

  TextColumn get coreType =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant(''),
      )();

  TextColumn get activation =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant(''),
      )();

  TextColumn get emissionColor =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant(''),
      )();

  TextColumn get behavior =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant(''),
      )();

  @override
  Set<Column<Object>> get primaryKey => {
        weaponId,
      };
}
```

Contoh:

```text
Energy Type
Void Energy

Core
Crystalline Void Core

Activation
Grip + neural recognition

Emission
Violet

Behavior
Energy edge intensifies during combat
```

## Step 12.12 — Weapon Header

```dart
class WeaponHeader extends StatelessWidget {
  const WeaponHeader({
    super.key,
    required this.name,
    required this.type,
  });

  final String name;
  final String type;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      height: 58,
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
        horizontal: 18,
      ),
      decoration: const BoxDecoration(
        color: VoidColors.panelSecondary,
        border: Border(
          bottom: BorderSide(
            color: VoidColors.borderSoft,
          ),
        ),
      ),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          const Icon(
            Icons.auto_fix_high_outlined,
            color: VoidColors.primaryBright,
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 10),

          Column(
            mainAxisAlignment:
                MainAxisAlignment.center,
            crossAxisAlignment:
                CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              Text(
                name,
                style: VoidTypography.title,
              ),
              Text(
                type,
                style: VoidTypography.caption,
              ),
            ],
          ),

          const Spacer(),

          IconButton(
            tooltip: 'Favorite',
            onPressed: () {},
            icon: const Icon(
              Icons.star_border,
            ),
          ),

          IconButton(
            tooltip: 'History',
            onPressed: () {},
            icon: const Icon(
              Icons.history,
            ),
          ),

          IconButton(
            tooltip: 'More',
            onPressed: () {},
            icon: const Icon(
              Icons.more_horiz,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

```dart
                    BorderRadius.circular(10),

                  color:
                      VoidColors.borderSoft,
                ),
              ),
              child: const Center(
                child: Icon(

                      VoidColors.textMuted,
```

## Step 12.14 — Specification Card

```dart
class WeaponSpecificationCard
    extends StatelessWidget {
  const WeaponSpecificationCard({
    super.key,
    required this.type,
    required this.length,
    required this.weight,
    required this.material,
    required this.energy,
  });

  final String type;
  final String length;
  final String weight;
  final String material;
  final String energy;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return VoidCard(
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment:
            CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          const Text(
            'Specification',
            style:
                VoidTypography.headingMedium,
          ),

          const SizedBox(height: 16),

          InspectorPropertyRow(
            label: 'Type',
            value: type,
          ),

          InspectorPropertyRow(
            label: 'Length',
            value: length,
          ),

          InspectorPropertyRow(
            label: 'Weight',
            value: weight,
          ),

          InspectorPropertyRow(
            label: 'Material',
            value: material,
          ),

          InspectorPropertyRow(
            label: 'Energy',
            value: energy,
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

# Step 12.18 — Weapon Workspace Screen

`weapon_workspace_screen.dart`

```dart
class WeaponWorkspaceScreen
    extends StatelessWidget {
  const WeaponWorkspaceScreen({
    super.key,
    required this.weaponId,
  });

  final String weaponId;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      color:
          VoidColors.backgroundSecondary,
      child: Column(
        children: [
          const WeaponHeader(
            name: 'Void Dual Dagger',
            type: 'Dual Blade',
          ),

          Expanded(
            child: LayoutBuilder(
              builder: (
                context,
                constraints,
              ) {
                final wide =
                    constraints.maxWidth >=
                        1000;

                return SingleChildScrollView(
                  padding:
                      const EdgeInsets.all(18),
                  child: wide
                      ? const _WeaponDesktop()
                      : const _WeaponCompact(),
                );
              },
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

Desktop:

```dart
class _WeaponDesktop
    extends StatelessWidget {

                length: '420 mm',

                    'Void Alloy',
                energy:
                    'Void Energy',

            ),
          ],

              CrossAxisAlignment.start,

              child:

                  WeaponDimensionsCard(),
```

```text
Combat

Relations
---------
used by ← Qowner

Tags
----
void
dagger
combat
```

Buat `WeaponInspector` dengan komponen Inspector reusable dari Sprint 10.

# Step 12.21 — Character → Weapon relation

Data:

```text
Qowner ──uses──► Void Dual Dagger
```

```text

Void Dual Dagger >
```

```text
Qowner >
```

```text
↓

↓
```

Tidak perlu route baru.

# Step 12.22 — Dual Weapon

Jangan mengasumsikan satu Weapon asset selalu satu benda fisik.

Untuk `Dual Dagger`, Weapon asset bisa memiliki:

```text
quantity = 2
```

Kita bisa menambahkan nanti:

```dart
IntColumn get quantity =>
    integer().withDefault(
      const Constant(1),
    )();
```

Atau component:

```text
Left Dagger
Right Dagger
```

Saya lebih menyarankan `quantity + components`, sehingga database tetap fleksibel.

# Step 12.23 — Weapon Geometry Lock

Sekarang kita juga mulai menyiapkan Weapon Consistency.

Mirip Character Identity:

```text
Weapon Preservation

Material Lock
Color Lock
Core Lock
Scale Lock
Effect Lock
```

Model:

```dart
class WeaponPreservationProfile {
  const WeaponPreservationProfile({
    required this.weaponId,
    this.geometryLock = true,
    this.materialLock = true,
    this.colorLock = true,
    this.coreLock = true,
    this.scaleLock = true,
    this.effectLock = false,
  });

  final String weaponId;

  final bool geometryLock;
  final bool materialLock;
  final bool colorLock;
  final bool coreLock;
  final bool scaleLock;
  final bool effectLock;
}
```

Table bisa kita tambahkan sekarang atau Sprint AI Context nanti.

Lebih baik dibuat sekarang karena ini bagian domain Weapon.

Berikutnya saya sarankan **Sprint 13 — Character Loadout + Variant/Mode System**.

Sprint itu akan menghubungkan Character, Wardrobe, Weapon, Accessories, hairstyle variant, dan mode karakter ke satu konfigurasi. Misalnya `Qowner → Daily Mode`, `Combat Mode`, `Desert Mode`, dan seterusnya, sehingga ketika mode berubah, outfit + weapon + references + preservation rules ikut berubah sebagai satu paket. memcite

lanjutkan

Lanjut ke **Sprint 13 — Character Loadout + Variant/Mode System**.

Sprint ini mulai menyatukan semua sistem yang sudah kita bangun: Character, Wardrobe, Weapon, Accessories, Identity Lock, dan Relation Engine menjadi satu konfigurasi yang bisa diganti per mode karakter.

## Target Sprint 13

Contoh:

```text
Qowner

    ├── Weapon: Dual Dagger
    ├── Hair: Tied
    └── Environment Preset: Desert
```

Jadi satu Character bisa memiliki banyak **Mode/Loadout**, tanpa menduplikasi Character itu sendiri.

### Step 13.1 — Character Mode model

Buat:

`features/character_mode/domain/character_mode.dart`

```dart
class CharacterMode {
  const CharacterMode({
    required this.id,
    required this.projectId,
    required this.characterId,
    required this.name,
    this.description = '',
    this.isDefault = false,
    this.thumbnailPath,
  });

  final String id;
  final String projectId;
  final String characterId;

  final String name;
  final String description;

  final bool isDefault;

  final String? thumbnailPath;
}
```

### Step 13.2 — Character Modes table

```dart
class CharacterModes extends Table {
  TextColumn get id => text()();

  TextColumn get projectId => text()();

  TextColumn get characterId => text()();

  TextColumn get name => text()();

  TextColumn get description =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant(''),
      )();

  BoolColumn get isDefault =>
      boolean().withDefault(
        const Constant(false),
      )();

  TextColumn get thumbnailPath =>
      text().nullable()();

  DateTimeColumn get createdAt =>
      dateTime()();

  DateTimeColumn get updatedAt =>
      dateTime()();

  @override
  Set<Column<Object>> get primaryKey => {
        id,
      };
}
```

Naikkan schema:

```dart
@override
int get schemaVersion => 6;
```

Migration:

```dart
if (from < 6) {
  await m.createTable(characterModes);
}
```

---

## Step 13.3 — Loadout entries

Jangan membuat kolom seperti:

```text
wardrobeId
weapon1Id
weapon2Id
weapon3Id
```

Itu akan cepat buntu.

Gunakan entry generik.

```dart
class CharacterLoadoutEntries extends Table {
  TextColumn get id => text()();

  TextColumn get modeId => text()();

  TextColumn get assetId => text()();

  TextColumn get slot => text()();

  IntColumn get sortOrder =>
      integer().withDefault(
        const Constant(0),
      )();

  BoolColumn get enabled =>
      boolean().withDefault(
        const Constant(true),
      )();

  @override
  Set<Column<Object>> get primaryKey => {
        id,
      };
}
```

Slot dapat berupa:

```text
wardrobe
primary_weapon
secondary_weapon
accessory
hair_variant
vehicle
companion
```

Dengan sistem ini nanti bisa ditambah tanpa migrasi besar.

---

## Step 13.4 — Slot type

Jangan memakai string tersebar di aplikasi.

```dart
enum LoadoutSlot {
  wardrobe,
  primaryWeapon,
  secondaryWeapon,
  accessory,
  hairVariant,
  companion,
  vehicle,
}
```

Helper database:

```dart
extension LoadoutSlotValue
    on LoadoutSlot {
  String get value {
    return switch (this) {
      LoadoutSlot.wardrobe =>
        'wardrobe',
      LoadoutSlot.primaryWeapon =>
        'primary_weapon',
      LoadoutSlot.secondaryWeapon =>
        'secondary_weapon',
      LoadoutSlot.accessory =>
        'accessory',
      LoadoutSlot.hairVariant =>
        'hair_variant',
      LoadoutSlot.companion =>
        'companion',
      LoadoutSlot.vehicle =>
        'vehicle',
    };
  }
}
```

---

## Step 13.5 — Mode-specific identity override

Character memiliki Identity Profile global.

Tetapi mode tertentu bisa override.

Contoh:

```text
Qowner Global
Face Lock = ON
Hair Lock = ON
Body Lock = ON

Combat Mode Override
Hair Color Change Allowed = YES
Outfit Lock = ON
Weapon Lock = ON
```

Buat:

```dart
class CharacterModeIdentityOverrides
    extends Table {
  TextColumn get modeId => text()();

  BoolColumn get faceLock =>
      boolean().nullable()();

  BoolColumn get hairLock =>
      boolean().nullable()();

  BoolColumn get bodyLock =>
      boolean().nullable()();

  BoolColumn get colorLock =>
      boolean().nullable()();

  BoolColumn get outfitLock =>
      boolean().nullable()();

  BoolColumn get weaponLock =>
      boolean().nullable()();

  @override
  Set<Column<Object>> get primaryKey => {
        modeId,
      };
}
```

`nullable` penting.

`null` artinya:

```text
inherit from Character
```

Sedangkan `true/false` adalah override.

---

## Step 13.6 — Effective identity resolver

Kita butuh service:

```text
Character Identity
       +
Mode Override
       ↓
Effective Identity
```

Contoh:

```dart
class EffectiveIdentityProfile {
  const EffectiveIdentityProfile({
    required this.faceLock,
    required this.hairLock,
    required this.bodyLock,
    required this.colorLock,
    required this.outfitLock,
    required this.weaponLock,
  });

  final bool faceLock;
  final bool hairLock;
  final bool bodyLock;
  final bool colorLock;
  final bool outfitLock;
  final bool weaponLock;
}
```

Resolver:

```dart
EffectiveIdentityProfile resolveIdentity({
  required CharacterIdentityProfile base,
  required CharacterModeIdentityOverride? override,
}) {
  return EffectiveIdentityProfile(
    faceLock:
        override?.faceLock ??
        base.faceLock,
    hairLock:
        override?.hairLock ??
        base.hairLock,
    bodyLock:
        override?.bodyLock ??
        base.bodyLock,
    colorLock:
        override?.colorLock ??
        base.colorLock,
    outfitLock:
        override?.outfitLock ??
        base.outfitLock,
    weaponLock:
        override?.weaponLock ??
        false,
  );
}
```

Ini nanti sangat penting untuk AI Context Engine.

```dart
class CharacterModeReferences
    extends Table {
  TextColumn get id => text()();

  TextColumn get modeId => text()();

  TextColumn get referenceType => text()();

  TextColumn get filePath => text()();

  IntColumn get priority =>
      integer().withDefault(
        const Constant(0),
      )();

  BoolColumn get primaryReference =>
      boolean().withDefault(
        const Constant(false),
      )();

  @override
  Set<Column<Object>> get primaryKey => {
        id,
      };
}
```

```dart
class CreateCharacterModeService {
  Future<String> create({
    required String projectId,
    required String characterId,
    required String name,
    bool isDefault = false,
  }) async {
    final id = const Uuid().v4();

    // transaction:
    // insert mode
    // create override row
    // optionally ensure only one default

    return id;
  }
}
```

## Step 13.15 — Duplicate slot rules

Untuk slot single:

```text
wardrobe
primary_weapon
secondary_weapon
hair_variant
companion
vehicle
```

hanya boleh satu aktif per mode.

Untuk multi:

```text
accessory
```

boleh banyak.

Buat metadata:

```dart
enum LoadoutSlotMultiplicity {
  single,
  multiple,
}
```

---

## Step 13.16 — Character Loadout Repository

```dart
abstract interface class
    CharacterLoadoutRepository {
  Future<List<CharacterMode>>
      getModes(
    String characterId,
  );

  Future<List<LoadoutEntry>>
      getLoadout(
    String modeId,
  );

  Future<void> setSlot({
    required String modeId,
    required LoadoutSlot slot,
    required String assetId,
  });

  Future<void> removeSlot({
    required String modeId,
    required LoadoutSlot slot,
    required String assetId,
  });
}
```

---

## Step 13.17 — Relation synchronization

Sekarang pertanyaan penting:

Jika Combat Mode memakai Void Dual Dagger, apakah kita juga perlu:

```text
Qowner uses Void Dual Dagger
```

Jawabannya: **ya, tetapi relation global dan loadout tidak sama**.

Loadout:

```text
Combat Mode
→ primary weapon
→ Void Dual Dagger
```

Relation:

```text
Qowner
→ uses
→ Void Dual Dagger
```

Relation adalah semantic link.

Loadout adalah konfigurasi aktif.

Saat sebuah asset ditambahkan ke loadout, kita bisa memastikan relation global ada.

Tetapi menghapus dari satu mode jangan langsung menghapus relation jika mode lain masih memakai asset itu.

---

## Step 13.18 — Derived relations

Lebih baik sebagian relation bisa dihitung.

Contoh:

```text
Combat Mode → Void Dagger
Daily Mode → Void Dagger
```

Maka:

```text
Qowner uses Void Dagger
```

bisa dianggap derived relation.

Ke depan kita bedakan:

```text
Manual Relation
Derived Relation
```

Belum perlu dibuat penuh sekarang.

---

```text
→

```

```text
characterId
previousModeId
currentModeId
```

Listener nanti:

```text

Thumbnail refresh
```

Berikutnya paling tepat adalah **Sprint 14 — Environment + Location System**. Setelah itu Character Mode tidak hanya menentukan outfit dan weapon, tetapi scene bisa menentukan `Character + Mode + Environment + Time of Day + Weather + Lighting`, yang akan menjadi fondasi langsung untuk Story, Timeline, dan AI cinematic generation. memcite

lanjutkan



Lanjut ke **Sprint 14 — Environment + Location System**.

Sprint ini membuat dunia tempat semua Character, Story, dan Timeline berlangsung. Setelah sprint ini, kita tidak hanya punya “Qowner memakai wardrobe dan weapon tertentu”, tetapi juga bisa menentukan **di mana adegan terjadi, kapan waktunya, bagaimana cuaca, lighting, atmosfer, dan kondisi lingkungan**.

## Target Sprint 14

Struktur Environment:

```text
ENVIRONMENT
│
├── Location
├── Architecture
├── Interior / Exterior
├── Terrain
├── Materials
├── Props
├── Lighting
├── Weather
├── Time of Day
├── Atmosphere
├── Continuity
├── Physics Rules
└── References
```

```text

Night

```

### Step 14.1 — Environment model

Buat:

`features/environment/domain/environment_model.dart`

```dart
class EnvironmentModel {
  const EnvironmentModel({
    required this.assetId,
    required this.projectId,
    required this.name,
    this.environmentType = '',
    this.description = '',
    this.location = '',
    this.architecture = '',
    this.terrain = '',
    this.notes = '',
  });

  final String assetId;
  final String projectId;

  final String name;
  final String environmentType;

  final String description;
  final String location;
  final String architecture;
  final String terrain;

  final String notes;
}
```

### Step 14.2 — Environment table

```dart
class Environments extends Table {
  TextColumn get assetId => text()();

  TextColumn get projectId => text()();

  TextColumn get name => text()();

  TextColumn get environmentType =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant(''),
      )();

  TextColumn get description =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant(''),
      )();

  TextColumn get location =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant(''),
      )();

  TextColumn get architecture =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant(''),
      )();

  TextColumn get terrain =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant(''),
      )();

  TextColumn get notes =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant(''),
      )();

  @override
  Set<Column<Object>> get primaryKey => {
        assetId,
      };
}
```

Schema:

```dart
@override
int get schemaVersion => 7;
```

Migration:

```dart
if (from < 7) {
  await m.createTable(environments);
}
```

### Step 14.4 — Environment zones

```text
├── Plaza
└── Underground
```

```dart
class EnvironmentWeatherProfiles

      real().withDefault(
        const Constant(0.0),

      };
```

### Step 14.12 — Continuity system

Ini sangat penting untuk Story dan Timeline.

Contoh:

```text
Scene 01
Rooftop intact

Scene 02
Window broken

Scene 03
Road damaged

Scene 04
Building partially collapsed
```

Environment harus bisa menyimpan state.

Buat:

```dart
class EnvironmentContinuityStates
    extends Table {
  TextColumn get id => text()();

  TextColumn get environmentId => text()();

  TextColumn get name => text()();

  TextColumn get description =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant(''),
      )();

  DateTimeColumn get createdAt =>
      dateTime()();

  @override
  Set<Column<Object>> get primaryKey => {
        id,
      };
}
```

Nanti state ini dihubungkan dengan Scene/Timeline.

# Step 14.16 — Create Environment

Transaction:

```text
Generate UUID
↓
Create Asset
↓
Create Environment
↓
Create default Lighting Profile
↓
Create default Weather Profile
↓
Create Physics Profile
↓
Create directory
↓
Commit
```

Service concept:

```dart
class CreateEnvironmentService {
  Future<String> create({
    required String projectId,
    required String name,
    required EnvironmentType type,
  }) async {
    final id = const Uuid().v4();

    // transaction

    return id;
  }
}
```

# Step 14.18 — Environment Header

```dart
class EnvironmentHeader extends StatelessWidget {
  const EnvironmentHeader({
    super.key,
    required this.name,
    required this.type,
  });

  final String name;
  final String type;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      height: 58,
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
        horizontal: 18,
      ),
      decoration: const BoxDecoration(
        color: VoidColors.panelSecondary,
        border: Border(
          bottom: BorderSide(
            color: VoidColors.borderSoft,
          ),
        ),
      ),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          const Icon(
            Icons.landscape_outlined,
            color: VoidColors.primaryBright,
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 10),
          Column(
            mainAxisAlignment:
                MainAxisAlignment.center,
            crossAxisAlignment:
                CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              Text(
                name,
                style: VoidTypography.title,
              ),
              Text(
                type,
                style: VoidTypography.caption,
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const Spacer(),
          IconButton(
            onPressed: () {},
            icon: const Icon(
              Icons.history,
            ),
          ),
          IconButton(
            onPressed: () {},
            icon: const Icon(
              Icons.more_horiz,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

# Step 14.23 — Environment relations

Sekarang Relation Engine mendapat relation penting:

```text
Character
locatedIn
Environment
```

atau nantinya:

```text
Scene
takesPlaceIn
Environment
```

Tambahkan relation type:

```dart
takesPlaceIn
```

Jadi:

```text
Qowner ─locatedIn→ Neo Megacity

Scene 01 ─takesPlaceIn→ Rooftop
```

---

# Step 14.24 — Parent/child environment

Rooftop sebaiknya tidak selalu menjadi environment terpisah.

Kita bisa pilih dua pendekatan:

```text
Environment
Neo Megacity

Zone
Rooftop
```

atau:

```text
Environment
Neo Megacity

Child Environment
Rooftop District
```

Untuk sekarang gunakan **Zones** untuk subdivision ringan.

Environment terpisah hanya jika asset tersebut punya lifecycle, reference, lighting, atau continuity sendiri.

---

# Step 14.25 — Scene Environment Context

Kita belum punya Scene table, tetapi kita sudah bisa mendesain context:

```dart
class SceneEnvironmentContext {
  const SceneEnvironmentContext({
    required this.environmentId,
    this.zoneId,
    this.lightingProfileId,
    this.weatherProfileId,
    this.continuityStateId,
  });

  final String environmentId;
  final String? zoneId;

  final String? lightingProfileId;
  final String? weatherProfileId;
  final String? continuityStateId;
}
```

Sprint Story/Timeline nanti memakai model ini.

## Status proyek

```text
01 Foundation                     ✅
02 Design System                  ✅
03 Adaptive Shell                ✅
04 Authentication                ✅
05 Dashboard                     ✅
06 Project System                ✅
07 Persistence                   ✅
08 Asset + Character Core        ✅
09 Character Workspace           ✅
10 Selection + Inspector         ✅
11 Wardrobe System               ✅
12 Weapon System                 ✅
13 Character Mode + Loadout      ✅
14 Environment System            ◀ CURRENT
```

Berikutnya paling tepat adalah **Sprint 15 — Story Bible + Scene System**. Di situ kita mulai membuat `Story → Chapter → Scene`, menghubungkan Character + Mode + Wardrobe/Weapon Loadout + Environment + Weather + Lighting, serta membuat struktur scene yang nantinya langsung bisa digunakan Timeline dan AI Cinematic Generator. memcite

lanjutkan

Lanjut ke **Sprint 15 — Story Bible + Scene System**.

Ini adalah sprint yang mulai menyatukan hampir semua sistem sebelumnya menjadi struktur naratif. Setelah ini, Void Scriptor tidak hanya tahu siapa karakternya dan apa outfit/senjatanya, tetapi juga **adegan apa yang terjadi, siapa yang terlibat, mode apa yang dipakai, lokasi apa, cuaca apa, lighting apa, dan urutan ceritanya bagaimana**.

## Target Sprint 15

```text
STORY

├── Scene
```

### Step 15.1 — Story model

Buat:

`features/story/domain/story_model.dart`

```dart
class StoryModel {
  const StoryModel({
    required this.assetId,
    required this.projectId,
    required this.title,
    this.synopsis = '',
    this.genre = '',
    this.status = '',
    this.notes = '',
  });

  final String assetId;
  final String projectId;

  final String title;
  final String synopsis;

  final String genre;
  final String status;

  final String notes;
}
```

Story tetap merupakan Asset.

### Step 15.2 — Story table

```dart
class Stories extends Table {
  TextColumn get assetId => text()();
  TextColumn get projectId => text()();

  TextColumn get title => text()();

  TextColumn get synopsis =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant(''),
      )();

  TextColumn get genre =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant(''),
      )();

  TextColumn get status =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant('draft'),
      )();

  TextColumn get notes =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant(''),
      )();

  @override
  Set<Column<Object>> get primaryKey => {
        assetId,
      };
}
```

Naikkan schema:

```dart
@override
int get schemaVersion => 8;
```

Migration:

```dart
if (from < 8) {
  await m.createTable(stories);
}
```

## Step 15.5 — Scene

Ini bagian terpenting.

```dart
class StoryScenes extends Table {
  TextColumn get id => text()();

  TextColumn get storyId => text()();
  TextColumn get chapterId => text()();

  TextColumn get title => text()();

  TextColumn get summary =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant(''),
      )();

  TextColumn get sceneType =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant('standard'),
      )();

  IntColumn get sceneNumber =>
      integer()();

  IntColumn get sortOrder =>
      integer().withDefault(
        const Constant(0),
      )();

  RealColumn get estimatedDurationSeconds =>
      real().nullable()();

  DateTimeColumn get createdAt =>
      dateTime()();

  DateTimeColumn get updatedAt =>
      dateTime()();

  @override
  Set<Column<Object>> get primaryKey => {
        id,
      };
}
```

Scene sekarang memiliki identitas sendiri.

## Step 15.6 — Scene Environment Context

Kita gunakan fondasi Sprint 14.

```dart
class SceneEnvironmentContexts
    extends Table {
  TextColumn get sceneId => text()();

  TextColumn get environmentId => text()();

  TextColumn get zoneId =>
      text().nullable()();

  TextColumn get weatherProfileId =>
      text().nullable()();

  TextColumn get lightingProfileId =>
      text().nullable()();

  TextColumn get continuityStateId =>
      text().nullable()();

  TextColumn get timePreset =>
      text().nullable()();

  @override
  Set<Column<Object>> get primaryKey => {
        sceneId,
      };
}
```

Sekarang satu Scene bisa menunjuk environment secara eksplisit.

## Step 15.7 — Scene Characters

Jangan simpan character sebagai array JSON.

Buat table:

```dart
class SceneCharacters extends Table {
  TextColumn get id => text()();

  TextColumn get sceneId => text()();

  TextColumn get characterId => text()();

  TextColumn get modeId =>
      text().nullable()();

  TextColumn get role =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant('participant'),
      )();

  IntColumn get sortOrder =>
      integer().withDefault(
        const Constant(0),
      )();

  @override
  Set<Column<Object>> get primaryKey => {
        id,
      };
}
```

Contoh:

```text
Scene 01

Qowner
mode = Combat Mode
role = protagonist

Neo Lyra
mode = Chase Mode
role = antagonist
```

## Step 15.8 — Scene action beats

Satu Scene sebaiknya tidak hanya satu paragraf.

Kita pecah menjadi beats:

```text
Scene 01

Beat 1
Qowner descends.

Beat 2
Void Boots activate.

Beat 3
Shockwave hits street.

Beat 4
Glass shatters.

Beat 5
Dust fills frame.
```

Buat:

```dart
class SceneBeats extends Table {
  TextColumn get id => text()();

  TextColumn get sceneId => text()();

  TextColumn get beatType =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant('action'),
      )();

  TextColumn get content => text()();

  RealColumn get startSecond =>
      real().nullable()();

  RealColumn get durationSeconds =>
      real().nullable()();

  IntColumn get sortOrder =>
      integer().withDefault(
        const Constant(0),
      )();

  @override
  Set<Column<Object>> get primaryKey => {
        id,
      };
}
```

Type:

```text
action
dialogue
camera
vfx
audio
transition
note
```

## Step 15.9 — Dialogue

Dialogue bisa dipisahkan bila nanti lebih kompleks.

Untuk tahap awal, `SceneBeats` sudah cukup.

Contoh:

```text
beatType = dialogue
content = "..."
```

Nanti kalau kita butuh voice actor, emotion, subtitle, localization, baru kita buat `DialogueLines`.

## Step 15.10 — Camera notes

Scene cinematic butuh camera.

```text
Camera

Shot
Wide

Movement
Tracking

Lens
35mm

Angle
Low Angle

Focus
Qowner

Duration
2.5 sec
```

Untuk sekarang bisa disimpan sebagai Beat bertipe `camera`.

Tetapi kita siapkan domain:

```dart
class CameraShotModel {
  const CameraShotModel({
    required this.id,
    required this.sceneId,
    required this.shotType,
    this.movement = '',
    this.lens = '',
    this.angle = '',
    this.focus = '',
  });

  final String id;
  final String sceneId;
  final String shotType;

  final String movement;
  final String lens;
  final String angle;
  final String focus;
}
```

Kita implementasikan table lengkap saat Timeline masuk.

## Step 15.11 — Scene Continuity

Ini akan menghubungkan Story ke Environment continuity.

Contoh:

```text
Scene 01
Street = intact

After Scene 01
Street = damaged
```

Kita butuh:

```dart
class SceneContinuityChanges
    extends Table {
  TextColumn get id => text()();

  TextColumn get sceneId => text()();

  TextColumn get targetAssetId => text()();

  TextColumn get changeType => text()();

  TextColumn get beforeState =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant(''),
      )();

  TextColumn get afterState =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant(''),
      )();

  @override
  Set<Column<Object>> get primaryKey => {
        id,
      };
}
```

Target asset bisa:

```text
environment
weapon
wardrobe
character
prop
```

## Step 15.12 — Scene relation types

Tambahkan:

```dart
takesPlaceIn
featuresCharacter
usesMode
continuesFrom
causesStateChange
```

Tetapi kita tidak wajib menyimpan semua sebagai AssetRelation, karena beberapa memang lebih cocok di tabel Scene.

Prinsipnya:

```text
Relation Engine = semantic relation lintas aset
Scene tables = runtime/narrative composition
```

## Step 15.15 — Story Explorer

Explorer sekarang:

```text
Story
└── Rise of the Void
    ├── Kokuen Arc
    │   ├── Chapter 01
    │   │   ├── Scene 01
    │   │   └── Scene 02
    │
    └── Neo Megacity Arc
        └── Chapter 02
```

Scene dapat dipilih.

Selection Engine perlu berkembang sedikit.

Scene bukan Asset utama? Saya menyarankan **Scene tetap bukan Asset global**, tetapi menjadi selectable entity.

Jadi `SelectionModel` sebaiknya berkembang dari:

```text
asset only
```

menjadi:

```text
SelectionTarget
```

dengan type:

```dart
enum SelectionKind {
  asset,
  storyArc,
  chapter,
  scene,
  sceneBeat,
}
```

Ini lebih scalable.

## Step 15.17 — Scene header

```dart
class SceneHeader extends StatelessWidget {
  const SceneHeader({
    super.key,
    required this.title,
    required this.sceneNumber,
    required this.duration,
  });

  final String title;
  final int sceneNumber;
  final double? duration;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      height: 58,
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
        horizontal: 18,
      ),
      decoration: const BoxDecoration(
        color: VoidColors.panelSecondary,
        border: Border(
          bottom: BorderSide(
            color: VoidColors.borderSoft,
          ),
        ),
      ),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          const Icon(
            Icons.movie_outlined,
            color: VoidColors.primaryBright,
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 10),
          Expanded(
            child: Column(
              mainAxisAlignment:
                  MainAxisAlignment.center,
              crossAxisAlignment:
                  CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text(
                  'Scene $sceneNumber — $title',
                  style: VoidTypography.title,
                ),
                Text(
                  duration == null
                      ? 'Duration not set'
                      : '${duration!.toStringAsFixed(1)} sec',
                  style: VoidTypography.caption,
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          IconButton(
            onPressed: () {},
            icon: const Icon(
              Icons.play_arrow_outlined,
            ),
          ),
          IconButton(
            onPressed: () {},
            icon: const Icon(
              Icons.more_horiz,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

## Step 15.24 — Story autosave

Story editor akan sering mengetik.

Gunakan debounce dari Sprint 07:

```text

↓
idle ~1.5 sec
↓
autosave
```

Jangan write database setiap keypress.

## Step 15.25 — Scene duration validation

Kalau Scene ditetapkan:

```text
duration = 10 sec
```

dan Beats:

```text
2 + 2 + 3 + 5 = 12 sec
```

berarti ada masalah.

Tampilkan:

```text
Scene duration mismatch

Scene: 10 sec
Beats: 12 sec
```

Ini nanti sangat berguna untuk video generation.

## Step 15.27 — Scene context resolver

Buat konsep:

```dart
class ResolvedSceneContext {
  const ResolvedSceneContext({
    required this.sceneId,
    required this.characters,
    required this.environment,
    required this.beats,
  });

  final String sceneId;

  final List<ResolvedSceneCharacter> characters;
  final ResolvedEnvironmentContext environment;
  final List<SceneBeatModel> beats;
}
```

Resolver nantinya:

```text
Scene
↓
Character
↓
Mode
↓
Loadout
↓
Wardrobe
↓
Weapons
↓
Identity Locks
↓
Environment
↓
Weather
↓
Lighting
↓
Continuity
```

Ini adalah salah satu service paling penting yang akan kita buat.

## Step 15.28 — Scene continuity validation

Misalnya:

```text
Scene 01
Weapon destroyed

Scene 02
Weapon appears normal
```

Void Scriptor nanti bisa memberi warning:

```text
Continuity warning:
Void Sword was destroyed in Scene 01.
```

Belum implement full checker sekarang, tetapi schema continuity yang kita buat memungkinkan ini.

```text
01 Foundation                     ✅

```

Berikutnya paling tepat adalah **Sprint 16 — Timeline + Cinematic Scene Editor**. Di situ Scene yang sekarang bersifat struktural akan ditampilkan sebagai timeline visual dengan track Character, Camera, Action, Dialogue, VFX, Audio, Environment, dan Continuity. Itu juga akan menjadi fondasi untuk workflow video 10–15 detik per scene dan AI cinematic generation. memcite

lanjutkan

Lanjut ke **Sprint 16 — Timeline + Cinematic Scene Editor**.

Sprint ini mengubah Scene yang sebelumnya berupa struktur naratif menjadi **timeline visual** yang bisa dipakai untuk mengatur durasi, urutan beat, character action, camera, VFX, audio, environment, dan continuity.

Target utamanya:

```text
Story
  ↓
Chapter
  ↓
Scene
  ↓
Timeline
```

dan satu Scene 10–15 detik bisa diedit seperti mini video editor.

## Target Sprint 16

Struktur timeline:

```text
SCENE TIMELINE
│
├── Character Track
├── Camera Track
├── Action Track
├── Dialogue Track
├── VFX Track
├── Audio Track
├── Environment Track
└── Continuity Track
```

Contoh:

```text
0s        2s        4s        6s        8s       10s

Qowner
| Descend | Boots Active | Impact | Recover |

Camera
| Wide    | Tracking     | Shake  | Close   |

VFX
           | Void Glow    | Shockwave | Dust |

Audio
| Wind    | Charge       | Impact    | Debris |
```

# Step 16.1 — Timeline domain model

Buat:

`features/timeline/domain/timeline_model.dart`

```dart
class TimelineModel {
  const TimelineModel({
    required this.id,
    required this.projectId,
    required this.sceneId,
    required this.durationSeconds,
    this.frameRate = 30,
  });

  final String id;
  final String projectId;
  final String sceneId;

  final double durationSeconds;
  final int frameRate;
}
```
