# Step 16.2 — Timeline table

Tambahkan ke Drift:

```dart
class Timelines extends Table {
  TextColumn get id => text()();

  TextColumn get projectId => text()();

  TextColumn get sceneId => text()();

  RealColumn get durationSeconds => real()();

  IntColumn get frameRate =>
      integer().withDefault(
        const Constant(30),
      )();

  DateTimeColumn get createdAt =>
      dateTime()();

  DateTimeColumn get updatedAt =>
      dateTime()();

  @override
  Set<Column<Object>> get primaryKey => {
        id,
      };
}
```

Schema naik:

```dart
@override
int get schemaVersion => 9;
```

Migration:

```dart
if (from < 9) {
  await m.createTable(timelines);
}
```

# Step 16.5 — Timeline Clips

Setiap track punya clips:

```dart
class TimelineClips extends Table {
  TextColumn get id => text()();

  TextColumn get timelineId => text()();

  TextColumn get trackId => text()();

  TextColumn get clipType => text()();

  TextColumn get label => text()();

  RealColumn get startSeconds => real()();

  RealColumn get durationSeconds => real()();

  TextColumn get sourceId =>
      text().nullable()();

  TextColumn get payload =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant('{}'),
      )();

  IntColumn get sortOrder =>
      integer().withDefault(
        const Constant(0),
      )();

  @override
  Set<Column<Object>> get primaryKey => {
        id,
      };
}
```

`payload` bisa menyimpan metadata ringan dalam JSON, tapi jangan dipakai untuk semua data utama.

# Step 16.6 — Timeline clip model

```dart
class TimelineClipModel {
  const TimelineClipModel({
    required this.id,
    required this.trackId,
    required this.startSeconds,
    required this.durationSeconds,
    required this.label,
    this.sourceId,
  });

  final String id;
  final String trackId;

  final double startSeconds;
  final double durationSeconds;

  final String label;
  final String? sourceId;

  double get endSeconds =>
      startSeconds + durationSeconds;
}
```

# Step 16.7 — Generate Timeline from Scene Beats

Scene dari Sprint 15 sudah punya:

```text
SceneBeats
```

Maka Timeline pertama bisa dibuat otomatis:

```text
Scene Beat
↓
Timeline Clip
```

Contoh:

```text
ACTION
0–2 sec
Qowner descends
```

menjadi:

```text
Action Track
clip:
Qowner descends
start = 0
duration = 2
```

Jadi user tidak mulai dari timeline kosong.

# Step 16.10 — Timeline header

```dart
class TimelineHeader extends StatelessWidget {
  const TimelineHeader({
    super.key,
    required this.sceneTitle,
    required this.duration,
  });

  final String sceneTitle;
  final double duration;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Container(
      height: 52,
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
        horizontal: 16,
      ),
      decoration: const BoxDecoration(
        color: VoidColors.panelSecondary,
        border: Border(
          bottom: BorderSide(
            color: VoidColors.borderSoft,
          ),
        ),
      ),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          const Icon(
            Icons.timeline_outlined,
            color: VoidColors.primaryBright,
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 10),
          Expanded(
            child: Text(
              sceneTitle,
              style: VoidTypography.title,
            ),
          ),
          Text(
            '${duration.toStringAsFixed(1)} sec',
            style: VoidTypography.caption,
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 12),
          IconButton(
            tooltip: 'Play',
            onPressed: () {},
            icon: const Icon(
              Icons.play_arrow,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

---

# Step 16.11 — Time ruler

Buat reusable:

```text
TimelineRuler
```

Props:

```text
duration
zoom
pixelsPerSecond
currentTime
```

Contoh:

```dart
class TimelineScale {
  const TimelineScale({
    required this.pixelsPerSecond,
  });

  final double pixelsPerSecond;

  double secondsToPixels(
    double seconds,
  ) {
    return seconds * pixelsPerSecond;
  }

  double pixelsToSeconds(
    double pixels,
  ) {
    return pixels / pixelsPerSecond;
  }
}
```

Ini penting untuk drag dan resize clip.

# Step 16.14 — Playback controller

Prototype:

```dart
class TimelinePlaybackController
    extends Notifier<bool> {
  @override
  bool build() => false;

  void play() {
    state = true;
  }

  void pause() {
    state = false;
  }

  void stop() {
    state = false;
  }
}
```

Nanti ditambah ticker untuk playhead.

---

# Step 16.15 — Clip widget

```dart
class TimelineClip extends StatelessWidget {
  const TimelineClip({
    super.key,
    required this.label,
    required this.width,
    required this.selected,
    required this.onTap,
  });

  final String label;
  final double width;
  final bool selected;
  final VoidCallback onTap;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return InkWell(
      onTap: onTap,
      child: Container(
        width: width,
        height: 28,
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
          horizontal: 8,
        ),
        alignment: Alignment.centerLeft,
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: selected
              ? VoidColors.primary
                  .withValues(alpha: 0.25)
              : VoidColors.surfaceElevated,
          borderRadius:
              BorderRadius.circular(5),
          border: Border.all(
            color: selected
                ? VoidColors.primary
                : VoidColors.border,
          ),
        ),
        child: Text(
          label,
          maxLines: 1,
          overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
          style: VoidTypography.caption,
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

# Step 16.17 — Drag clip

User harus bisa:

```text
drag horizontally
```

untuk mengubah start time.

Flow:

```text
drag
↓
pixel delta
↓
seconds delta
↓
snap
↓
preview
↓
drop
↓
database save
```

Jangan write DB setiap pointer move.

Update local state saat drag dan commit ketika drop selesai.

---

# Step 16.18 — Resize clip

Clip punya handle kiri/kanan:

```text
|◀ █████████████ ▶|
```

Resize kanan:

```text
duration changes
```

Resize kiri:

```text
start + duration change
```

Tetapi:

```text
duration >= minimum
```

misalnya:

```text
0.1 sec
```

---

# Step 16.19 — Snap system

Buat:

```dart
enum TimelineSnapMode {
  none,
  frame,
  tenthSecond,
  quarterSecond,
  halfSecond,
  second,
}
```

Untuk video AI 10 detik, `0.1 sec` atau `frame` akan berguna.

---

# Step 16.20 — Frame support

Jika:

```text
30 FPS
```

maka:

```text
1 frame = 1 / 30 sec
```

Helper:

```dart
double frameToSeconds(
  int frame,
  int fps,
) {
  return frame / fps;
}

int secondsToFrame(
  double seconds,
  int fps,
) {
  return (seconds * fps).round();
}
```

# Step 16.27 — Continuity Track

```text

Road Damaged

Dust Begins
```

Clip/event bisa sangat pendek.

Ini nantinya dipakai untuk continuity checker Scene berikutnya.

---

# Step 16.28 — Timeline ↔ Scene Beat synchronization

Kita harus menentukan sumber kebenaran.

Saya sarankan:

```text
Scene Beat = narrative structure
Timeline Clip = production timing
```

Keduanya berhubungan melalui ID.

Contoh:

```text
SceneBeat ID
beat-001

TimelineClip
sourceId = beat-001
```

Kalau text Beat berubah:

```text
clip label update
```

Kalau timing berubah:

```text
SceneBeat start/duration update
```

Gunakan synchronization service, bukan manual di UI.

---

# Step 16.29 — Timeline validation

Validator:

```text
Scene duration
10.0 sec
```

Warning jika:

```text
clip ends > 10 sec
```

atau:

```text
negative start
```

atau single track clips yang tidak boleh overlap.

Example:

```text
Timeline Validation

⚠ Camera clip exceeds scene duration
⚠ Two environment states overlap
✓ Character clips valid
✓ Audio clips valid
```

## Status Void Scriptor

```text
01 Foundation                     ✅
02 Design System                  ✅
03 Adaptive Shell                ✅
04 Authentication               ✅
05 Dashboard                    ✅
06 Project System               ✅
07 Persistence                  ✅
08 Asset + Character Core       ✅
09 Character Workspace          ✅
10 Selection + Inspector        ✅
11 Wardrobe                     ✅
12 Weapon                       ✅
13 Character Mode + Loadout     ✅
14 Environment                  ✅
15 Story + Scene                ✅
16 Timeline + Cinematic Editor  ◀ CURRENT
```

Berikutnya paling tepat adalah **Sprint 17 — AI Context Engine + Prompt Composer**. Di situ seluruh data yang sekarang sudah terstruktur—Character Identity, Mode, Wardrobe, Weapon, Environment, Scene, Camera, VFX, dan Timeline—akan dirakit otomatis menjadi konteks/prompt yang konsisten untuk text, image, dan video AI.

lanjutkan

Lanjut ke **Sprint 17 — AI Context Engine + Prompt Composer**.

Ini sprint yang mulai menghubungkan semua data terstruktur yang sudah kita bangun ke workflow AI. Jadi user tidak perlu lagi merakit prompt panjang secara manual; Void Scriptor akan mengambil Character Identity, Mode, Wardrobe, Weapon, Environment, Scene, Camera, VFX, Timeline, dan Preservation Locks lalu menyusunnya menjadi konteks AI yang konsisten.

## Target Sprint 17

Flow utama:

```text
PROJECT DATA
   │
   ├── Character
   ├── Character Mode
   ├── Wardrobe
   ├── Weapon
   ├── Environment
   ├── Story Scene
   ├── Camera
   ├── VFX
   └── Timeline
        │
        ▼
AI CONTEXT RESOLVER
        │
        ▼
PROMPT COMPOSER
        │
        ├── Image Prompt
        ├── Video Prompt
        ├── Story Prompt
        └── Consistency Prompt
```

### Step 17.1 — AI context domain

Buat:

`features/ai/domain/ai_context.dart`

```dart
class AiContext {
  const AiContext({
    required this.projectId,
    required this.characterContexts,
    required this.environmentContext,
    required this.sceneContext,
    required this.timelineContext,
  });

  final String projectId;

  final List<AiCharacterContext> characterContexts;
  final AiEnvironmentContext? environmentContext;
  final AiSceneContext? sceneContext;
  final AiTimelineContext? timelineContext;
}
```

Lalu:

```dart
class AiCharacterContext {

    required this.characterId,
    required this.name,
    this.alias,

    this.preservationRules = const [],
  });

  final String name;
```

### Step 17.7 — Prompt document model

```dart
class PromptDocument {
  const PromptDocument({
    required this.id,
    required this.projectId,
    required this.target,
    required this.title,
    required this.sections,
    required this.createdAt,
    this.negativePrompt = '',
  });

  final String id;
  final String projectId;

  final PromptTarget target;

  final String title;
  final List<PromptSection> sections;

  final String negativePrompt;

  final DateTime createdAt;
}
```

```dart
    required this.title,
    required this.content,
    required this.order,
    this.enabled = true,
  });

  final String title;
```

### Step 17.8 — Prompt Composer

Buat:

`features/ai/application/prompt_composer.dart`

```dart
class PromptComposer {
  PromptDocument compose({
    required AiContext context,
    required PromptTarget target,
  }) {
    final sections = <PromptSection>[];

    // project / scene
    // character
    // mode
    // wardrobe
    // weapon
    // environment
    // camera
    // timeline
    // preservation locks
    // quality / technical constraints

    return PromptDocument(
      id: const Uuid().v4(),
      projectId: context.projectId,
      target: target,
      title: 'Generated Prompt',
      sections: sections,
      createdAt: DateTime.now(),
    );
  }
}
```

### Step 17.10 — Preservation prompt resolver

Buat service kecil:

```dart
class PreservationPromptResolver {
  List<String> resolve(
    EffectiveIdentityProfile profile,
  ) {
    final result = <String>[];

    if (profile.faceLock) {
      result.add(
        'Preserve exact facial identity and facial proportions.',
      );
    }

    if (profile.hairLock) {
      result.add(
        'Preserve hairstyle geometry, length and strand direction.',
      );
    }

    if (profile.bodyLock) {
      result.add(
        'Preserve body proportions and topology.',
      );
    }

    if (profile.colorLock) {
      result.add(
        'Preserve established character color palette.',
      );
    }

    if (profile.outfitLock) {
      result.add(
        'Preserve wardrobe geometry and construction.',
      );
    }

    if (profile.weaponLock) {
      result.add(
        'Preserve weapon geometry and proportions.',
      );
    }

    return result;
  }
}
```

Nanti wording bisa dibuat template/configurable.

### Step 17.12 — Prompt templates table

Tambahkan Drift:

```dart
class PromptTemplates extends Table {
  TextColumn get id => text()();

  TextColumn get projectId =>
      text().nullable()();

  TextColumn get name => text()();

  TextColumn get target => text()();

  TextColumn get content => text()();

  BoolColumn get builtIn =>
      boolean().withDefault(
        const Constant(false),
      )();

  DateTimeColumn get createdAt =>
      dateTime()();

  DateTimeColumn get updatedAt =>
      dateTime()();

  @override
  Set<Column<Object>> get primaryKey => {
        id,
      };
}
```

Built-in preset bisa hidup di app. Custom preset bisa project-specific.

### Step 17.13 — Prompt history

```dart
class AiPromptHistory extends Table {
  TextColumn get id => text()();

  TextColumn get projectId => text()();

  TextColumn get sceneId =>
      text().nullable()();

  TextColumn get assetId =>
      text().nullable()();

  TextColumn get target => text()();

  TextColumn get prompt => text()();

  TextColumn get negativePrompt =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant(''),
      )();

  TextColumn get provider =>
      text().nullable()();

  TextColumn get model =>
      text().nullable()();

  DateTimeColumn get createdAt =>
      dateTime()();

  @override
  Set<Column<Object>> get primaryKey => {
        id,
      };
}
```

Ini sangat penting agar user bisa mengulang atau membandingkan generation.

### Step 17.16 — Context configuration

```dart
class AiContextOptions {
  const AiContextOptions({
    this.includeCharacter = true,
    this.includeMode = true,
    this.includeIdentity = true,
    this.includeWardrobe = true,
    this.includeWeapons = true,
    this.includeEnvironment = true,
    this.includeTimeline = true,
    this.includeContinuity = true,
  });

  final bool includeCharacter;
  final bool includeMode;
  final bool includeIdentity;

  final bool includeWardrobe;
  final bool includeWeapons;

  final bool includeEnvironment;
  final bool includeTimeline;
  final bool includeContinuity;
}
```

### Step 17.22 — Provider registry

### Step 17.25 — Prompt compile stage

Pisahkan:

```text
Context Resolve
↓
Prompt Compose
↓
Prompt Compile
↓
Provider Request
```

`Compile` mengubah PromptDocument menjadi string final.

```dart
class PromptCompiler {
  String compile(
    PromptDocument document,
  ) {
    final buffer = StringBuffer();

    final sections = [...document.sections]
      ..sort(
        (a, b) => a.order.compareTo(b.order),
      );

    for (final section in sections) {
      if (!section.enabled) continue;

      buffer
        ..writeln(section.title.toUpperCase())
        ..writeln()
        ..writeln(section.content)
        ..writeln();
    }

    return buffer.toString().trim();
  }
}
```

### Step 17.26 — Token/length awareness

Kita belum menghitung token provider secara akurat, tetapi kita bisa mulai menampilkan:


dan warning:

```text
Prompt is very large.
Consider disabling optional context.
```

Nanti provider adapter bisa memiliki token estimator.

lanjutkan

```
{"system1_search_query":[{"q":"site:platform.openai.com/docs OpenAI API image generation official docs Responses API"},{"q":"site:ai.google.dev Gemini API image generation official docs"},{"q":"site:flutter.dev secure storage plugin flutter official docs credentials"},{"q":"site:riverpod.dev AsyncNotifier official docs"}],"response_length":"short"}
```

The output of this plugin was redacted.

The output of this plugin was redacted.



Lanjut ke **Sprint 18 — AI Provider Integration + Generation Queue**.

Mulai sprint ini, AI Studio tidak hanya menyusun prompt. Kita mulai membuat **provider adapter nyata, secure credentials, generation jobs, queue, retry/cancel, dan hasil generation**. Karena API dan model provider berubah cepat, struktur ini sengaja dibuat abstrak supaya OpenAI, Gemini, Ollama, ComfyUI, dan provider lain bisa diganti tanpa membongkar UI. OpenAI memang menyediakan API untuk image generation, sementara Gemini saat ini mendukung image generation/editing melalui Gemini API dan model media khusus; jadi adapter capability-based adalah pilihan yang tepat. citeturn414052search0turn414052search1turn414052search27

### Step 18.1 — Jangan simpan API key di source code

Ini wajib.

Jangan pernah:

```dart
const apiKey = 'sk-xxxxx';
```

dan jangan taruh secret di:

```text
project.json
project.db
SharedPreferences
Git repository
```

Flutter sendiri memperingatkan bahwa menyimpan secret di aplikasi adalah praktik keamanan yang buruk dan obfuscation bukan perlindungan secret. citeturn414052search18

Untuk desktop/mobile kita bisa memakai secure credential abstraction. Package `flutter_secure_storage`, misalnya, menyediakan secure key-value storage lintas Android, iOS, macOS, Windows, dan Linux. citeturn414052search13

Tambahkan:

```bash
flutter pub add flutter_secure_storage
flutter pub add http
```

---

## Step 18.2 — Credential store abstraction

Buat:

`core/security/credential_store.dart`

```dart
abstract interface class CredentialStore {
  Future<void> write({
    required String key,
    required String value,
  });

  Future<String?> read(
    String key,
  );

  Future<void> delete(
    String key,
  );
}
```

Implementasi:

```dart
import 'package:flutter_secure_storage/flutter_secure_storage.dart';

class SecureCredentialStore
    implements CredentialStore {
  const SecureCredentialStore(
    this.storage,
  );

  final FlutterSecureStorage storage;

  @override
  Future<void> write({
    required String key,
    required String value,
  }) {
    return storage.write(
      key: key,
      value: value,
    );
  }

  @override
  Future<String?> read(
    String key,
  ) {
    return storage.read(
      key: key,
    );
  }

  @override
  Future<void> delete(
    String key,
  ) {
    return storage.delete(
      key: key,
    );
  }
}
```

Provider:

```dart
final credentialStoreProvider =
    Provider<CredentialStore>(
  (ref) {
    return const SecureCredentialStore(
      FlutterSecureStorage(),
    );
  },
);
```

# Step 18.8 — OpenAI adapter

Jangan panggil OpenAI langsung dari widget.

Struktur:

```text
services/
└── ai/
    └── providers/
        └── openai/
            ├── openai_provider.dart
            ├── openai_client.dart
            └── openai_mapper.dart
```

OpenAI API saat ini menyediakan image generation/editing endpoints/capabilities, jadi adapter OpenAI bisa memetakan `PromptTarget.image` ke flow image generation dan text ke flow text generation. citeturn414052search0turn414052search7

Skeleton:

```dart
class OpenAiProvider implements AiProvider {
  OpenAiProvider({
    required this.credentialStore,
    required this.client,
  });

  final CredentialStore credentialStore;
  final OpenAiClient client;

  @override
  String get id => 'openai';

  @override
  String get displayName => 'OpenAI';

  @override
  Set<AiCapability> get capabilities => {
        AiCapability.textGeneration,
        AiCapability.imageGeneration,
        AiCapability.imageEditing,
        AiCapability.visionInput,
        AiCapability.referenceImages,
      };

  @override
  Future<List<AiModelInfo>> listModels() {
    return client.listModels();
  }

  @override
  Future<AiGenerationResult> generate(
    AiGenerationRequest request,
  ) async {
    final key = await credentialStore.read(
      'ai.openai.api_key',
    );

    if (key == null || key.isEmpty) {
      throw StateError(
        'OpenAI credentials are not configured.',
      );
    }

    return client.generate(
      apiKey: key,
      request: request,
    );
  }

  @override
  Future<void> cancel(
    String requestId,
  ) async {}
}
```

---

# Step 18.9 — Gemini adapter

Google Gemini API saat ini mendukung generation/editing gambar dan juga menyediakan Gen Media APIs untuk media seperti image/video. Jadi Gemini provider juga cocok dengan abstraction capability-based kita. citeturn414052search1turn414052search26turn414052search27

Skeleton:

```dart
class GeminiProvider implements AiProvider {
  GeminiProvider({
    required this.credentialStore,
    required this.client,
  });

  final CredentialStore credentialStore;
  final GeminiClient client;

  @override
  String get id => 'gemini';

  @override
  String get displayName => 'Gemini';

  @override
  Set<AiCapability> get capabilities => {
        AiCapability.textGeneration,
        AiCapability.imageGeneration,
        AiCapability.imageEditing,
        AiCapability.visionInput,
        AiCapability.referenceImages,
      };

  @override
  Future<List<AiModelInfo>> listModels() {
    return client.listModels();
  }

  @override
  Future<AiGenerationResult> generate(
    AiGenerationRequest request,
  ) async {
    final key = await credentialStore.read(
      'ai.gemini.api_key',
    );

    if (key == null || key.isEmpty) {
      throw StateError(
        'Gemini credentials are not configured.',
      );
    }

    return client.generate(
      apiKey: key,
      request: request,
    );
  }

  @override
  Future<void> cancel(
    String requestId,
  ) async {}
}
```

Gemini API memerlukan authentication key untuk requests, sehingga key tersebut sebaiknya tetap berada di secure credential store, bukan project data. citeturn414052search28

# Step 18.12 — Provider Registry

Perbaiki registry:

```dart
class AiProviderRegistry {
  AiProviderRegistry(
    Iterable<AiProvider> providers,
  ) : _providers = {
          for (final provider in providers)
            provider.id: provider,
        };

  final Map<String, AiProvider> _providers;

  AiProvider require(
    String id,
  ) {
    final provider = _providers[id];

    if (provider == null) {
      throw StateError(
        'AI provider "$id" is not registered.',
      );
    }

    return provider;
  }

  List<AiProvider> get all =>
      List.unmodifiable(
        _providers.values,
      );
}
```

# Step 18.14 — Generation jobs table

```dart
class AiGenerationJobs extends Table {
  TextColumn get id => text()();

  TextColumn get projectId => text()();

  TextColumn get providerId => text()();

  TextColumn get modelId =>
      text().nullable()();

  TextColumn get promptHistoryId =>
      text()();

  TextColumn get status => text()();

  RealColumn get progress =>
      real().withDefault(
        const Constant(0),
      )();

  TextColumn get error =>
      text().nullable()();

  DateTimeColumn get createdAt =>
      dateTime()();

  DateTimeColumn get startedAt =>
      dateTime().nullable()();

  DateTimeColumn get completedAt =>
      dateTime().nullable()();

  @override
  Set<Column<Object>> get primaryKey => {
        id,
      };
}
```

---

# Step 18.15 — Job outputs

Satu job bisa menghasilkan beberapa file.

```dart
class AiGenerationOutputs extends Table {
  TextColumn get id => text()();

  TextColumn get jobId => text()();

  TextColumn get outputType => text()();

  TextColumn get localPath =>
      text().nullable()();

  TextColumn get remoteUrl =>
      text().nullable()();

  TextColumn get mimeType =>
      text().nullable()();

  IntColumn get sortOrder =>
      integer().withDefault(
        const Constant(0),
      )();

  @override
  Set<Column<Object>> get primaryKey => {
        id,
      };
}
```

---

# Step 18.16 — Generation Queue

Kita jangan menjalankan semua request sekaligus.

Buat:

```dart
class GenerationQueue {
  GenerationQueue({
    this.maxConcurrentJobs = 2,
  });

  final int maxConcurrentJobs;

  final List<GenerationJob> _pending = [];
  final Set<String> _running = {};

  void enqueue(
    GenerationJob job,
  ) {
    _pending.add(job);
  }
}
```

Lalu queue controller menentukan kapan job boleh jalan.

---

# Step 18.17 — Concurrency

Default:

```text
Cloud
2 concurrent jobs

Local GPU
1 job
```

Tetapi provider bisa menentukan sendiri.

Contoh:

```dart
class AiProviderLimits {
  const AiProviderLimits({
    required this.maxConcurrentJobs,
  });

  final int maxConcurrentJobs;
}
```

Jangan menghantam provider dengan banyak request paralel.

---

# Step 18.18 — Queue Controller

Gunakan Riverpod `AsyncNotifier` karena state asynchronous dan berubah karena interaksi user; ini sesuai pola Riverpod untuk state async/user interaction. citeturn414052search6turn414052search14

```dart
class GenerationQueueController
    extends AsyncNotifier<List<GenerationJob>> {
  @override
  Future<List<GenerationJob>> build() async {
    return repository.getJobs();
  }

  Future<void> enqueue({
    required String providerId,
    required String promptHistoryId,
    String? modelId,
  }) async {
    // create job
    // persist
    // refresh state
    // start scheduler
  }

  Future<void> retry(
    String jobId,
  ) async {}

  Future<void> cancel(
    String jobId,
  ) async {}
}
```

---

# Step 18.19 — Job Executor

Pisahkan queue dengan executor.

```dart
class GenerationJobExecutor {
  GenerationJobExecutor({
    required this.registry,
    required this.repository,
    required this.outputService,
  });

  final AiProviderRegistry registry;
  final GenerationRepository repository;
  final AiOutputService outputService;

  Future<void> execute(
    GenerationJob job,
  ) async {
    final provider = registry.require(
      job.providerId,
    );

    // load prompt
    // compile request
    // provider.generate
    // save output
    // update status
  }
}
```

# Step 18.20 — Output directory

Simpan hasil per project:

```text
assets/
└── ai/
    ├── generations/
    │   └── <job-uuid>/
    │       ├── output_01.png
    │       ├── output_02.png
    │       └── metadata.json
    │
    └── temporary/
```

Untuk scene:

```text
stories/
<story-id>/
scene_art/
```

boleh dibuat relation/link, tetapi file fisik sebaiknya tetap punya canonical AI generation directory.

---

# Step 18.21 — Jangan hanya menyimpan remote URL

Remote URL bisa expired.

Flow:

```text
provider output
↓
download immediately
↓
validate
↓
write local file
↓
save localPath
```

Flutter mendukung file persistence lintas mobile/desktop dengan `path_provider` + `dart:io`. citeturn414052search11

---

# Step 18.22 — Output metadata

Buat:

```json
{
  "jobId": "...",
  "provider": "gemini",
  "model": "...",
  "createdAt": "...",
  "promptHistoryId": "...",
  "references": [],
  "parameters": {}
}
```

Jangan simpan API key.

---

# Step 18.23 — Retry policy

Request bisa gagal karena:

```text
network
rate limit
timeout
provider error
invalid request
```

Kita bedakan error:

```dart
enum AiErrorType {
  authentication,
  rateLimit,
  network,
  timeout,
  unsupportedCapability,
  invalidRequest,
  provider,
  cancelled,
  unknown,
}
```

---

# Step 18.24 — Automatic retry

Jangan retry semua error.

Boleh retry:

```text
network
timeout
some 5xx
rate limit dengan backoff
```

Jangan retry otomatis:

```text
invalid API key
unsupported model
invalid request
```

Policy:

```dart
class RetryPolicy {
  const RetryPolicy({
    this.maxAttempts = 3,
  });

  final int maxAttempts;
}
```

---

# Step 18.25 — Exponential backoff

Konsep:

```text
attempt 1
2 sec

attempt 2
4 sec

attempt 3
8 sec
```

dengan jitter.

Jangan spin retry loop.

---

# Step 18.26 — Cancel

Cancellation tergantung provider.

Ada provider yang bisa cancel remote operation, ada yang tidak.

Jadi:

```text
cancel requested
↓
provider supports cancel?
├─ yes → remote cancel
└─ no  → mark local cancelled and ignore output
```

Capability tambahan bisa:

```dart
AiCapability.remoteCancellation
```

# Step 18.28 — Queue card

```dart
class GenerationJobCard
    extends StatelessWidget {
  const GenerationJobCard({
    super.key,
    required this.job,
  });

  final GenerationJob job;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return VoidCard(
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment:
            CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            children: [
              Expanded(
                child: Text(
                  job.id,
                  style:
                      VoidTypography.title,
                ),
              ),
              Text(
                job.status.name,
                style:
                    VoidTypography.caption,
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 12),
          LinearProgressIndicator(
            value:
                job.status ==
                        GenerationJobStatus.running
                    ? job.progress
                    : null,
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

# Step 18.31 — Never display secret after save

Setelah key tersimpan, lebih baik UI menunjukkan:

```text
Configured
••••••••••••1234
```

atau hanya:

```text
Credential configured
```

Jangan load dan tampilkan full key kecuali user benar-benar meminta reveal.

# Step 18.32 — Provider health

```dart
enum ProviderHealth {
  unknown,
  checking,
  online,
  unauthorized,
  offline,
  error,
}
```

Test connection tidak otomatis dilakukan terus-menerus.

# Step 18.33 — Model browser

Jangan hard-code model list selamanya.

Provider boleh:

```text
listModels()
```

Lalu cache:

```text
Provider
Model ID
Capabilities
Last Refreshed
```

Karena model provider dapat berubah. OpenAI dan Gemini sama-sama memiliki evolving model/API catalogs, jadi model availability harus dianggap dinamis. citeturn414052search7turn414052search15

# Step 18.34 — Capability validation

Sebelum enqueue:

```text
Prompt target = video
Provider = OpenAI adapter
```

check:

```dart
if (!provider.capabilities.contains(
  AiCapability.videoGeneration,
)) {
  throw UnsupportedError(
    'Selected provider does not support video generation.',
  );
}
```

UI juga disable provider yang tidak cocok.

```text
Gemini

[dynamic]
```

`Generate Now` tetap membuat Job; bedanya job mendapat priority tinggi.

Jangan bypass queue.

# Step 18.36 — Job priority

```dart
enum GenerationPriority {
  low,
  normal,
  high,
}
```

Queue:

```text
Generate Now → high
manual queue → normal
background batch → low
```

# Step 18.37 — Batch generation

Fondasi queue memungkinkan:

```text
Generate 4 Variations
```

dibuat menjadi:

```text
Job A
Job B
Job C
Job D
```

bukan satu job misterius.

Setiap output punya history sendiri.

# Step 18.38 — Reference bundle

Saat job dibuat, snapshot daftar references:

```text
Face
Hair
Wardrobe
Weapon
Environment
```

Jangan hanya query current references saat request nanti dieksekusi, karena asset bisa berubah ketika job masih antre.

Lebih aman simpan:

```text
Generation Input Snapshot
```

```dart
class AiGenerationInputs extends Table {

      text().nullable()();

  TextColumn get filePath =>
      text().nullable()();

      text().nullable()();

      integer().withDefault(
        const Constant(0),

        id,
      };
```

Dengan begitu generation reproducible.

# Step 18.44 — Delete semantics

Delete generation record:

```text
Delete metadata only?
Delete local files too?
```

```text
This output is used by Qowner as a primary face reference.
```

Jangan menghapus file diam-diam.

```text
Unavailable — Offline
```

Jangan membuat seluruh Studio gagal hanya karena tidak ada internet.

# Step 18.47 — Test flow

Test tanpa API dulu menggunakan:

```text
MockAiProvider
```

Mock provider:

```dart
class MockAiProvider implements AiProvider {
  @override
  String get id => 'mock';

  @override
  String get displayName =>
      'Mock Provider';

  @override
  Set<AiCapability> get capabilities => {
        AiCapability.textGeneration,
        AiCapability.imageGeneration,
      };

  @override
  Future<List<AiModelInfo>> listModels() async {
    return const [
      AiModelInfo(
        id: 'mock-image',
        displayName: 'Mock Image',
        capabilities: {
          AiCapability.imageGeneration,
        },
      ),
    ];
  }

  @override
  Future<AiGenerationResult> generate(
    AiGenerationRequest request,
  ) async {
    await Future<void>.delayed(
      const Duration(seconds: 2),
    );

    return const AiGenerationResult(
      providerRequestId: 'mock-request',
      outputs: [
        AiGeneratedOutput(
          type: AiOutputType.text,
          text: 'Mock generation complete.',
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  @override
  Future<void> cancel(
    String requestId,
  ) async {}
}
```

Ini memungkinkan kita menguji Queue sebelum menghabiskan API credit.

Expected:

## Status proyek

```text
01 Foundation                       ✅
02 Design System                    ✅
03 Adaptive Shell                   ✅
04 Authentication                  ✅
05 Dashboard                       ✅
06 Project System                  ✅
07 Persistence                     ✅
08 Asset + Character Core          ✅
09 Character Workspace             ✅
10 Selection + Inspector           ✅
11 Wardrobe                        ✅
12 Weapon                          ✅
13 Character Mode + Loadout        ✅
14 Environment                     ✅
15 Story + Scene                   ✅
16 Timeline + Cinematic Editor     ✅
17 AI Context + Prompt Composer    ✅
18 AI Providers + Generation Queue ◀ CURRENT
```

Setelah Sprint 18 stabil, saya sarankan **Sprint 19 — Asset Browser + Global Search + Tagging System**. Sampai sekarang kita sudah punya banyak asset dan relation; Sprint 19 akan membuat semuanya mudah ditemukan lewat search seperti `type:weapon tag:void`, collections, favorites, filters, grid/list browser, recent assets, dan global command/search bar.

lanjutkan









```
search("lanjutkan")
```

The output of this plugin was redacted.

Lanjut ke **Sprint 19 — Asset Browser + Global Search + Tagging System**.

Sekarang database kita sudah mulai besar: Character, Wardrobe, Weapon, Environment, Story, Scene, Timeline, AI Generation, dan Reference. Sprint ini membuat semuanya **mudah ditemukan dan dinavigasi**, bukan hanya lewat sidebar.

Untuk desktop, kita juga akan menyiapkan keyboard-first workflow. Flutter memang menyediakan `Actions` + `Shortcuts` untuk accelerator keyboard, focus system untuk navigasi keyboard, dan komponen seperti `SearchBar`/`SearchAnchor`; ini cocok untuk aplikasi desktop produktivitas seperti yang kita bangun. citeturn0search2turn0search3turn0search7

## Step 19.1 — Perluas Asset Core

Asset kita sekarang perlu metadata universal.

```dart
class Assets extends Table {
  TextColumn get id => text()();

  TextColumn get projectId => text()();

  TextColumn get type => text()();

  TextColumn get name => text()();

  TextColumn get description =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant(''),
      )();

  TextColumn get thumbnailPath =>
      text().nullable()();

  BoolColumn get favorite =>
      boolean().withDefault(
        const Constant(false),
      )();

  DateTimeColumn get createdAt =>
      dateTime()();

  DateTimeColumn get updatedAt =>
      dateTime()();

  DateTimeColumn get lastOpenedAt =>
      dateTime().nullable()();

  @override
  Set<Column<Object>> get primaryKey => {id};
}
```

Jangan menduplikasi data Character/Weapon/etc ke sini.

`Assets` hanya menyimpan metadata universal.

# Step 19.4 — Asset Card

```dart
class AssetCard extends StatelessWidget {
  const AssetCard({
    super.key,
    required this.asset,
    required this.selected,
    required this.onPressed,
  });

  final AssetModel asset;
  final bool selected;
  final VoidCallback onPressed;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return InkWell(
      onTap: onPressed,
      child: Container(
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: VoidColors.surface,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
          border: Border.all(
            color: selected
                ? VoidColors.primary
                : VoidColors.borderSoft,
          ),
        ),
        child: Column(
          children: [
            Expanded(
              child: AssetThumbnail(
                asset: asset,
              ),
            ),
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
              child: Row(
                children: [
                  Expanded(
                    child: Text(
                      asset.name,
                      maxLines: 1,
                      overflow:
                          TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                    ),
                  ),
                  if (asset.favorite)
                    const Icon(
                      Icons.star,
                      size: 16,
                    ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
```

# Step 19.6 — Tags

Kita jangan menyimpan:

```text
"void,combat,purple"
```

sebagai satu string.

Buat normalized tables.

```dart
class Tags extends Table {
  TextColumn get id => text()();

  TextColumn get projectId => text()();

  TextColumn get name => text()();

  @override
  Set<Column<Object>> get primaryKey => {
        id,
      };
}
```

Mapping:

```dart
class AssetTags extends Table {
  TextColumn get assetId => text()();

  TextColumn get tagId => text()();

  @override
  Set<Column<Object>> get primaryKey => {
        assetId,
        tagId,
      };
}
```

# Step 19.8 — Tag Editor

Inspector:

```text
TAGS

[void ×]
[combat ×]
[main-character ×]

[ + Add Tag ]
```

Autocomplete dari tag existing.

Jangan membuat:

```text
Void
void
VOID
```

sebagai tiga tag.

Normalize pencarian:

```dart
String normalizeTag(
  String value,
) {
  return value
      .trim()
      .toLowerCase();
}
```

Display name masih boleh mempertahankan formatting terpisah bila diperlukan.

# Step 19.13 — Search parser

Buat:

```dart
class SearchQueryParser {
  AssetSearchQuery parse(
    String input,
  ) {
    // tokenize
    // detect field:value
    // remaining text = free search

    return const AssetSearchQuery();
  }
}
```

Support awal:

```text
type:
tag:
favorite:
```

Jangan membuat parser terlalu kompleks dulu.

---

# Step 19.14 — Search Repository

```dart
abstract interface class AssetSearchRepository {
  Future<List<AssetSearchResult>> search(
    AssetSearchQuery query, {
    int limit = 50,
  });
}
```

Result:

```dart
class AssetSearchResult {
  const AssetSearchResult({
    required this.asset,
    required this.score,
  });

  final AssetModel asset;
  final double score;
}
```

# Step 19.15 — Search ranking

Urutan sederhana:

```text
Exact name match
        ↓
Name prefix
        ↓
Name contains
        ↓
Tag match
        ↓
Description
        ↓
Recent usage
```

Jangan masuk semantic/vector search dulu.

Kita akan tambahkan itu nanti.

# Step 19.19 — Collection table

```dart
class AssetCollections extends Table {
  TextColumn get id => text()();

  TextColumn get projectId => text()();

  TextColumn get name => text()();

  TextColumn get description =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant(''),
      )();

  DateTimeColumn get createdAt =>
      dateTime()();

  @override
  Set<Column<Object>> get primaryKey => {
        id,
      };
}
```

Mapping:

```dart
class AssetCollectionItems extends Table {
  TextColumn get collectionId => text()();

  TextColumn get assetId => text()();

  IntColumn get sortOrder =>
      integer().withDefault(
        const Constant(0),
      )();

  @override
  Set<Column<Object>> get primaryKey => {
        collectionId,
        assetId,
      };
}
```

# Step 19.26 — Command Palette

```text
qowner
```

# Step 19.27 — Command model

```dart
class AppCommand {
  const AppCommand({
    required this.id,
    required this.label,
    required this.execute,
    this.keywords = const [],
  });

  final String id;
  final String label;

  final List<String> keywords;

  final Future<void> Function() execute;
}
```

Registry:

```dart
class CommandRegistry {
  final Map<String, AppCommand> _commands = {};

  void register(
    AppCommand command,
  ) {
    _commands[command.id] = command;
  }

  List<AppCommand> search(
    String query,
  ) {
    // ranking
    return [];
  }
}
```

```dart
class NavigationHistory {

```

Jangan membuat history sendiri jika routing utama sudah menyelesaikannya dengan baik. Flutter merekomendasikan pendekatan Router/routing package untuk aplikasi dengan kebutuhan navigasi dan deep-link yang lebih kompleks. citeturn0search1

# Step 19.31 — Deep-linkable assets

```text
/project/<projectId>/story/<storyId>/scene/<sceneId>
```

AI:

```text
/project/<projectId>/ai
```

Ini penting supaya navigation state tidak menjadi spaghetti.

## Status proyek

```text
01 Foundation                       ✅
02 Design System                    ✅
03 Adaptive Shell                   ✅
04 Authentication                  ✅
05 Dashboard                       ✅
06 Project System                  ✅
07 Persistence                     ✅
08 Asset + Character Core          ✅
09 Character Workspace             ✅
10 Selection + Inspector           ✅
11 Wardrobe                        ✅
12 Weapon                          ✅
13 Character Mode + Loadout        ✅
14 Environment                     ✅
15 Story + Scene                   ✅
16 Timeline + Cinematic Editor     ✅
17 AI Context + Prompt Composer    ✅
18 AI Providers + Generation Queue ✅
19 Asset Browser + Global Search   ◀ CURRENT
```

Setelah **Sprint 19**, struktur inti aplikasinya sudah jauh lebih matang. **Sprint 20** sebaiknya menjadi **Reference Library + Image Comparison + Identity Consistency System**: kita membuat Face Reference, Hair Reference, Wardrobe/Weapon reference priority, side-by-side comparison, reference versioning, Primary Reference, serta sistem yang memberi peringatan jika hasil AI menyimpang dari Identity Lock. Itu akan sangat penting untuk menjaga karakter tetap konsisten di banyak scene. memcite

lanjutkan







Lanjut ke **Sprint 20 — Reference Library + Identity Consistency System**.

Sprint ini penting karena aplikasi mulai memiliki banyak gambar referensi dan hasil AI. Kita perlu memastikan **identitas karakter, rambut, wardrobe, weapon, environment, dan desain lainnya tidak berubah tanpa sengaja**.

Target arsitekturnya:

```text
REFERENCE SYSTEM
│
├── Reference Library
│   ├── Character
│   ├── Face
│   ├── Hair
│   ├── Body
│   ├── Wardrobe
│   ├── Weapon
│   ├── Environment
│   ├── Pose
│   └── Style
│
├── Primary Reference
├── Reference Priority
├── Reference Sets
├── Versions
├── Comparison
├── Identity Locks
├── Consistency Rules
├── Consistency Report
└── AI Context Integration
```

Tujuan akhirnya:

```text
Qowner
   ↓
Identity Profile
   ↓
Primary Face Reference
Primary Hair Reference
Primary Body Reference
Primary Wardrobe Reference
Primary Accessories
   ↓
Identity Locks
   ↓
AI Context Resolver
   ↓
Generation
   ↓
Consistency Check
```

# Step 20.1 — Reference tidak boleh sekadar file path

Kita sebelumnya punya beberapa reference table per jenis asset.

Sekarang buat abstraction global:

```dart
enum ReferenceType {
  face,
  hair,
  body,
  fullBody,
  wardrobe,
  weapon,
  accessory,
  environment,
  pose,
  expression,
  material,
  style,
  lighting,
  other,
}
```

Tambahkan source:

```dart
enum ReferenceSource {
  imported,
  generated,
  captured,
  linkedAsset,
}
```

---

# Step 20.2 — Global Reference table

Schema:

```dart
class References extends Table {
  TextColumn get id => text()();

  TextColumn get projectId => text()();

  TextColumn get ownerAssetId =>
      text().nullable()();

  TextColumn get type => text()();

  TextColumn get source => text()();

  TextColumn get name => text()();

  TextColumn get filePath => text()();

  TextColumn get description =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant(''),
      )();

  IntColumn get priority =>
      integer().withDefault(
        const Constant(0),
      )();

  BoolColumn get primaryReference =>
      boolean().withDefault(
        const Constant(false),
      )();

  BoolColumn get enabled =>
      boolean().withDefault(
        const Constant(true),
      )();

  DateTimeColumn get createdAt =>
      dateTime()();

  DateTimeColumn get updatedAt =>
      dateTime()();

  @override
  Set<Column<Object>> get primaryKey => {
        id,
      };
}
```

Sekarang reference menjadi first-class data.

---

# Step 20.3 — Reference owner

Contoh:

```text
Qowner
├── Face Reference
├── Hair Reference
├── Body Reference
└── Pose Reference
```

atau:

```text

├── Front Reference

└── Material Reference
```

`ownerAssetId` menunjuk Asset pemiliknya.

# Step 20.4 — Reference model

```dart
class ReferenceModel {
  const ReferenceModel({
    required this.id,
    required this.projectId,
    required this.type,
    required this.name,
    required this.filePath,
    required this.priority,
    required this.primary,
    required this.enabled,
    this.ownerAssetId,
  });

  final String id;
  final String projectId;

  final String? ownerAssetId;

  final ReferenceType type;

  final String name;
  final String filePath;

  final int priority;

  final bool primary;
  final bool enabled;
}
```

# Step 20.5 — Primary Reference

Untuk setiap kombinasi:

```text
Asset + ReferenceType
```

hanya boleh ada **satu Primary Reference**.

Contoh:

```text
Qowner

FACE
★ qowner_face_master.png
  qowner_face_side.png
  qowner_face_expression.png
```

Service:

```dart
class SetPrimaryReferenceService {
  Future<void> execute({
    required String assetId,
    required ReferenceType type,
    required String referenceId,
  }) async {
    // transaction:
    // unset previous primary
    // set new primary
  }
}
```

Jangan implement logic ini langsung di widget.

```dart
class ReferenceSets extends Table {
  TextColumn get id => text()();

  TextColumn get projectId => text()();

  TextColumn get ownerAssetId =>
      text().nullable()();

  TextColumn get name => text()();

  TextColumn get description =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant(''),
      )();

  BoolColumn get defaultSet =>
      boolean().withDefault(
        const Constant(false),
      )();

  DateTimeColumn get createdAt =>
      dateTime()();

  @override
  Set<Column<Object>> get primaryKey => {
        id,
      };
}
```

Mapping:

```dart
class ReferenceSetItems extends Table {
  TextColumn get referenceSetId => text()();

  TextColumn get referenceId => text()();

  IntColumn get sortOrder =>
      integer().withDefault(
        const Constant(0),
      )();

  @override
  Set<Column<Object>> get primaryKey => {
        referenceSetId,
        referenceId,
      };
}
```

# Step 20.8 — Character Identity Profile

Sekarang kita formalize Identity Lock.

```dart
class CharacterIdentityProfile {
  const CharacterIdentityProfile({
    required this.characterId,
    this.faceLock = true,
    this.hairLock = true,
    this.bodyLock = true,
    this.colorLock = true,
    this.accessoryLock = false,
  });

  final String characterId;

  final bool faceLock;
  final bool hairLock;
  final bool bodyLock;
  final bool colorLock;
  final bool accessoryLock;
}
```

---

# Step 20.9 — Identity Profile table

```dart
class CharacterIdentityProfiles
    extends Table {
  TextColumn get characterId => text()();

  BoolColumn get faceLock =>
      boolean().withDefault(
        const Constant(true),
      )();

  BoolColumn get hairLock =>
      boolean().withDefault(
        const Constant(true),
      )();

  BoolColumn get bodyLock =>
      boolean().withDefault(
        const Constant(true),
      )();

  BoolColumn get colorLock =>
      boolean().withDefault(
        const Constant(true),
      )();

  BoolColumn get accessoryLock =>
      boolean().withDefault(
        const Constant(false),
      )();

  TextColumn get notes =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant(''),
      )();

  @override
  Set<Column<Object>> get primaryKey => {
        characterId,
      };
}
```

```text
face/master.png
hair/master.png
body/full_body.png
```

---

# Step 20.18 — Duplicate file protection

Saat import:

```text
calculate SHA-256
```

Simpan hash:

```dart
TextColumn get contentHash =>
    text().nullable()();
```

Jika file sama sudah ada:

```text
This image already exists as:

Qowner / Face / Master Face
```

User bisa:

```text
Use Existing
Import Anyway
Cancel
```

---

# Step 20.19 — Reference Versioning

Jangan overwrite Master Reference tanpa history.

Contoh:

```text
Qowner Face

v1
2026-07

v2
2026-08

v3
Current
```

Buat:

```dart
class ReferenceVersions extends Table {
  TextColumn get id => text()();

  TextColumn get referenceId => text()();

  IntColumn get version => integer()();

  TextColumn get filePath => text()();

  TextColumn get contentHash =>
      text().nullable()();

  TextColumn get changeNote =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant(''),
      )();

  DateTimeColumn get createdAt =>
      dateTime()();

  @override
  Set<Column<Object>> get primaryKey => {
        id,
      };
}
```

---

# Step 20.20 — Replace Reference

Flow:

```text
Replace Master Face
↓
current file → Version History
↓
new file → Current
↓
update hash
↓
keep same Reference ID
```

Ini penting karena Scene/Prompt tidak perlu berubah reference ID.

```text
[Side by Side] [Overlay] [Swipe]
```

# Step 20.26 — Consistency Rule

```dart
enum ConsistencyRuleType {
  identity,
  hair,
  wardrobe,
  weapon,
  color,
  accessory,
  environment,
  custom,
}
```

Model:

```dart
class ConsistencyRule {
  const ConsistencyRule({
    required this.id,
    required this.assetId,
    required this.type,
    required this.description,
    required this.severity,
  });

  final String id;
  final String assetId;

  final ConsistencyRuleType type;

  final String description;
  final ConsistencySeverity severity;
}
```

---

# Step 20.27 — Severity

```dart
enum ConsistencySeverity {
  info,
  warning,
  critical,
}
```

Contoh:

```text
CRITICAL
Face identity must remain unchanged.

CRITICAL
Weapon blade geometry must remain unchanged.

WARNING
Hair strand arrangement may vary naturally.

INFO
Lighting may change according to scene.
```

```dart
class ConsistencyRules extends Table {

  TextColumn get assetId => text()();

  TextColumn get type => text()();

  TextColumn get description => text()();

  TextColumn get severity => text()();

  BoolColumn get enabled =>
      boolean().withDefault(
        const Constant(true),
      )();

  @override
  Set<Column<Object>> get primaryKey => {
        id,
      };
}
```

# Step 20.33 — Regenerate from inconsistency

```text

failed rules
↓

↓

↓
include relevant references
↓
new generation job
```

Ini mulai membuat aplikasi benar-benar pintar.

# Step 20.37 — Reference priority budget

Provider mungkin hanya menerima beberapa reference image.

Misalnya kita punya:

```text
Face        100
Hair         90
Body         80
Wardrobe     70
Weapon       70
Pose         30
Style        20
```

Jika provider hanya menerima 4:

```text
Face
Hair
Body
Wardrobe
```

Resolver harus memilih berdasarkan priority.

---

# Step 20.38 — Tetapi priority harus context-aware

Untuk Portrait:

```text
Face
Hair
Expression
```

lebih penting.

Untuk Combat Scene:

```text
Face
Wardrobe
Weapon
Body
```

Untuk Wardrobe Detail Sheet:

```text
Wardrobe
Material
Color
```

Jadi resolver menerima:

```dart
PromptTarget target
```

---

# Step 20.39 — Reference Set per Character Mode

Ini sangat penting.

Contoh:

```text
Qowner
│
├── Base Identity
│   ├── Face
│   ├── Hair
│   └── Body
│
├── Combat Mode
│   ├── Combat Hair
│   ├── Combat Wardrobe
│   └── Weapons
│
└── Daily Mode
    ├── Casual Wardrobe
    └── Hair
```

Mode bisa punya:

```text
referenceSetId
```

Jika null:

```text
fallback → Character default set
```

# Step 20.40 — Reference inheritance

Resolver:

```text
Character Default
        ↓
Character Mode
        ↓
Scene Override
```

Contoh:

```text

Character default

Hair
Combat Mode override

Wardrobe
Scene override

Weapon
Combat Mode
```

Ini konsep yang sama dengan CSS inheritance, tetapi untuk character identity.

## Status proyek

```text
01 Foundation                         ✅
02 Design System                      ✅
03 Adaptive Shell                     ✅
04 Authentication                    ✅
05 Dashboard                         ✅
06 Project System                    ✅
07 Persistence                       ✅
08 Asset + Character Core            ✅
09 Character Workspace               ✅
10 Selection + Inspector             ✅
11 Wardrobe                          ✅
12 Weapon                            ✅
13 Character Mode + Loadout          ✅
14 Environment                       ✅
15 Story + Scene                     ✅

17 AI Context + Prompt Composer      ✅
18 AI Providers + Generation Queue   ✅
19 Asset Browser + Global Search     ✅
20 Reference + Consistency System    ◀ CURRENT
```

Setelah Sprint 20, fondasi konsistensi visual sudah jauh lebih kuat. **Sprint 21 — Project Bible + Canon Database + Worldbuilding System** menjadi langkah berikutnya: kita akan menyatukan Character Bible, World Rules, Factions, Locations, Species, Technology, Power System, Terminology, Canon Status, Timeline Era, Lore, dan Canon Conflict Detector. Dengan itu aplikasi mulai berfungsi bukan sekadar sebagai asset manager, tetapi sebagai **database universe/franchise** yang menjaga konsistensi cerita dan visual sekaligus. memcite

lanjutkan





Kita lanjut ke **Sprint 21 — Project Bible + Canon Database + Worldbuilding System**.

Sprint ini membuat Void Scriptor mulai berfungsi sebagai **database universe/franchise**, bukan hanya pengelola asset. Kita akan menghubungkan Story, Character, Environment, Faction, Species, Power System, Technology, Terminology, Era, dan Canon Rules dalam satu sistem yang bisa saling memeriksa konflik.

## Target Sprint 21

Struktur utamanya:

```text
PROJECT BIBLE
│
├── Universe Overview
├── Canon Rules
├── Timeline / Eras
├── Factions
├── Species
├── Locations
├── Technology
├── Power Systems
├── Terminology
├── Historical Events
├── Lore Entries
├── Character Canon
├── Story Canon
└── Conflict Detector
```

Contoh:

```text
Universe
Void Universe

Canon Rules
- Void Black cannot be created artificially
- Byte is unique
- Clan Kokuen existed before the Neo Megacity era

Faction
Clan Kokuen

Character
Reika Kokuen

Historical Event
Fall of Clan Kokuen

Story
Rise of the Void
```

### Step 21.1 — Bible entry model

Buat:

`features/bible/domain/bible_entry.dart`

```dart
enum BibleEntryType {
  overview,
  canonRule,
  faction,
  species,
  location,
  technology,
  powerSystem,
  terminology,
  historicalEvent,
  lore,
  era,
}
```

Model:

```dart
class BibleEntry {
  const BibleEntry({
    required this.id,
    required this.projectId,
    required this.type,
    required this.title,
    required this.content,
    required this.createdAt,
    required this.updatedAt,
    this.status = CanonStatus.canon,
    this.tags = const [],
  });

  final String id;
  final String projectId;

  final BibleEntryType type;

  final String title;
  final String content;

  final CanonStatus status;

  final List<String> tags;

  final DateTime createdAt;
  final DateTime updatedAt;
}
```

# Step 21.4 — Canon Rules

Jangan simpan rule hanya sebagai paragraph.

Buat rule terstruktur:

```dart
enum CanonRuleSeverity {
  info,
  warning,
  hard,
}
```

Table:

```dart
class CanonRules extends Table {
  TextColumn get id => text()();

  TextColumn get projectId => text()();

  TextColumn get title => text()();

  TextColumn get ruleText => text()();

  TextColumn get severity =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant('hard'),
      )();

  BoolColumn get enabled =>
      boolean().withDefault(
        const Constant(true),
      )();

  TextColumn get scope =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant('global'),
      )();

  @override
  Set<Column<Object>> get primaryKey => {id};
}
```

Contoh:

```text
HARD RULE
Byte is unique.

HARD RULE
Void Black cannot be artificially reproduced.

WARNING
Purple energy must not appear in Reika Kokuen's Void Black form.
```

```dart
class PowerAbilities extends Table {

      )();

  IntColumn get sortOrder =>
      integer().withDefault(
        const Constant(0),
      )();

  @override
  Set<Column<Object>> get primaryKey => {id};
}
```

---

# Step 21.14 — Terminology database

Universe besar butuh glossary.

Contoh:

```text
Void Scriptor
Void Black
Combat Mode
Byte
Neo-Lyra
```

Buat:

```dart
class TerminologyEntries extends Table {
  TextColumn get id => text()();

  TextColumn get projectId => text()();

  TextColumn get term => text()();

  TextColumn get definition => text()();

  TextColumn get category =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant('general'),
      )();

  TextColumn get alternateTerms =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant(''),
      )();

  BoolColumn get caseSensitive =>
      boolean().withDefault(
        const Constant(false),
      )();

  @override
  Set<Column<Object>> get primaryKey => {id};
}
```

---

# Step 21.15 — Alias system

Terminology harus mendukung alias.

Lebih baik akhirnya normalize:

```text
term
alias
```

Contoh:

```text
Qowner
Void Scriptor
```

tapi jangan anggap alias selalu synonym.

Untuk sekarang `alternateTerms` cukup. Nanti bisa dinormalisasi.

```dart
      text().nullable()();
```

# Step 21.24 — Canon Conflict Engine

Ini inti sprint.

Kita mulai dengan **rule-based**, belum AI.

Flow:

```text
Project Data
↓
Canon Rules
↓
Validation Rules
↓
Conflicts
```

Contoh:

```text
RULE
Reika's Void Black has no purple visual effects.

SCENE
Reika Battle Scene

VFX
Purple Void Aura

RESULT
CANON CONFLICT
```

---

# Step 21.25 — Canon conflict model

```dart
enum CanonConflictSeverity {
  info,
  warning,
  error,
  critical,
}
```

Model:

```dart
class CanonConflict {
  const CanonConflict({
    required this.id,
    required this.ruleId,
    required this.targetId,
    required this.message,
    required this.severity,
  });

  final String id;

  final String ruleId;
  final String targetId;

  final String message;

  final CanonConflictSeverity severity;
}
```

# Step 21.26 — Conflicts table

```dart
      boolean().withDefault(
        const Constant(false),
      )();

      text().withDefault(
        const Constant(''),
      )();

      dateTime()();
```

# Step 21.27 — Validation rule types

Untuk awal:

```dart
enum CanonValidatorType {
  requiredRelation,
  forbiddenRelation,

  forbiddenTag,
```

atau:

# Step 21.28 — Structured canon rule

Tambahkan:

```dart
class CanonRuleConditions extends Table {
  TextColumn get id => text()();

  TextColumn get ruleId => text()();

  TextColumn get validatorType => text()();

  TextColumn get targetField =>
      text().nullable()();

  TextColumn get operator =>
      text().nullable()();

  TextColumn get expectedValue =>
      text().nullable()();

  Set<Column<Object>> get primaryKey => {id};
}
```

Jadi rule tidak hanya human-readable text.

---

# Step 21.29 — Human + machine rule

Satu rule punya dua bagian:

```text
Human Rule:
Byte is unique in the universe.

Machine Rule:
assetType=character
name=Byte
count <= 1
```

Human text untuk user.

Machine condition untuk validator.

# Step 21.34 — Resolve conflict

```text

Fix
Mark Intentional
Ignore Rule Here
```

```text

```

Buat exception.

# Step 21.50 — Test scenario

Buat faction:

```text
Clan Kokuen
Status: Destroyed
```

Character relation:

```text
Reika Kokuen
memberOf
Clan Kokuen
```

Buat Power System:

```text
Void Black
```

Rule:

```text
Reika Void Black cannot contain purple visual effects.
```

Machine rule contoh:

```text
target = Reika
field = visualPalette
operator = notContains
value = purple
```

Buat Scene test yang memakai:

```text
Purple Void Aura
```

Jalankan:

```text
Validate Canon
```

Expected:

```text
ERROR

Reika Kokuen
Scene XX

Purple visual effect conflicts with
Void Black canon rule.
```

Perbaiki menjadi:

```text
Black + Silver
```

Scan kembali.

Conflict harus resolved/removed sesuai policy.

## Status proyek

```text
01 Foundation                         ✅
02 Design System                      ✅
03 Adaptive Shell                     ✅
04 Authentication                    ✅
05 Dashboard                         ✅
06 Project System                    ✅
07 Persistence                       ✅
08 Asset + Character Core            ✅
09 Character Workspace               ✅
10 Selection + Inspector             ✅
11 Wardrobe                          ✅
12 Weapon                            ✅
13 Character Mode + Loadout          ✅
14 Environment                       ✅
15 Story + Scene                     ✅
16 Timeline + Cinematic Editor       ✅
17 AI Context + Prompt Composer      ✅
18 AI Providers + Generation Queue   ✅
19 Asset Browser + Global Search     ✅
20 Reference + Consistency System    ✅
21 Project Bible + Canon System      ◀ CURRENT
```

Berikutnya saya sarankan **Sprint 22 — Creature + Vehicle + Prop System**. Itu akan melengkapi database produksi: Creature/Companion, Vehicle, Background Prop, interactive prop, ownership/usage relations, technical detail workspaces, dan Scene integration. Setelah Sprint 22, hampir seluruh jenis asset produksi utama sudah memiliki domain sendiri. memcite

lanjutkan







Lanjut ke **Sprint 22 — Creature + Vehicle + Prop Production System**.

Sprint ini melengkapi database produksi utama. Setelah Character, Wardrobe, Weapon, Environment, Story, Timeline, AI, Reference, dan Canon tersedia, sekarang kita tambahkan tiga kelompok asset besar: **Creature/Companion, Vehicle, dan Prop**.

Arsitekturnya:

```text
PRODUCTION ASSET DATABASE
│
├── Character
├── Wardrobe
├── Weapon
├── Environment
├── Creature       ← NEW
├── Vehicle        ← NEW
├── Prop           ← NEW
├── Story
├── Scene
└── VFX / Audio    ← sprint berikutnya
```

Untuk proyek seperti Void Scriptor, sistem ini memungkinkan Byte, dragon, combat drone, kendaraan, capsule, furniture, cup kopi, perangkat teknologi, dan berbagai object scene menjadi asset yang benar-benar dapat digunakan ulang.

---

# Step 22.1 — Tambahkan Asset Types

Perluas:

```dart
enum AssetType {
  character,
  wardrobe,
  weapon,
  environment,

  creature,
  vehicle,
  prop,

  story,
  scene,
  faction,
  species,
  technology,
  powerSystem,
  aiGeneration,
}
```

Semua tetap menggunakan `Assets` sebagai metadata universal.

---

# Step 22.2 — Creature Category

Creature jangan diasumsikan selalu hewan.

```dart
enum CreatureCategory {
  animal,
  companion,
  monster,
  dragon,
  spirit,
  synthetic,
  biological,
  hybrid,
  unknown,
}
```

Ini memungkinkan:

```text
Dragon
Wolf
Mascot
Artificial creature
Alien organism
Spirit entity
```

# Step 22.3 — Creature Model

Buat:

`features/creatures/domain/creature_model.dart`

```dart
class CreatureModel {
  const CreatureModel({
    required this.assetId,
    required this.projectId,
    required this.name,
    required this.category,
    this.speciesId,
    this.description = '',
    this.behavior = '',
    this.abilities = '',
    this.origin = '',
  });

  final String assetId;
  final String projectId;

  final String name;

  final CreatureCategory category;

  final String? speciesId;

  final String description;
  final String behavior;
  final String abilities;
  final String origin;
}
```

```dart
      text().nullable()();
```

```dart
      boolean().withDefault(
        const Constant(false),

      };
}
```

Kita langsung mendapatkan Reference inheritance dari Sprint 20.

# Step 22.14 — Vehicle Model

```dart
class VehicleModel {
  const VehicleModel({
    required this.assetId,
    required this.projectId,
    required this.name,
    required this.category,
    this.description = '',
    this.manufacturer = '',
    this.role = '',
  });

  final String assetId;
  final String projectId;

  final String name;

  final VehicleCategory category;
```

# Step 22.30 — Prop Timeline Clip

Contoh adegan pantai:

```text

Coffee Cup
State: Half

12–14 sec
Coffee Cup
State: Empty
```

Kemudian:

```text
14 sec
Qowner places cup on chair
```

```text
EMPTY
ON CHAIR
```

bukan kembali ke tangan karakter.

# Step 22.31 — Scene Asset Instances

```text

vs

```

```text

Coffee Cup
```

tetapi Scene memiliki:

```text
INSTANCE
Coffee Cup #1

State: Half
Location: Qowner right hand
```

Jangan mengubah master Prop hanya karena state di Scene berubah.

# Step 22.41 — Scene Continuity Snapshot Table

```dart
class SceneContinuitySnapshots
    extends Table {
  TextColumn get id => text()();

  TextColumn get sceneId => text()();

  TextColumn get instanceId => text()();

  TextColumn get snapshotData => text()();

  DateTimeColumn get createdAt =>
      dateTime()();

  @override
  Set<Column<Object>> get primaryKey => {
        id,
      };
}
```

`snapshotData` boleh JSON karena ini memang serialized state snapshot.

# Step 22.42 — Next Scene inheritance

Saat membuat Scene berikutnya:

```text

```

```text
Qowner
Byte
Coffee Cup
Vehicle
```

bisa dibawa dengan state akhir sebelumnya.

---

# Step 22.43 — Continuity Conflict

Contoh:

Scene 04:

```text
Coffee Cup
EMPTY
```

Scene 05:

```text
Coffee Cup
FULL
```

tanpa event refill.

Report:

```text
CONTINUITY WARNING

Coffee Cup changed:

EMPTY → FULL

No transition was recorded.
```

Ini memperluas Canon Conflict Engine menjadi production continuity.

## Status Proyek

```text
01 Foundation                           ✅
02 Design System                        ✅
03 Adaptive Shell                       ✅
04 Authentication                      ✅
05 Dashboard                           ✅
06 Project System                      ✅
07 Persistence                         ✅
08 Asset + Character Core              ✅
09 Character Workspace                 ✅
10 Selection + Inspector               ✅
11 Wardrobe                            ✅
12 Weapon                              ✅
13 Character Mode + Loadout            ✅
14 Environment                         ✅
15 Story + Scene                       ✅
16 Timeline + Cinematic Editor         ✅
17 AI Context + Prompt Composer        ✅
18 AI Providers + Generation Queue     ✅
19 Asset Browser + Global Search       ✅
20 Reference + Consistency System      ✅
21 Project Bible + Canon System        ✅
22 Creature + Vehicle + Prop System    ◀ CURRENT
```

**Sprint 23** berikutnya sebaiknya **VFX + Audio + Music + Cinematic Production Database**. Kita akan menambahkan VFX assets, particle/effect states, sound effects, ambience, dialogue/voice, music cues, audio timeline, scene mixing, VFX continuity, serta menghubungkannya ke Timeline dan AI Prompt Composer. Setelah itu seluruh komponen utama sebuah scene—Character, Creature, Vehicle, Prop, Environment, Camera, VFX, Audio, dan Story—sudah berada dalam satu production pipeline. memcite

lajutkan

Lanjut ke **Sprint 23 — VFX + Audio + Music + Cinematic Production System**.

Sprint ini mengisi bagian produksi yang belum lengkap. Setelah selesai, satu Scene tidak hanya mengetahui siapa karakter dan objeknya, tetapi juga **efek visual apa yang terjadi, suara apa yang terdengar, ambience, dialog, musik, kapan semuanya mulai/berakhir, dan bagaimana continuity-nya**.

```text
SCENE
├── Character
├── Creature
├── Vehicle
├── Wardrobe
├── Weapon
├── Prop
├── Environment
├── Camera
├── Action
├── VFX          ← NEW
├── Dialogue     ← EXPANDED
├── SFX          ← NEW
├── Ambience     ← NEW
├── Music        ← NEW
└── Continuity
```

# Sprint 23 — Production FX & Audio

## Step 23.1 — Tambahkan Asset Type

Perluas `AssetType`:

```dart
enum AssetType {
  character,
  creature,

  wardrobe,
  weapon,

  vehicle,
  prop,

  environment,

  vfx,
  soundEffect,
  ambience,
  music,

  story,
  scene,

  faction,
  species,
  technology,
  powerSystem,

  aiGeneration,
}
```

Kita tetap menggunakan `Assets` sebagai universal metadata.

# Step 23.3 — VFX Model

```dart
class VfxModel {
  const VfxModel({
    required this.assetId,
    required this.projectId,
    required this.name,
    required this.category,
    this.description = '',
    this.visualBehavior = '',
    this.colorPalette = '',
  });

  final String assetId;
  final String projectId;

  final String name;

  final VfxCategory category;

  final String description;
  final String visualBehavior;

}
```

---

# Step 23.4 — VFX Table

```dart
class VfxAssets extends Table {
  TextColumn get assetId => text()();

  TextColumn get projectId => text()();

  TextColumn get name => text()();

  TextColumn get category => text()();

  TextColumn get description =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant(''),
      )();

  TextColumn get visualBehavior =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant(''),
      )();

  TextColumn get colorPalette =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant(''),
      )();

  @override
  Set<Column<Object>> get primaryKey => {
        assetId,
      };
}
```

# Step 23.32 — Waveform

Audio clip harus punya waveform preview.

Jangan decode seluruh audio setiap frame.

Saat import:

```text
Audio File
↓
Decode once
↓
Generate waveform peaks
↓
Cache
```

Buat:

```dart
class AudioWaveformCache extends Table {
  TextColumn get audioFileId => text()();

  TextColumn get peaksData => text()();

  IntColumn get resolution => integer()();

  @override
  Set<Column<Object>> get primaryKey => {
        audioFileId,
      };
}
```

`peaksData` boleh serialized compressed data.

---

# Step 23.33 — Timeline Waveform

Visual:

```text
Dialogue

   ╭─╮    ╭────╮
───╯ ╰────╯    ╰────
```

Zoom timeline harus memengaruhi waveform density.

Jangan regenerate waveform ketika zoom.

Gunakan cached peaks dan resample untuk viewport.

# Step 23.46 — Audio Import Pipeline

Flow:

```text
Import Audio
↓
Copy into project
↓
Calculate SHA-256
↓
Read metadata
↓
Generate waveform
↓
Create AudioFile
↓
Create Asset
```

Directory:

```text
assets/
└── audio/
    ├── dialogue/
    ├── sfx/
    ├── ambience/
    ├── music/
    └── voice_reference/
```

Jangan gunakan Desktop/Downloads path sebagai canonical file.

# Step 23.51 — Provider Capability Matrix

Ini penting.

Perluas provider capabilities:

```dart
class AiProviderCapabilities {
  const AiProviderCapabilities({
    this.imageInput = false,
    this.imageGeneration = false,
    this.videoGeneration = false,
    this.audioGeneration = false,
    this.voiceGeneration = false,
    this.referenceImages = false,
    this.referenceAudio = false,
  });

  final bool imageInput;
  final bool imageGeneration;
  final bool videoGeneration;

  final bool audioGeneration;
  final bool voiceGeneration;

  final bool referenceImages;
  final bool referenceAudio;
}
```

Jangan asumsikan semua provider mendukung semuanya.

### Berikutnya — Sprint 24

Sekarang fondasi produksinya sudah cukup lengkap sehingga **jangan langsung menambah jenis asset baru lagi**. Sprint 24 sebaiknya menjadi **Project File System + Import/Export + Backup + Portable Project Package**.

Kita akan membuat format project seperti:

```text
MyProject.voidproject
│
├── project.json
├── database/
│   └── project.db
│
├── assets/
│   ├── images/
│   ├── references/
│   ├── audio/
│   ├── video/
│   └── generated/
│
├── cache/
│   ├── thumbnails/
│   └── waveforms/
│
├── exports/
│
└── backups/
```

Kemudian kita tambahkan **autosave, crash recovery, backup versioning, project duplication, portable export, archive/import project, missing-file repair, cache rebuild, storage analyzer, dan safe project migration**. Ini penting sebelum proyek mulai menyimpan ratusan atau ribuan asset. memcite

lanjutkan





Lanjut ke **Sprint 24 — Project File System + Import/Export + Backup + Portable Project Package**.

Sprint ini fokus ke ketahanan proyek. Setelah ini, project tidak hanya bisa dibuat dan diedit, tetapi juga **dipindahkan ke komputer lain, di-backup, dipulihkan setelah crash, diperbaiki jika ada file hilang, dan diekspor sebagai satu paket portabel**.

Target akhirnya:

```text
MyProject.voidproject
│
├── manifest/
│   └── project.json
├── database/
│   └── project.db
├── assets/
│   ├── images/
│   ├── references/
│   ├── audio/
│   ├── video/
│   ├── generated/
│   └── attachments/
├── cache/
│   ├── thumbnails/
│   ├── waveforms/
│   └── previews/
├── backups/
└── exports/
```

Dan flow:

```text
Open Project
   ↓
Validate Package
   ↓
Open DB
   ↓
Validate Files
   ↓
Restore Session
```

## Step 24.1 — Project Package abstraction

Buat:

`core/project_package/project_package.dart`

```dart
import 'dart:io';

class ProjectPackage {
  const ProjectPackage({
    required this.projectId,
    required this.root,
  });

  final String projectId;
  final Directory root;

  Directory get manifestDirectory =>
      Directory('${root.path}/manifest');

  File get manifestFile =>
      File('${manifestDirectory.path}/project.json');

  Directory get databaseDirectory =>
      Directory('${root.path}/database');

  File get databaseFile =>
      File('${databaseDirectory.path}/project.db');

  Directory get assetsDirectory =>
      Directory('${root.path}/assets');

  Directory get cacheDirectory =>
      Directory('${root.path}/cache');

  Directory get backupDirectory =>
      Directory('${root.path}/backups');

  Directory get exportDirectory =>
      Directory('${root.path}/exports');
}
```

Mulai sekarang jangan lagi membentuk path project secara acak di berbagai fitur.

---

## Step 24.2 — Directory resolver

Buat:

```dart
class ProjectPackagePaths {
  static Directory references(
    ProjectPackage package,
  ) {
    return Directory(
      '${package.assetsDirectory.path}/references',
    );
  }

  static Directory audio(
    ProjectPackage package,
  ) {
    return Directory(
      '${package.assetsDirectory.path}/audio',
    );
  }

  static Directory generated(
    ProjectPackage package,
  ) {
    return Directory(
      '${package.assetsDirectory.path}/generated',
    );
  }

  static Directory thumbnails(
    ProjectPackage package,
  ) {
    return Directory(
      '${package.cacheDirectory.path}/thumbnails',
    );
  }

  static Directory waveforms(
    ProjectPackage package,
  ) {
    return Directory(
      '${package.cacheDirectory.path}/waveforms',
    );
  }
}
```

---

## Step 24.3 — Package manifest

`project.json` sekarang perlu lebih lengkap.

```json
{
  "format": "voidproject",
  "schemaVersion": 16,
  "projectId": "UUID",
  "name": "Void Universe",
  "description": "",
  "createdAt": "",
  "updatedAt": "",
  "application": {
    "name": "Void Scriptor",
    "version": "0.1.0"
  },
  "database": {
    "file": "database/project.db"
  },
  "assets": {
    "root": "assets"
  }
}
```

Perbedakan:

```text
schemaVersion
```

dengan:

```text
application.version
```

Keduanya tidak sama.

---

## Step 24.4 — Package version

Tambahkan:

```dart
abstract final class VoidProjectFormat {
  static const String name = 'voidproject';
  static const int currentSchema = 16;
}
```

Jangan menyebarkan angka `16` ke seluruh aplikasi.

Create Project flow sekarang:

```text
Create Project
↓

↓

↓
Create manifest/
↓

↓
Create assets/
↓
Create cache/
↓
Create backups/
↓

↓
Create DB
↓

↓

```

Jika satu tahap gagal:
