```text
ROLLBACK PACKAGE
```

---

## Step 24.6 — Relative paths only

Sangat penting.

Database jangan menyimpan:

```text
C:\Users\Choirudin\Documents\VoidScriptor\...
```

atau:

```text
/home/user/VoidScriptor/...
```

Simpan:

```text
assets/references/<uuid>/face/master.png
```

Lalu resolve:

```dart
String resolveProjectPath(
  ProjectPackage package,
  String relativePath,
) {
  return '${package.root.path}/$relativePath';
}
```

Inilah yang membuat project benar-benar portabel.

---

## Step 24.7 — Path migration

Karena sprint sebelumnya mungkin masih menyimpan absolute path, buat migrator:

```text
Absolute Path
↓
Is inside project?
↓
Convert to relative
```

Contoh:

```text
Before:
C:\...\Projects\UUID\assets\audio\ocean.wav

After:
assets/audio/ocean.wav
```

File eksternal di luar project jangan dipaksa.

Tandai sebagai external dependency dulu.

---

# Step 24.8 — File reference model

Kita butuh abstraction universal.

```dart
enum ProjectFileLocation {
  internal,
  external,
  missing,
}
```

```dart
class ProjectFileReference {
  const ProjectFileReference({
    required this.path,
    required this.location,
    this.contentHash,
  });

  final String path;
  final ProjectFileLocation location;
  final String? contentHash;
}
```

---

# Step 24.9 — Internal vs external files

Import dialog:

```text
Import Asset

○ Copy into Project
○ Link External File
```

Default:

```text
Copy into Project
```

External linking hanya untuk user advanced.

---

# Step 24.10 — External dependency registry

Buat:

```dart
class ExternalFiles extends Table {
  TextColumn get id => text()();

  TextColumn get projectId => text()();

  TextColumn get absolutePath => text()();

  TextColumn get contentHash =>
      text().nullable()();

  BoolColumn get available =>
      boolean().withDefault(
        const Constant(true),
      )();

  @override
  Set<Column<Object>> get primaryKey => {id};
}
```

Kalau project dipindah komputer, dependency external bisa menjadi missing.

# Step 24.13 — `.voidproject` format

Ada dua pendekatan:

```text
folder
```

atau:

```text
single archive
```

Saya sarankan selama editing gunakan folder package.

Contoh:

```text
Void Universe.voidproject/
```

Untuk transfer/export baru zip menjadi:

```text
Void Universe.voidproject
```

satu file archive.

---

# Step 24.14 — Working package vs portable archive

Bedakan:

```text
WORKING PROJECT
directory

PORTABLE PROJECT
archive
```

Jangan edit database langsung di dalam zip.

Flow:

```text
Import .voidproject
↓
Validate archive
↓
Extract to project directory
↓
Open working package
```

---

# Step 24.15 — Archive manifest

Saat export:

```text
voidproject.archive.json
```

bisa berisi:

```json
{
  "format": "voidproject-archive",
  "version": 1,
  "projectId": "...",
  "createdAt": "...",
  "sourceSchemaVersion": 16,
  "checksum": "..."
}
```

Ini membantu validasi sebelum import.

# Step 24.16 — Export Project service

```dart
abstract interface class ProjectExporter {
  Future<File> exportPortable(
    ProjectPackage package,
  );
}
```

Flow:

```text
Validate
↓
Checkpoint DB
↓
Create temporary export directory
↓
Copy required files
↓
Exclude disposable cache
↓
Create archive
↓
Validate archive
↓
Return file
```

# Step 24.22 — Backup Engine

# Step 24.23 — Backup record

```dart
class ProjectBackups extends Table {
  TextColumn get id => text()();

  TextColumn get projectId => text()();

  TextColumn get type => text()();

  TextColumn get relativePath => text()();

  IntColumn get schemaVersion => integer()();

  IntColumn get sizeBytes => integer()();

  DateTimeColumn get createdAt => dateTime()();

  TextColumn get note =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant(''),
      )();

  @override
  Set<Column<Object>> get primaryKey => {id};
}
```

---

# Step 24.24 — Backup contents

```text
database
```

bukan semua asset setiap kali.

# Step 24.25 — Full backup

Manual option:

```text
Create Full Backup
```

isi:

```text
manifest
database
assets
```

Digunakan sebelum perubahan besar atau transfer.

---

# Step 24.26 — Automatic backup retention

Policy contoh:

```text
Last 10 hourly
Last 7 daily
Last 4 weekly
```

Jangan simpan ribuan backup tanpa cleanup.

Buat:

```dart
class BackupRetentionPolicy {
  const BackupRetentionPolicy({
    this.maxAutomatic = 20,
    this.maxManual = 50,
  });

  final int maxAutomatic;
  final int maxManual;
}
```

---

# Step 24.27 — Pre-migration backup

Ini wajib.

Saat schema:

```text
15 → 16
```

sebelum migration:

```text
CREATE PRE-MIGRATION BACKUP
```

baru:

```text
migration
```

Jika gagal:

```text
restore
```

---

# Step 24.28 — Migration transaction

Flow:

```text
Open Project
↓
Detected old schema
↓
Backup
↓
Migration
↓
Integrity check
↓
Update manifest
↓
Open
```

Jika gagal:

```text
Rollback
↓
Recovery screen
```

---

# Step 24.29 — Crash Recovery

Kita butuh session marker.

Saat Project dibuka:

```text
session.lock
```

Saat clean shutdown:

```text
delete session.lock
```

Jika startup menemukan lock:

```text
Previous session may have ended unexpectedly.
```

---

# Step 24.30 — Session lock content

```json
{
  "sessionId": "...",
  "projectId": "...",
  "openedAt": "...",
  "appVersion": "..."
}
```

Jangan menyimpan secret.

# Step 24.31 — Recovery screen

```text
VOID UNIVERSE

The previous session did not close normally.

Last autosave:
10:37

Recovery snapshot:
10:38

[Open Latest Saved]
[Recover Autosave]
```

---

# Step 24.32 — Recovery snapshots

Folder:

```text
recovery/
├── session.json
├── project.db.recovery
└── manifest.recovery.json
```

Ini berbeda dari backup.

Recovery = short-lived crash protection.

Backup = long-term restore point.

---

# Step 24.33 — Autosave journal

Jangan copy full DB setiap 1 detik.

Gunakan DB transaction/autosave normal dan buat periodic recovery checkpoint.

Misalnya:

```text
normal save
continuous

recovery checkpoint
every few minutes / meaningful state
```

---

# Step 24.34 — Dirty state integration

Project status:

```text
Void Universe
```

Saved.

```text
Void Universe •
```

Dirty.

```text
Void Universe ◌
```

Saving.

```text
Void Universe ✓
```

Saved briefly, lalu kembali normal.

---

# Step 24.35 — Save coordinator

Buat:

```dart
class ProjectSaveCoordinator {
  Future<void> save(
    String projectId,
  ) async {
    // flush repositories
    // checkpoint db
    // write manifest
    // clear dirty state
  }
}
```

Semua `Ctrl/Cmd + S` menuju service ini.

---

# Step 24.36 — Cache System

Cache harus disposable.

```text
cache/
├── thumbnails/
├── waveforms/
├── image_previews/
├── search/
└── temp/
```

Tidak boleh ada satu-satunya salinan data penting di sini.

---

# Step 24.37 — Cache index

```dart
class CacheEntries extends Table {
  TextColumn get key => text()();

  TextColumn get relativePath => text()();

  TextColumn get cacheType => text()();

  IntColumn get sizeBytes => integer()();

  DateTimeColumn get createdAt => dateTime()();

  DateTimeColumn get lastAccessedAt =>
      dateTime()();

  @override
  Set<Column<Object>> get primaryKey => {key};
}
```

```dart
class TrashEntries extends Table {
  TextColumn get id => text()();

  TextColumn get originalPath => text()();

  TextColumn get trashPath => text()();

  TextColumn get entityId =>
      text().nullable()();

  DateTimeColumn get deletedAt => dateTime()();

  @override
  Set<Column<Object>> get primaryKey => {id};
}
```

# Step 24.76 — Android considerations

Android tidak selalu nyaman dengan arbitrary folders.

`path_provider` tetap menjadi working root.

Untuk import/export gunakan file picker/share flow nanti.

Project format internal tetap sama:

```text
manifest
database
assets
```

Jadi portable archive bisa dipindah antar platform.

---

# Step 24.77 — Cross-platform compatibility

Project dari:

```text
Windows
```

harus bisa dibuka di:

```text
Linux
macOS
Android
```

karena semua path internal relative dengan `/`-style logical path dan di-resolve menggunakan `path` package.

Jangan simpan separator platform sebagai domain data.

---

# Step 24.78 — File naming

Canonical file names:

```text
UUID.ext
```

bukan nama display.

Contoh:

```text
assets/references/
7ac0...e91.png
```

Metadata:

```text
Master Face
```

tetap ada di DB.

Ini menghindari masalah karakter ilegal dan duplicate names.

---

# Step 24.79 — File extension preservation

Import:

```text
original.jpg
```

menjadi:

```text
UUID.jpg
```

Jangan ubah semua menjadi `.png` tanpa alasan.

Simpan mime/type metadata.

---

# Step 24.80 — Collision avoidance

Karena UUID:

```text
no filename collision
```

bahkan kalau 100 file bernama:

```text
image.png
```

di-import.

---

# Step 24.81 — Atomic manifest save

Jangan:

```text
write project.json directly
```

Gunakan:

```text
project.json.tmp
↓
flush
↓
validate
↓
project.json → .bak
↓
.tmp → project.json
```

Ini implementasi nyata dari rancangan Sprint 07.

---

# Step 24.82 — Database checkpoint

Sebelum archive/backup:

```text

checkpoint
```

supaya file DB konsisten.

Jika nanti SQLite WAL dipakai, pastikan WAL di-checkpoint sebelum copy.

# Sprint 24 Definition of Done

Sprint ini dianggap selesai hanya jika:

```text
✓ semua canonical project paths relatif
✓ project dapat diekspor menjadi portable archive
✓ archive dapat di-import kembali
✓ backup dan restore bekerja
✓ migration membuat pre-migration backup
✓ crash session terdeteksi
✓ recovery flow bekerja
✓ missing files tidak menyebabkan crash
✓ cache bisa dihapus dan dibangun ulang
✓ trash/restore bekerja
✓ project lock tersedia
✓ read-only open mode tersedia
```

## Status Void Scriptor

```text
01 Foundation                           ✅
02 Design System                        ✅
03 Adaptive Shell                       ✅
04 Authentication                      ✅
05 Dashboard                           ✅
06 Project System                      ✅
07 Persistence                         ✅
08 Asset + Character Core              ✅
09 Character Workspace                 ✅
10 Selection + Inspector               ✅
11 Wardrobe                            ✅
12 Weapon                              ✅
13 Character Mode + Loadout            ✅
14 Environment                         ✅
15 Story + Scene                       ✅
16 Timeline + Cinematic Editor         ✅
17 AI Context + Prompt Composer        ✅
18 AI Providers + Generation Queue     ✅
19 Asset Browser + Global Search       ✅
20 Reference + Consistency System      ✅
21 Project Bible + Canon System        ✅
22 Creature + Vehicle + Prop System    ✅
23 VFX + Audio + Production System     ✅
24 Project Package + Backup/Recovery   ◀ CURRENT
```

**Sprint 25** sebaiknya menjadi **Undo/Redo + Command System + Transaction History + Version Control**. Semua editor yang sudah kita buat—Character, Wardrobe, Weapon, Scene, Timeline, Canon, VFX, dan Audio—akan mulai menggunakan satu mutation pipeline sehingga `Ctrl/Cmd+Z`, redo, history, compare revision, autosave, dan recovery bekerja konsisten di seluruh aplikasi. Ini sebaiknya kita selesaikan sebelum menambah fitur besar lain.

lanjutkan







Kita lanjut ke **Sprint 25 — Undo/Redo + Command System + Transaction History + Version Control**.

Sprint ini penting karena aplikasi kita sudah punya banyak editor. Mulai sekarang perubahan data tidak boleh dilakukan langsung oleh UI secara acak. Semua perubahan utama melewati satu **Command/Mutation Pipeline**.

```text
USER ACTION
    ↓
COMMAND
    ↓
VALIDATE
    ↓
TRANSACTION
    ↓
DATABASE
    ↓
HISTORY
    ↓
EVENT BUS
    ↓
UI UPDATE
```

Hasil akhirnya:

```text
Ctrl+Z        Undo
Ctrl+Shift+Z  Redo
Ctrl+S        Save

History
Revisions
Snapshots
Compare
Restore
```

Dan semuanya bekerja konsisten pada Character, Wardrobe, Weapon, Environment, Story, Timeline, VFX, Audio, Canon, dan asset lainnya.

---

# Step 25.1 — Mutation architecture

Sebelumnya kita mungkin punya pola:

```dart
await repository.updateCharacter(character);
```

langsung dari widget.

Mulai Sprint 25, untuk operasi yang perlu undo/history gunakan:

```dart
await commandBus.execute(
  UpdateCharacterCommand(...),
);
```

Struktur:

```text
Presentation
    ↓
Command Bus
    ↓
Command Handler
    ↓
Repository
    ↓
Database
```

Widget tidak perlu mengetahui bagaimana undo/history disimpan.

---

# Step 25.2 — Command interface

Buat:

`core/commands/app_command.dart`

```dart
abstract interface class AppCommand {
  String get id;

  String get projectId;

  String get label;

  Future<void> execute();

  Future<void> undo();
}
```

Contoh label:

```text
Rename Qowner
Change Wardrobe
Move Timeline Clip
Delete Weapon
Update Canon Rule
```

---

# Step 25.3 — Base command

Kita bisa buat:

```dart
abstract class BaseCommand
    implements AppCommand {
  BaseCommand({
    required this.id,
    required this.projectId,
    required this.label,
  });

  @override
  final String id;

  @override
  final String projectId;

  @override
  final String label;
}
```

---

# Step 25.4 — Command Bus

Buat:

```dart
class CommandBus {
  CommandBus({
    required this.history,
  });

  final CommandHistory history;

  Future<void> execute(
    AppCommand command,
  ) async {
    await command.execute();

    await history.push(command);
  }
}
```

Tetapi ini baru bentuk sederhana.

Kita akan memperkuatnya dengan transaction.

---

# Step 25.5 — Command History

```dart
class CommandHistory {
  final List<AppCommand> _undoStack = [];
  final List<AppCommand> _redoStack = [];

  bool get canUndo => _undoStack.isNotEmpty;

  bool get canRedo => _redoStack.isNotEmpty;

  Future<void> push(
    AppCommand command,
  ) async {
    _undoStack.add(command);

    _redoStack.clear();
  }
}
```

Aturan penting:

```text
New command
→ clear redo stack
```

---

# Step 25.6 — Undo

```dart
Future<void> undo() async {
  if (_undoStack.isEmpty) {
    return;
  }

  final command = _undoStack.removeLast();

  await command.undo();

  _redoStack.add(command);
}
```

---

# Step 25.7 — Redo

Lebih baik command mempunyai:

```dart
redo()
```

daripada mengasumsikan `execute()` selalu aman dipanggil ulang.

Upgrade:

```dart
abstract interface class AppCommand {
  String get id;

  String get projectId;

  String get label;

  Future<void> execute();

  Future<void> undo();

  Future<void> redo();
}
```

---

# Step 25.8 — Example Rename Command

```dart
class RenameAssetCommand extends BaseCommand {
  RenameAssetCommand({
    required super.id,
    required super.projectId,
    required super.label,
    required this.repository,
    required this.assetId,
    required this.oldName,
    required this.newName,
  });

  final AssetRepository repository;

  final String assetId;

  final String oldName;
  final String newName;

  @override
  Future<void> execute() {
    return repository.rename(
      assetId,
      newName,
    );
  }

  @override
  Future<void> undo() {
    return repository.rename(
      assetId,
      oldName,
    );
  }

  @override
  Future<void> redo() {
    return repository.rename(
      assetId,
      newName,
    );
  }
}
```

Sekarang:

```text
Qowner
↓ rename
Void Scriptor
↓ Ctrl+Z
Qowner
↓ Redo
Void Scriptor
```

---

# Step 25.9 — Jangan hitung old state setelah mutation

Command harus mengetahui state sebelumnya **sebelum** perubahan.

Salah:

```text
Update DB
↓
query old value
```

karena old value sudah hilang.

Benar:

```text
Read current
↓
construct command
↓
execute
```

---

# Step 25.10 — Command Factory

Supaya UI tidak mengambil old state manual di banyak tempat:

```dart
class AssetCommandFactory {
  const AssetCommandFactory(
    this.repository,
  );

  final AssetRepository repository;

  Future<RenameAssetCommand> rename({
    required String projectId,
    required String assetId,
    required String newName,
  }) async {
    final asset =
        await repository.getById(assetId);

    return RenameAssetCommand(
      id: const Uuid().v4(),
      projectId: projectId,
      label: 'Rename ${asset.name}',
      repository: repository,
      assetId: assetId,
      oldName: asset.name,
      newName: newName,
    );
  }
}
```

---

# Step 25.11 — Database transaction

Satu command bisa mengubah beberapa table.

Contoh:

```text
Delete Character
```

mungkin memengaruhi:

```text
Assets
Characters
Relations
References
Scene Instances
```

Semua harus atomic.

Flow:

```text
BEGIN TRANSACTION
↓
execute command
↓
write history
↓
COMMIT
```

Jika gagal:

```text
ROLLBACK
```

---

# Step 25.12 — Transaction Coordinator

Buat abstraction:

```dart
abstract interface class TransactionCoordinator {
  Future<T> run<T>(
    Future<T> Function() action,
  );
}
```

Drift implementation nanti menggunakan:

```dart
database.transaction(...)
```

---

# Step 25.13 — Command Bus final flow

Konsep:

```dart
Future<void> execute(
  AppCommand command,
) async {
  await transactionCoordinator.run(
    () async {
      await command.execute();

      await historyStore.record(
        command,
      );
    },
  );

  history.pushExecuted(command);

  eventBus.emit(
    CommandExecutedEvent(command.id),
  );
}
```

---

# Step 25.14 — Undo juga transaction

```text
BEGIN
↓
command.undo()
↓
history update
↓
COMMIT
```

Bukan hanya execute.

Hal yang sama berlaku untuk redo.

---

# Step 25.15 — Persistent History

In-memory undo stack hilang setelah app ditutup.

Kita butuh persistent history untuk audit/versioning.

Buat:

```dart
class CommandHistoryEntries extends Table {
  TextColumn get id => text()();

  TextColumn get projectId => text()();

  TextColumn get commandType => text()();

  TextColumn get label => text()();

  TextColumn get payload => text()();

  TextColumn get undoPayload =>
      text().nullable()();

  DateTimeColumn get createdAt =>
      dateTime()();

  TextColumn get status =>
      text().withDefault(
        const Constant('applied'),
      )();

  @override
  Set<Column<Object>> get primaryKey => {id};
}
```

---

# Step 25.16 — Command payload

Contoh:

```json
{
  "assetId": "abc",
  "newName": "Void Scriptor"
}
```

Undo:

```json
{
  "assetId": "abc",
  "name": "Qowner"
}
```

Payload harus berisi data yang dibutuhkan untuk reproduksi command.

Jangan simpan object Dart serialized sembarangan.

---

# Step 25.17 — Command registry

Saat history dibaca kembali:

```text
commandType
↓
handler
```

Buat:

```dart
class CommandRegistry {
  final Map<
      String,
      AppCommand Function(
        CommandHistoryRecord record,
      )> _factories = {};

  void register(
    String type,
    AppCommand Function(
      CommandHistoryRecord record,
    ) factory,
  ) {
    _factories[type] = factory;
  }
}
```

---

# Step 25.18 — Command type constants

Jangan gunakan nama class runtime.

Gunakan stable IDs:

```dart
abstract final class CommandTypes {
  static const renameAsset =
      'asset.rename';

  static const updateCharacter =
      'character.update';

  static const moveTimelineClip =
      'timeline.clip.move';

  static const deleteAsset =
      'asset.delete';

  static const createRelation =
      'relation.create';
}
```

Kalau nama class berubah, history tetap valid.

---

# Step 25.19 — Undo session vs historical revisions

Kita perlu membedakan dua hal:

```text
UNDO HISTORY
```

untuk edit cepat.

dan:

```text
PROJECT REVISION HISTORY
```

untuk version control.

Jangan menyamakan keduanya.

---

# Step 25.20 — Undo stack scope

Undo/Redo sebaiknya per project session.

Contoh:

```text
Open Project
↓
perform edits
↓
undo/redo
```

Setelah close/reopen, kita **tidak wajib** menghidupkan kembali seluruh undo stack.

Persistent command history tetap ada untuk audit/revision.

Ini jauh lebih aman.

---

# Step 25.21 — Maximum Undo

Jangan simpan command tak terbatas di RAM.

Contoh:

```dart
class UndoSettings {
  const UndoSettings({
    this.maxCommands = 200,
  });

  final int maxCommands;
}
```

Saat > 200:

```text
remove oldest
```

Persistent history tetap tersimpan.

```text
Command Merge
```

```dart
abstract interface class MergeableCommand {

    AppCommand next,
  );
}
```

Contoh:

```text
same asset
same field
within short editing session
```

menjadi satu command.

# Step 25.26 — Timeline drag

---

# Step 25.27 — Timeline Move Command

```dart
class MoveTimelineClipCommand
    extends BaseCommand {
  MoveTimelineClipCommand({
    required super.id,
    required super.projectId,
    required super.label,
    required this.repository,
    required this.clipId,
    required this.fromSeconds,
    required this.toSeconds,
  });

  final TimelineRepository repository;

  final String clipId;

  final double fromSeconds;
  final double toSeconds;

  @override
  Future<void> execute() =>
      repository.moveClip(
        clipId,
        toSeconds,
      );

  @override
  Future<void> undo() =>
      repository.moveClip(
        clipId,
        fromSeconds,
      );

  @override
  Future<void> redo() =>
      repository.moveClip(
        clipId,
        toSeconds,
      );
}
```

---

# Step 25.28 — Composite Command

Beberapa user actions sebenarnya terdiri dari banyak command.

Contoh:

```text
Add Character to Scene
```

bisa:

```text
Create Scene Instance
Create Timeline Clip
Create Relation
Apply Mode
```

User tetap mengharapkan:

```text
Ctrl+Z
```

sekali.

Buat:

```dart
class CompositeCommand
    extends BaseCommand {
  CompositeCommand({
    required super.id,
    required super.projectId,
    required super.label,
    required this.commands,
  });

  final List<AppCommand> commands;

  @override
  Future<void> execute() async {
    for (final command in commands) {
      await command.execute();
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<void> undo() async {
    for (final command
        in commands.reversed) {
      await command.undo();
    }
  }

  @override
  Future<void> redo() async {
    for (final command in commands) {
      await command.redo();
    }
  }
}
```

Perhatikan undo reverse order.

---

# Step 25.29 — Example Composite

```text
ADD BYTE TO SCENE

1 Create SceneAssetInstance
2 Set Combat Mode
3 Add Timeline Clip
4 Attach Reference Context
```

History:

```text
Add Byte to Scene
```

bukan empat baris.

---

# Step 25.30 — Destructive Command

Delete harus mempunyai informasi recovery.

Contoh:

```text
Delete Character
```

jangan hanya:

```text
DELETE FROM characters
```

Gunakan Project Trash Sprint 24.

Flow:

```text
Delete Asset Command
↓
capture dependencies
↓
move files to Trash
↓
soft-delete / remove records
↓
history
```

Undo:

```text
restore records
restore files
restore relations
```

---

# Step 25.31 — Soft Delete

Tambahkan universal metadata jika belum:

```text
deletedAt
```

Untuk asset penting.

Atau gunakan dedicated deleted records.

Kita tidak perlu permanent-delete data saat user hanya menekan Delete.

---

# Step 25.32 — Permanent Delete

`Empty Trash` berbeda.

Permanent deletion:

```text
NOT UNDOABLE
```

UI harus jelas:

```text
This action cannot be undone.
```

Sebelum empty trash, Sprint 24 backup bisa digunakan.

# Step 25.46 — Revision snapshot

Jangan copy semua assets.

```text

manifest metadata
```

---

# Step 25.47 — Asset immutability principle

Ini penting agar revisions aman.

Imported source file sebaiknya:

```text
immutable
```

Jika image diedit:

```text
old asset file
+
new asset file
```

bukan overwrite source.

Ini membuat historical revision jauh lebih dapat dipercaya.

```text

...
```

atau lebih baik masing-masing punya file UUID sendiri dan version relation.

Jangan overwrite `master.png`.

# Step 25.58 — Branching problem

Misalnya:

```text

↓
B
↓
C
```

restore B lalu edit lagi:

```text
B
↓
D
```

Kita belum perlu Git-style branch penuh.

Untuk sekarang history bisa linear dengan:

```text
parentRevisionId
```

# Step 25.67 — Dirty state

Sprint 24 dirty state sekarang berasal dari Command Bus.

```text
command executed
→ dirty
```

Setelah save:

```text
savedSequence = currentSequence
```

Dirty jika:

```text
currentSequence != savedSequence
```

lebih kuat daripada bool manual.

---

# Step 25.68 — Command sequence

Setiap mutation user-visible mendapat:

```text
sequence
```

misalnya:

```text
1041
1042
1043
```

Berguna untuk:

```text
save point
revision point
history
```

---

# Step 25.69 — Project mutation state

```dart
class ProjectMutationState {
  const ProjectMutationState({
    required this.currentSequence,
    required this.savedSequence,
  });

  final int currentSequence;
  final int savedSequence;

  bool get isDirty =>
      currentSequence != savedSequence;
}
```

---

# Step 25.70 — Save behavior

`Ctrl+S`:

```text
Flush
↓
DB checkpoint
↓
manifest
↓
savedSequence = currentSequence
```

Undo setelah save:

```text
currentSequence changes
```

project menjadi dirty lagi.

Benar.

---

# Step 25.71 — History panel + Undo stack

History panel bisa menunjukkan:

```text
● Applied
↶ Undone
```

Tetapi persistent history jangan dihapus ketika undo.

Status cukup berubah.

---

# Step 25.72 — Redo invalidation

Sequence:

```text
A
B
C
```

Undo C:

```text
A
B
```

Redo tersedia C.

Kemudian user melakukan D:

```text
A
B
D
```

C tidak lagi ada di redo stack.

Tetapi persistent history C tetap bisa tercatat sebagai undone/abandoned.

---

# Step 25.73 — Conflict protection

Sebelum undo:

```text
Can this command still be safely undone?
```

Contoh:

Asset sudah dipakai oleh command lain.

Karena stack undo LIFO, umumnya aman.

Tetapi external/system changes bisa menyebabkan conflict.

Tambahkan:

```dart
Future<bool> canUndo();
```

opsional untuk command kompleks.

---

# Step 25.74 — Non-undoable command

Beberapa operasi:

```text
Empty Trash
External export
Cache clear
Login
API call
```

bukan bagian Undo.

Buat:

```dart
enum CommandUndoPolicy {
  undoable,
  nonUndoable,
}
```

---

# Step 25.75 — External side effects

AI generation request:

```text
Send request
```

tidak bisa benar-benar di-undo setelah provider menerima request.

Tetapi:

```text
Import generated result
```

bisa undo.

Pisahkan kedua operasi tersebut.

---

# Step 25.76 — Command Progress

Command besar:

butuh:

```text
progress
cancel
```

Buat:

```dart
abstract interface class ProgressCommand {
  Stream<double> get progress;

  Future<void> cancel();
}
```

Tidak semua command harus implement.

Jangan menyisakan 2.300 dari 4.000 records kecuali command memang resumable.

---

# Step 25.86 — Backup vs Revision

Sekarang struktur jelas:

| System | Fungsi |
|---|---|
| Undo | Membatalkan edit terakhir |
| Redo | Mengulang edit yang dibatalkan |
| History | Audit perubahan |
| Revision | Checkpoint kreatif |
| Autosave | Menyimpan current state |
| Recovery | Memulihkan crash |
| Backup | Perlindungan data |
| Trash | Memulihkan deletion |

Jangan mencampur kedelapan konsep ini.

---

```text
asset_versions
```

Tambahkan bila diperlukan:

```text
currentSequence
savedSequence
```

ke project metadata.

```text
revision_test.dart
asset_version_test.dart
```

---

# Step 25.90 — Rename Test

```text
Qowner
```

Execute:

```text
Rename → Void Scriptor
```

Expected:

```text
Void Scriptor
```

Undo:

```text
Qowner
```

Redo:

```text
Void Scriptor
```

# Step 25.91 — Timeline Test

```text
Clip = 3 sec
```

Drag:

```text

```

Undo:

```text
3 sec
```

```text
8 sec
```

satu entry.

# Step 25.92 — Composite Test

Action:

```text
Add Byte to Scene
```

menghasilkan:

```text

Timeline Clip

Relation
```

Undo sekali.

Expected semuanya hilang.

# Step 25.93 — Transaction Failure Test

Composite:

```text
Command A ✓
Command B ✓
Command C ✕
```

Expected:

```text
ROLLBACK
```

Bukan:

```text
A saved
B saved
C failed
```

Database harus kembali ke keadaan sebelum command.

# Step 25.94 — Revision Test

State A:

```text

```

```text
Before Hair Redesign
```

Edit:

```text
Qowner hair = white
```

Restore revision.

Expected:

```text
Qowner hair = black
```

Kemudian recovery revision dari state putih tetap tersedia.

```text
v2
```

```text
v1 active
```

Saat release:

```text
ONE database command
```

Ini sangat penting untuk desktop dan Android.

```text

✓ Redo bekerja

✓ timeline drag menghasilkan satu command
✓ composite command atomic
✓ command failure rollback
✓ destructive edit bisa dipulihkan
✓ persistent history tersedia
✓ revisions bisa dibuat
✓ revisions bisa dibandingkan
✓ revision restore aman
✓ asset versions tidak overwrite source
✓ AI-applied changes masuk history
✓ validators menerima mutation events
✓ dirty state berdasarkan sequence
```

## Berikutnya: Sprint 26

Sekarang kita sudah punya **data, project package, recovery, dan mutation architecture**. Sprint 26 sebaiknya fokus pada bagian yang sangat penting untuk aplikasi multi-platform ini:

**Performance + Background Jobs + Thumbnail Pipeline + Virtualized UI + Large Project Optimization.**

Targetnya adalah membuat Void Scriptor tetap lancar ketika project bukan lagi berisi puluhan asset, tetapi misalnya:

```text
50 Characters
300 Wardrobes
500 Weapons/Props
2,000 References
1,000 AI Generations
500 Scenes
10,000 Timeline Clips
20,000+ searchable records
```

Kita akan membuat **background job manager, priority queue, thumbnail worker, lazy loading, pagination, virtualized asset grid/list, timeline viewport rendering, image memory cache, DB indexes, debounced search, cancellation, task center, dan performance diagnostics** sebelum menambahkan fitur besar berikutnya. memcite

Lanjutkan 





Lanjut ke **Sprint 26 — Performance + Background Jobs + Large Project Optimization**.

Sprint ini tidak menambah banyak fitur visual baru, tetapi sangat penting. Kalau kita melewatkannya, aplikasi bisa terasa cepat saat project kecil tetapi mulai berat ketika berisi ribuan asset, reference image, AI generation, scene, audio, dan timeline clip.

Targetnya adalah membuat Void Scriptor tetap responsif pada project besar tanpa mengubah arsitektur inti.

```text
UI THREAD
│
├── Navigation
├── Selection
├── Editing
└── Rendering
        │
        ▼
BACKGROUND JOB SYSTEM
│
├── Thumbnail generation
├── Waveform generation
├── Integrity scan
├── Search indexing
├── Hash calculation
├── Cache rebuild
├── Export
├── Import
└── AI downloads
```

## Step 26.1 — Jangan jalankan pekerjaan berat di UI path

Contoh yang sebaiknya tidak dilakukan langsung saat widget dibangun:

```dart
final bytes = await file.readAsBytes();

final hash = sha256.convert(bytes);

final thumbnail = await createThumbnail(bytes);
```

Pekerjaan seperti hashing, thumbnail generation, scanning ribuan file, dan export harus masuk job system.

UI hanya menerima:

```text
queued
running
completed
failed
```

---

## Step 26.2 — Background Job model

Buat:

`core/jobs/background_job.dart`

```dart
enum BackgroundJobStatus {
  queued,
  running,
  completed,
  failed,
  cancelled,
}

enum BackgroundJobPriority {
  low,
  normal,
  high,
  critical,
}

enum BackgroundJobType {
  thumbnail,
  waveform,
  searchIndex,
  fileHash,
  integrityScan,
  cacheRebuild,
  import,
  export,
  backup,
  generationDownload,
  custom,
}

class BackgroundJob {
  const BackgroundJob({
    required this.id,
    required this.projectId,
    required this.type,
    required this.label,
    required this.status,
    required this.priority,
    required this.createdAt,
    this.progress = 0,
    this.error,
  });

  final String id;
  final String projectId;

  final BackgroundJobType type;
  final String label;

  final BackgroundJobStatus status;
  final BackgroundJobPriority priority;

  final double progress;

  final DateTime createdAt;

  final String? error;
}
```

---

## Step 26.3 — Job interface

```dart
abstract interface class BackgroundTask {
  String get id;

  BackgroundJobType get type;

  Future<void> execute(
    JobContext context,
  );

  Future<void> cancel();
}
```

`JobContext`:

```dart
class JobContext {
  const JobContext({
    required this.reportProgress,
    required this.isCancelled,
  });

  final void Function(double progress)
      reportProgress;

  final bool Function()
      isCancelled;
}
```

---

## Step 26.4 — Job Queue

Buat:

```dart
class BackgroundJobQueue {
  BackgroundJobQueue({
    this.maxConcurrentJobs = 2,
  });

  final int maxConcurrentJobs;

  final List<BackgroundTask> _pending = [];
  final Set<String> _running = {};

  void enqueue(
    BackgroundTask task,
  ) {
    _pending.add(task);
  }
}
```

Tetapi jangan pakai FIFO murni.

Kita punya priority.

---

## Step 26.5 — Priority Scheduler

Urutan:

```text
CRITICAL
↓
HIGH
↓
NORMAL
↓
LOW
```

Contoh:

```text
User membuka image
Thumbnail missing
→ HIGH

Background cache rebuild
→ LOW

Portable export yang diminta user
→ HIGH

Search index maintenance
→ NORMAL
```

Buat:

```dart
int priorityWeight(
  BackgroundJobPriority priority,
) {
  return switch (priority) {
    BackgroundJobPriority.critical => 4,
    BackgroundJobPriority.high => 3,
    BackgroundJobPriority.normal => 2,
    BackgroundJobPriority.low => 1,
  };
}
```

---

## Step 26.6 — Concurrency per category

Jangan membiarkan:

```text
4 video hash jobs
+
4 thumbnail jobs
+
3 export jobs
```

jalan bersamaan di laptop.

Buat resource classes:

```dart
enum JobResource {
  cpu,
  disk,
  network,
  gpu,
}
```

Task dapat meminta:

```text
thumbnail
CPU + Disk

AI download
Network + Disk

Export
CPU + Disk

Search Query
Database
```

Scheduler nanti membatasi resource-heavy concurrency.

---

## Step 26.7 — Worker isolate

Flutter/Dart UI isolate jangan menangani CPU-heavy task.

Task seperti:

```text
SHA-256
image thumbnail processing
large JSON parsing
waveform analysis
```

gunakan isolate.

Untuk operasi sederhana bisa:

```dart
final result = await Isolate.run(
  () {
    // CPU-heavy work
  },
);
```

Jangan memindahkan database connection sembarangan antar isolate tanpa desain yang sesuai.

---

## Step 26.8 — File IO tidak semuanya butuh isolate

Bedakan:

```text
CPU-heavy
→ isolate

Async file IO
→ async API

Database query
→ repository/database layer
```

Jangan membuat isolate untuk setiap `File.exists()`.

Overhead-nya sendiri bisa mahal.

---

## Step 26.9 — Job persistence

Job penting yang bisa bertahan lama perlu persistence.

Buat:

```dart
class BackgroundJobs extends Table {
  TextColumn get id => text()();

  TextColumn get projectId => text()();

  TextColumn get type => text()();

  TextColumn get label => text()();

  TextColumn get status => text()();

  TextColumn get priority => text()();

  RealColumn get progress =>
      real().withDefault(
        const Constant(0),
      )();

  TextColumn get payload =>
      text().nullable()();

  TextColumn get error =>
      text().nullable()();

  DateTimeColumn get createdAt =>
      dateTime()();

  DateTimeColumn get startedAt =>
      dateTime().nullable()();

  DateTimeColumn get completedAt =>
      dateTime().nullable()();

  @override
  Set<Column<Object>> get primaryKey => {id};
}
```

Tidak semua job perlu disimpan permanen.

---

## Step 26.10 — Transient vs persistent job

Buat:

```dart
enum JobPersistence {
  transient,
  persistent,
}
```

Contoh:

```text
Generate one thumbnail
transient

Project export
persistent

AI download
persistent

Full validation
persistent
```

---

# Step 26.11 — Task Center

Sekarang kita bisa membuat satu UI global.

Top bar:

```text
Tasks 3
```

Klik:

```text
TASK CENTER

RUNNING

Export Void Universe
██████████████░░ 81%

Generate thumbnails
████████░░░░░░░ 52%

QUEUED

Rebuild Search Index

COMPLETED

Backup Project
```

Task Center menyatukan background work dari berbagai subsystem.

---

## Step 26.12 — Job cancellation

Task yang cancellable:

```text
thumbnail
search indexing
export
integrity scan
```

Jika user cancel:

```text
RUNNING
↓
cancel requested
↓
cleanup
↓
CANCELLED
```

Task harus sering memeriksa:

```dart
if (context.isCancelled()) {
  return;
}
```

---

## Step 26.13 — Cancellation bukan kill paksa

Jangan mengandalkan hard-kill arbitrary code.

Gunakan cooperative cancellation.

Untuk task yang sedang melakukan atomic database transaction:

```text
finish or rollback
```

baru berhenti.

---

# Step 26.14 — Thumbnail Pipeline

Sekarang banyak asset membutuhkan thumbnail.

Daripada menyimpan thumbnail manual untuk setiap layar, buat satu service.

Flow:

```text
Source Image
↓
Thumbnail Request
↓
Cache Lookup
↓
Exists?
├── yes → return
└── no
     ↓
Queue thumbnail job
     ↓
Generate
     ↓
Cache
     ↓
Notify UI
```

---

## Step 26.15 — Thumbnail request

```dart
class ThumbnailRequest {
  const ThumbnailRequest({
    required this.sourcePath,
    required this.cacheKey,
    required this.width,
    required this.height,
  });

  final String sourcePath;
  final String cacheKey;

  final int width;
  final int height;
}
```

---

## Step 26.16 — Multiple sizes

Jangan membuat satu thumbnail 2000×2000 untuk semua UI.

Preset:

```dart
enum ThumbnailSize {
  tiny,
  small,
  medium,
  large,
}
```

Contoh:

```text
Tiny
64 px

Small
128 px

Medium
256 px

Large
512 px
```

Asset Browser memakai `small/medium`.

Reference Compare memakai source image atau large preview.

---

## Step 26.17 — Thumbnail cache key

Gunakan:

```text
source content hash
+
requested size
+
thumbnail algorithm version
```

Contoh:

```text
abc123_256_v1.webp
```

Kalau source berubah:

```text
hash berubah
```

cache lama tidak digunakan.

---

# Step 26.18 — Jangan invalidate cache berdasarkan filename

Karena:

```text
master.png
```

bisa berubah content.

Hash jauh lebih aman.

---

# Step 26.19 — Lazy Thumbnail

Asset grid menampilkan:

```text
placeholder
↓
thumbnail arrives
↓
fade in
```

Jangan membuat layar freeze menunggu semua 500 thumbnails selesai.

---

# Step 26.20 — Viewport priority

Asset yang terlihat di viewport:

```text
HIGH
```

asset di bawah viewport:

```text
NORMAL
```

asset beberapa halaman jauh:

```text
LOW
```

Ini menghasilkan perceived performance yang jauh lebih baik.

---

# Step 26.21 — Grid virtualization

Jangan render:

```text
10,000 AssetCard
```

sekaligus.

Gunakan lazy builder:

```dart
GridView.builder(
  gridDelegate: ...,
  itemCount: items.length,
  itemBuilder: ...,
)
```

Widget hanya dibuat untuk area yang diperlukan.

---

# Step 26.22 — Pagination

Tetapi `GridView.builder` saja tidak cukup jika query memuat 100.000 rows.

Repository harus paginated.

Buat:

```dart
class PageRequest {
  const PageRequest({
    required this.limit,
    required this.offset,
  });

  final int limit;
  final int offset;
}
```

Response:

```dart
class PageResult<T> {
  const PageResult({
    required this.items,
    required this.hasMore,
  });

  final List<T> items;
  final bool hasMore;
}
```

---

# Step 26.23 — Cursor pagination lebih baik

Offset untuk dataset besar bisa makin mahal.

Kita bisa menuju keyset/cursor:

```text
lastUpdatedAt
lastId
```

Buat abstraction yang tidak memaksa UI tahu mekanisme.

```dart
class AssetPageCursor {
  const AssetPageCursor({
    this.value,
  });

  final String? value;
}
```

---

# Step 26.24 — Asset Browser page size

Awal:

```text
50–100 items
```

Misalnya:

```dart
const assetPageSize = 80;
```

Saat scroll mendekati akhir:

```text
load next page
```

---

# Step 26.25 — Infinite scroll loading

UI:

```text
Assets

[80 items]

↓

Loading more...

↓

next 80
```

Jangan request 10 pages sekaligus.

---

# Step 26.26 — Search debounce

User mengetik:

```text
q
qo
qow
qown
qowner
```

Jangan query database lima kali terlalu cepat.

Gunakan debounce sekitar:

```text
200–300 ms
```

untuk interactive search.

---

# Step 26.27 — Search cancellation

Jika query:

```text
qow
```

belum selesai, lalu user mengetik:

```text
qowner
```

hasil `qow` jangan menimpa hasil terbaru.

Gunakan request generation/token:

```dart
int requestGeneration = 0;
```

Setiap search:

```dart
final generation =
    ++requestGeneration;
```

Saat selesai:

```dart
if (generation != requestGeneration) {
  return;
}
```

---

# Step 26.28 — SQLite indexes

Sekarang database kita besar.

Tambahkan indexes untuk field yang sering dicari:

```text
assets.projectId
assets.type
assets.name
assets.updatedAt
asset_tags.assetId
asset_tags.tagId
relations.sourceAssetId
relations.targetAssetId
scene_characters.sceneId
timeline_clips.trackId
timeline_clips.startSeconds
```

Jangan membuat index setiap field secara buta.

Index juga punya biaya write/storage.

---

# Step 26.29 — Composite indexes

Query:

```text
project + type + updatedAt
```

bisa mendapat composite index.

Contoh konseptual:

```sql
CREATE INDEX idx_assets_project_type_updated
ON assets(project_id, type, updated_at);
```

Test dengan query nyata.

---

# Step 26.30 — Search FTS

Sprint 19 awalnya menggunakan query biasa.

Sekarang sudah waktunya menyiapkan **SQLite FTS** untuk text search besar.

Index:

```text
asset name
description
alias
tags
story title
scene title
bible terminology
```

Jangan taruh raw image metadata di FTS.

---

# Step 26.31 — FTS document abstraction

Buat:

```dart
class SearchDocument {
  const SearchDocument({
    required this.entityId,
    required this.entityType,
    required this.title,
    required this.body,
  });

  final String entityId;
  final String entityType;

  final String title;
  final String body;
}
```

Search Index Service menerima domain event dan update index.

---

# Step 26.32 — Incremental index update

Jangan:

```text
edit one character
↓
rebuild entire search index
```

Gunakan:

```text
CharacterChangedEvent
↓
update document Qowner only
```

Full rebuild hanya untuk repair/cache maintenance.

---

# Step 26.33 — Search index queue

Update indexing bisa:

```text
NORMAL priority
```

Tetapi user search langsung harus tetap menggunakan index versi terakhir yang tersedia.

---

# Step 26.34 — Timeline virtualization

Ini bagian kritis.

Timeline 10 detik kecil mudah.

Tapi project bisa punya:

```text
500 clips
```

atau lebih.

Jangan render semua clip di luar viewport.

Timeline tahu:

```text
visibleStart
visibleEnd
```

Query/render hanya clip yang intersect:

```text
clip.end >= visibleStart
AND
clip.start <= visibleEnd
```

---

# Step 26.35 — Visible timeline interval

```dart
class TimelineViewport {
  const TimelineViewport({
    required this.startSeconds,
    required this.endSeconds,
    required this.pixelsPerSecond,
  });

  final double startSeconds;
  final double endSeconds;

  final double pixelsPerSecond;
}
```

---

# Step 26.36 — Timeline clip query

Repository:

```dart
Future<List<TimelineClipModel>>
    getVisibleClips({
  required String timelineId,
  required double start,
  required double end,
});
```

Jangan load entire timeline setiap pan/zoom.

---

# Step 26.37 — Overscan

Kalau viewport:

```text
10–20 sec
```

render sedikit extra:

```text
8–22 sec
```

supaya scroll/pan halus.

Ini disebut overscan.

---

# Step 26.38 — Timeline track virtualization

Jika ada 100 tracks, viewport vertikal juga perlu virtualization.

Render tracks yang terlihat + beberapa overscan.

---

# Step 26.39 — Timeline repaint boundaries

Clip yang bergerak jangan membuat seluruh app repaint.

Gunakan:

```text
RepaintBoundary
```

secara strategis pada area timeline.

Jangan membungkus setiap tiny widget tanpa profiling.

---

# Step 26.40 — Preview image memory

Salah satu penyebab aplikasi kreatif berat adalah image memory.

Contoh:

```text
6000×4000 RGBA
```

bisa membutuhkan memory sangat besar ketika decoded.

Thumbnail grid jangan decode full resolution.

Gunakan thumbnail cache.

---

# Step 26.41 — Full image hanya saat perlu

Asset Browser:

```text
256px preview
```

Reference Comparison:

```text
larger preview
```

Export/detail view:

```text
original
```

Jangan gunakan original untuk semuanya.

---

# Step 26.42 — Image prefetch

Ketika user membuka asset 10:

```text
prefetch asset 11/12 thumbnails
```

boleh dilakukan low priority.

Jangan prefetch ratusan.

---

# Step 26.43 — Memory cache budget

Buat configurable memory policy.

```dart
class MemoryCachePolicy {
  const MemoryCachePolicy({
    this.maxPreviewItems = 100,
  });

  final int maxPreviewItems;
}
```

Nanti bisa berdasarkan estimated bytes, lebih baik daripada jumlah item.

---

# Step 26.44 — LRU cache

Thumbnail/previews yang baru dipakai bertahan.

Yang lama:

```text
evict
```

Gunakan Least Recently Used concept.

Disk cache tetap tersedia.

---

# Step 26.45 — Audio waveform performance

Waveform sudah di-cache Sprint 23.

Timeline hanya meminta peaks yang relevan.

Jangan menyimpan jutaan sample mentah di widget.

---

# Step 26.46 — Multi-resolution waveform

Untuk file panjang:

```text
full waveform
medium
coarse
```

Saat timeline zoom out:

```text
coarse
```

Saat zoom in:

```text
fine
```

Mirip mipmaps untuk audio waveform.

---

# Step 26.47 — Large audio lazy load

Audio Library:

```text
title
duration
waveform preview
```

jangan decode file penuh sampai user menekan Play/Edit.

---

# Step 26.48 — Reference Library pagination

Reference Library juga harus paginated.

Filter:

```text
Qowner / Face
```

hanya query matching references.

Jangan memuat semua project references lalu filter di Dart.

---

# Step 26.49 — Relation graph loading

Asset bisa punya ribuan relation.

Inspector hanya load:

```text
first N immediate relations
```

misalnya:

```text
20
```

dengan:

```text
View All
```

Relation Graph bisa load neighborhoods on-demand.

---

# Step 26.50 — Story tree lazy loading

Story Explorer:

```text
Story
```

load arcs.

Expand Arc:

```text
load chapters
```

Expand Chapter:

```text
load scenes
```

Jangan load semua beats/timeline saat tree dibuka.

---

# Step 26.51 — Bible lazy loading

Project Bible sidebar hanya load category counts.

Content loaded setelah user memilih section.

---

# Step 26.52 — Database connection lifecycle

Satu project session:

```text
one ProjectDatabase instance
```

Jangan membuka DB connection baru pada setiap widget.

ProjectSession owns:

```text
database
repositories
services
```

Saat project close:

```text
dispose
```

---

# Step 26.53 — Repository provider scope

Riverpod providers yang bergantung project harus memiliki lifecycle project/session.

Jangan meninggalkan provider project lama aktif setelah user membuka project lain.

Gunakan `family`/scope dengan project ID atau session object secara konsisten.

---

# Step 26.54 — Avoid giant global provider

Jangan buat:

```text
ProjectEverythingProvider
```

yang load:

```text
all assets
all scenes
all references
all timeline
all story
all AI
```

Setiap workspace meminta domain yang diperlukan saja.

---

# Step 26.55 — Selective watching

Widget kecil jangan:

```dart
ref.watch(
  entireHugeStateProvider,
);
```

kalau hanya membutuhkan:

```text
favorite
```

Gunakan selectors / provider granularity supaya perubahan kecil tidak rebuild seluruh workspace.

---

# Step 26.56 — Immutable state discipline

State update:

```text
new instance
```

bukan mutate object besar secara diam-diam.

Ini membuat rebuild lebih mudah diprediksi.

---

# Step 26.57 — Large list keys

Asset cards:

```dart
ValueKey(asset.id)
```

Timeline clip:

```dart
ValueKey(clip.id)
```

agar Flutter bisa mempertahankan element identity saat reorder/update.

---

# Step 26.58 — Batch database writes

Import 1000 assets.

Salah:

```text
transaction
asset 1
commit

transaction
asset 2
commit
...
```

Gunakan:

```text
ONE reasonable transaction
batch insert
```

atau chunk transaction jika sangat besar.

---

# Step 26.59 — Import chunking

Untuk 100.000 files, satu giant transaction juga buruk.

Gunakan chunk misalnya:

```text
250–1000 records
```

tergantung data.

Job progress:

```text
12%
38%
71%
```

---

# Step 26.60 — Prepared statements/batch

Gunakan fasilitas batch Drift/SQLite untuk repetitive insert.

Kurangi round trips.

---

# Step 26.61 — Database maintenance

Tambahkan:

```text
Project
→ Database Maintenance
```

Operations:

```text
Analyze
Optimize
Integrity Check
Vacuum
```

`VACUUM` jangan dijalankan otomatis terlalu sering karena bisa berat.

---

# Step 26.62 — Maintenance warning

Untuk large DB:

```text
VACUUM may take several minutes.
```

Run sebagai background job setelah DB berada dalam state aman.

---

# Step 26.63 — Project startup fast path

Jangan saat Open Project menjalankan:

```text
hash 30,000 files
rebuild all caches
validate everything
```

Fast open:

```text
manifest
schema
DB
quick integrity
session
workspace
```

Lalu optional jobs:

```text
background health scan
```

---

# Step 26.64 — Startup phases

UI:

```text
Opening Void Universe...

Reading manifest
Opening database
Restoring workspace
```

Begitu cukup:

```text
Studio opens
```

Background:

```text
Checking references...
```

Task Center menangani sisanya.

---

# Step 26.65 — Startup metrics

Catat internally:

```text
manifest load
DB open
workspace restore
first frame
```

supaya kita tahu bottleneck.

Jangan menebak.

---

# Step 26.66 — Performance diagnostics

Tambahkan development/performance page:

```text
PERFORMANCE

Project Assets
21,482

Database
182 MB

Search Index
Healthy

Thumbnail Cache
1.8 GB

Running Jobs
2

Recent Query
12 ms

Last Project Open
820 ms
```

---

# Step 26.67 — Slow query log

Repository/database wrapper dapat log query yang melampaui threshold.

Contoh:

```text
>100 ms
```

development builds.

Jangan log sensitive content berlebihan.

---

# Step 26.68 — UI frame monitoring

Development mode bisa menunjukkan:

```text
UI frame build
raster
dropped frames
```

Tetapi jangan membuat custom profiler berlebihan jika tooling Flutter sudah cukup.

Kita hanya butuh lightweight indicators di diagnostics.

---

# Step 26.69 — Performance mode

Settings:

```text
Performance

Thumbnail Quality
Low / Balanced / High

Background Workers
1 / 2 / 4

AI Downloads
1 / 2 / 4

Preload
Minimal / Balanced / Aggressive
```

Default `Balanced`.

Jangan meminta user mengerti thread count untuk menjalankan aplikasi normal.

---

# Step 26.70 — Battery awareness mobile

Android/mobile:

```text
background expensive jobs
```

lebih konservatif.

Misalnya:

```text
workers desktop = 2–4
workers mobile = 1–2
```

Battery/thermal lebih penting.

---

# Step 26.71 — Pause background jobs

Task Center:

```text
Pause Background Work
```

Tidak mem-pause critical user task/export kecuali diminta.

Low priority jobs berhenti scheduling.

---

# Step 26.72 — Network job policy

AI download/cloud work dapat punya:

```text
Wi-Fi only
```

option di mobile bila nantinya diperlukan.

Jangan hardcode sekarang.

---

# Step 26.73 — Duplicate task suppression

Jika thumbnail `ABC-256` sudah:

```text
queued
```

request kedua jangan membuat job baru.

Gunakan task key:

```text
thumbnail:ABC:256
```

deduplicate.

---

# Step 26.74 — Work coalescing

Search index receives:

```text
Qowner changed
Qowner changed
Qowner changed
```

dalam 200ms.

Bisa coalesce menjadi:

```text
Update Qowner search document
```

sekali.

---

# Step 26.75 — Thumbnail failure

Jika source corrupt:

```text
Thumbnail Failed
```

jangan retry tanpa batas.

Cache error state sementara.

UI:

```text
broken image placeholder
```

Validation Center bisa mencatat source file issue.

---

# Step 26.76 — Job retry

Background jobs:

```dart
class JobRetryPolicy {
  const JobRetryPolicy({
    this.maxAttempts = 2,
  });

  final int maxAttempts;
}
```

Thumbnail failure karena corrupted file tidak akan sembuh dengan 10 retries.

---

# Step 26.77 — Task dependency

Beberapa jobs:

```text
Import
↓
Hash
↓
Thumbnail
↓
Search Index
```

Jangan buat semuanya tightly coupled satu giant function.

Job graph/dependencies bisa sederhana:

```dart
class JobDependency {
  const JobDependency({
    required this.jobId,
    required this.dependsOnId,
  });

  final String jobId;
  final String dependsOnId;
}
```

---

# Step 26.78 — User task vs derived task

Import is user-facing:

```text
Import 50 References
```

Derived:

```text
Generate 50 thumbnails
```

Task Center bisa collapse derived children di bawah parent.

---

# Step 26.79 — Task hierarchy

```text
Import References                      62%

  ✓ Copy files
  ✓ Add database records
  ◌ Calculate hashes
  ◌ Generate thumbnails
  ○ Update search index
```

Lebih mudah dipahami user.

---

# Step 26.80 — Background Jobs DB schema

Naikkan schema:

```text
18 → 19
```

Tambahkan:

```text
background_jobs
```

Jika perlu:

```text
job_dependencies
```

Cache entries sudah ada dari Sprint 24.

Search FTS mungkin menggunakan virtual tables/database-specific migration.

---

# Step 26.81 — Database indexes migration

Tambahkan index penting melalui migration.

Sebelum dan sesudah, benchmark beberapa query.

Acceptance bukan sekadar:

```text
index exists
```

tetapi:

```text
query improves / remains reasonable
```

---

# Step 26.82 — Performance benchmark dataset

Buat test project generator untuk development.

Contoh:

```text
50 Characters
200 Wardrobes
300 Weapons
500 Props
100 Environments
2,000 References
1,000 Generations
500 Scenes
10,000 Clips
```

Jangan isi manual.

Buat seed generator khusus test/dev.

---

# Step 26.83 — Jangan ship dev seed generator di production UI

Pisahkan:

```text
dev_tools/
```

atau compile/debug guarded.

---

# Step 26.84 — Asset Browser benchmark

Target praktis awal:

```text
open asset browser
< ~1s reasonable desktop test hardware

scroll
no major stutter

search
interactive response
```

Jangan mengunci angka absolut tanpa hardware baseline.

Yang penting baseline + regression test.

---

# Step 26.85 — Timeline benchmark

Dataset:

```text
10,000 clips
```

Tetapi viewport hanya mungkin 100–300 visible.

Pastikan render complexity bergantung pada visible items, bukan total clips.

---

# Step 26.86 — Search benchmark

FTS:

```text
20,000+ docs
```

Search:

```text
qowner
```

harus terasa instant/interaktif.

Pagination tetap berlaku pada result besar.

---

# Step 26.87 — Memory benchmark

Scroll 2,000 image references.

Memory seharusnya tidak terus meningkat tanpa batas.

Kalau:

```text
500 MB
→ 1 GB
→ 2 GB
```

setelah scroll terus, berarti preview/cache lifecycle bermasalah.

---

# Step 26.88 — Dispose controllers

Pastikan:

```text
TextEditingController
ScrollController
AnimationController
FocusNode
```

di-dispose.

Pada workspace yang dibuka berulang kali ini bisa menjadi leak kecil tapi kumulatif.

---

# Step 26.89 — Subscription lifecycle

Streams/listeners:

```text
database
job progress
event bus
```

harus dilepas ketika provider/widget/session mati.

Riverpod dapat membantu, tetapi jangan menyimpan listener manual global tanpa cleanup.

---

# Step 26.90 — Project close stress test

Open project A.

Browse 100 assets.

Close.

Open project B.

Expected:

```text
project A database closed
providers disposed
old thumbnail requests cancelled where appropriate
old listeners removed
```

Tidak ada data A muncul di B.

---

# Step 26.91 — Task session ownership

Setiap task punya:

```text
projectId
```

Saat project ditutup:

Transient task:

```text
cancel
```

Persistent export/job:

```text
prompt user
```

atau task manager ownership sesuai jenis.

---

# Step 26.92 — Thumbnail jobs after close

Jika thumbnail derived cache untuk project sudah tidak open:

```text
LOW jobs can cancel
```

Tidak ada alasan terus menghabiskan CPU kecuali user memang meminta cache rebuild.

---

# Step 26.93 — Import/export tasks

User close project saat export:

```text
Export is still running.

[Keep Project Open]
[Cancel Export & Close]
```

Jangan menutup database/resources di bawah task yang masih membutuhkannya.

---

# Step 26.94 — UI skeletons

Daripada spinner besar untuk seluruh screen:

```text
Asset Browser
```

tampilkan:

```text
toolbar ready
filters ready
grid skeleton
```

Lalu data muncul.

Perceived performance lebih baik.

---

# Step 26.95 — Error isolation

Jika thumbnail service gagal:

```text
Asset Browser tetap jalan
```

Jika waveform generation gagal:

```text
Audio metadata tetap tampil
```

Derived service failure jangan menjatuhkan project.

# Step 26.96 — Task Center notification

Jika user pindah workspace dan export selesai:

```text
Portable project export completed.
```

Notification kecil.

Jangan modal kecuali perlu keputusan user.

```text
BACKGROUND TASKS
```

Tetapi jangan membuat Dashboard penuh telemetry.

Task Center adalah detail utamanya.

# Step 26.99 — Definition of Done

Sprint 26 selesai jika:

```text
✓ heavy jobs tidak memblok UI
✓ background queue punya priority
✓ jobs bisa cancel
✓ duplicate jobs dideduplicate
✓ thumbnail generation lazy
✓ thumbnail berdasarkan viewport priority
✓ asset browser paginated
✓ search debounced
✓ search lama tidak menimpa query baru
✓ DB indexes tersedia untuk hot queries
✓ FTS search tersedia
✓ timeline query berdasarkan viewport
✓ image grid tidak decode source penuh
✓ waveform memakai cache
✓ project startup menggunakan fast path
✓ session dispose bersih
✓ task center bekerja
✓ large project benchmark tetap responsif
```

```text
18 AI Providers + Generation Queue     ✅
19 Asset Browser + Global Search       ✅
20 Reference + Consistency System      ✅
21 Project Bible + Canon System        ✅
22 Creature + Vehicle + Prop System    ✅
23 VFX + Audio + Production System     ✅
24 Project Package + Backup/Recovery   ✅
25 Undo/Redo + History + Versioning    ✅
26 Performance + Background Jobs       ◀ CURRENT
```

**Sprint 27** sebaiknya menjadi **Workspace Layout Manager + Docking Panels + Multi-Tab + Multi-Window Desktop System**. Kita akan membuat layout seperti aplikasi profesional: panel Explorer/Inspector bisa resize dan collapse, workspace tabs, split editor, saved layouts, fullscreen preview, detachable window untuk Reference Compare/Timeline, keyboard focus navigation, dan restoration layout per project. Setelah itu struktur desktop yang sejak awal ingin kita tiru akan terasa jauh lebih matang dan fleksibel.
